'#6bd8cb'\n  on-tertiary-fixed: '#00201d'\n  on-tertiary-fixed-variant: '#005049'\n  background: '#f7f9fb'\n  on-background: '#191c1e'\n  surface-variant: '#e0e3e5'\ntypography:\n  headline-xl:\n    fontFamily: Inter\n    fontSize: 36px\n    fontWeight: '700'\n    lineHeight: 44px\n    letterSpacing: -0.02em\n  headline-lg:\n    fontFamily: Inter\n    fontSize: 30px\n    fontWeight: '600'\n    lineHeight: 38px\n    letterSpacing: -0.01em\n  headline-md:\n    fontFamily: Inter\n    fontSize: 24px\n    fontWeight: '600'\n    lineHeight: 32px\n  body-lg:\n    fontFamily: Inter\n    fontSize: 18px\n    fontWeight: '400'\n    lineHeight: 28px\n  body-md:\n    fontFamily: Inter\n    fontSize: 16px\n    fontWeight: '400'\n    lineHeight: 24px\n  body-sm:\n    fontFamily: Inter\n    fontSize: 14px\n    fontWeight: '400'\n    lineHeight: 20px\n  label-md:\n    fontFamily: Inter\n    fontSize: 12px\n    fontWeight: '600'\n    lineHeight: 16px\n    letterSpacing: 0.05em\n  code-sm:\n    fontFamily: JetBrains Mono\n    fontSize: 13px\n    fontWeight: '400'\n    lineHeight: 18px\nrounded:\n  sm: 0.125rem\n  DEFAULT: 0.25rem\n  md: 0.375rem\n  lg: 0.5rem\n  xl: 0.75rem\n  full: 9999px\nspacing:\n  base: 4px\n  xs: 4px\n  sm: 8px\n  md: 16px\n  lg: 24px\n  xl: 32px\n  gutter: 24px\n  margin: 32px\n---\n\n## Brand & Style\nThe design system is engineered for technical precision and high-stakes decision-making. It embodies a **Corporate/Modern** aesthetic that prioritizes clarity, reliability, and data density without sacrificing airiness. The brand personality is that of a \"Silent Expert\"—authoritative but unobtrusive, providing a sophisticated framework for complex data monitoring.\n\nThe visual language focuses on a \"Data-Driven & Professional\" ethos. It uses a disciplined structure, utilizing heavy whitespace to prevent cognitive overload, paired with a sophisticated color palette that directs attention to critical system health indicators. The style avoids unnecessary decoration, instead finding beauty in the alignment, crispness of borders, and the rhythmic regularity of its grid.\n\n## Colors\nThe palette is anchored by a deep navy and slate foundation to establish a sense of permanence and trust. Vibrant teal serves as the primary action and monitoring accent, distinguishing \"system\" actions from \"data\" insights.\n\n- **Primary (Deep Navy):** Used for core navigation, high-level headers, and primary text to ground the interface.\n- **Secondary (Slate):** Applied to supporting UI elements, icons, and secondary text to create a hierarchical recession.\n- **Tertiary (Vibrant Teal):** Reserved for interactive elements and \"healthy\" data state indicators.\n- **Semantic Palette:** Functional colors (Success, Warning, Alert) are high-chroma to ensure they remain legible even when used as small indicators or sparklines against neutral backgrounds.\n\n## Typography\nThis design system utilizes **Inter** for all UI and prose elements. Its tall x-height and systematic design make it ideal for data-heavy applications where legibility at small sizes (e.g., table cells and metadata) is paramount. \n\n- **Headlines:** Use tighter letter spacing and bold weights to provide strong visual anchors.\n- **Data Display:** For raw data values, SQL snippets, or schema paths, **JetBrains Mono** is introduced to provide a clear distinction between technical strings and UI labels.\n- **Mobile Scaling:** Headlines scale down by roughly 20% on mobile devices, while body text remains consistent at 16px to ensure accessibility.\n\n## Layout & Spacing\nThe layout follows a **12-column fluid grid** system. To maintain \"Technical Sophistication,\" the design system employs an 8pt spacing rhythm (with 4px increments for micro-adjustments). \n\n- **Margins & Gutters:** Desktop layouts use 32px outer margins and 24px gutters to allow the content to breathe. \n- **Density:** In data-grid views, vertical spacing can be \"compacted\" to 8px (sm), while dashboard overview cards use 24px (lg) padding to emphasize key metrics.\n- **Responsive Behavior:** On tablet, gutters reduce to 16px. On mobile, the grid collapses to a single column with 16px side margins, prioritizing vertical stackability.\n\n## Elevation & Depth\nDepth is conveyed through **Tonal Layers** and **Low-Contrast Outlines** rather than heavy shadows. This reinforces the \"flat and precise\" nature of a data tool.\n\n- **Level 0 (Background):** The base surface uses the neutral slate-tinted white (#F8FAFC).\n- **Level 1 (Cards/Containers):** Raised elements use a pure white background with a 1px border (#E2E8F0).\n- **Interactive Elevation:** Only upon interaction (hover/active) do elements gain an **Ambient Shadow**—a soft, diffused 10% opacity shadow with a slight navy tint to prevent a \"dirty\" gray look.\n- **Focus States:** High-visibility focus rings use a 2px teal stroke with a 2px offset to ensure clarity for keyboard navigation.\n\n## Shapes\nThe design system adopts a **Soft** shape language. This subtle rounding (4px/0.25rem) strikes a balance between the clinical coldness of sharp corners and the overly casual nature of fully rounded UI.\n\n- **Components:** Buttons and input fields use the base 4px radius.\n- **Large Containers:** Cards and modals may use `rounded-lg` (8px) to soften the large surface areas.\n- **Status Pills:** Status indicators (e.g., \"Healthy,\" \"Critical\") use a full pill-shape (999px) to distinguish them instantly from actionable buttons.\n\n## Components\nConsistent component behavior is essential for building user trust in a data quality context.\n\n- **Buttons:** Primary buttons use a solid teal background. Secondary buttons use a slate outline. Ghost buttons are reserved for utility actions in tables.\n- **Data Tables:** These are the heart of the system. They use 1px slate-200 horizontal dividers, no vertical borders, and a subtle slate-50 hover state.\n- **Status Badges:** Use a \"Dot + Label\" pattern. A vibrant colored dot (Green, Amber, Red) accompanies the text to ensure accessibility for color-blind users.\n- **Input Fields:** Use a 1px slate-300 border that transitions to teal on focus. Error states use a red border with a bottom-aligned error message in `body-sm`.\n- **Monitoring Indicators:** Progress bars and health gauges use a \"Thin Track\" design (4px height) to remain unobtrusive within dense dashboard layouts.\n- **Cards:** Used for grouping related metrics. They feature a 1px border and no shadow by default, gaining a soft shadow only when they are clickable.","contentType":"text/plain","isBase64":false,"truncated":false}

'color-mix(in srgb, var(--red-500) 78%, var(--ink))' : 'color-mix(in srgb, var(--indigo-500) 62%, var(--ink))',\n            borderTop: `2px solid ${b.fail ? 'var(--red-500)' : 'var(--indigo-400)'}`,\n          }} />\n        ))}\n      </div>\n      <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', marginTop: 12, paddingTop: 10, borderTop: '1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.08)' }}>\n        {labels.map((l) => <span key={l} style={{ fontSize: 11, fontFamily: 'var(--font-mono)', color: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.4)' }}>{l}</span>)}\n      </div>\n    </DQd.Card>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction SuitePerformance() {\n  const { Card, ProgressBar } = DQd;\n  const rows = [\n    { name: 'Financial Transactions', state: 'Optimal', value: 96, tone: 'pass' },\n    { name: 'User Metadata', state: 'Stable', value: 74, tone: 'warning' },\n    { name: 'Real-time Telemetry', state: 'Critical', value: 34, tone: 'fail' },\n    { name: 'Audit Logs', state: 'Optimal', value: 92, tone: 'pass' },\n  ];\n  const stateColor = { Optimal: 'var(--status-pass)', Stable: 'var(--status-warn)', Critical: 'var(--status-fail)' };\n  return (\n    <Card padding={24}>\n      <div style={{ fontSize: 20, fontWeight: 700, color: 'var(--text-primary)' }}>Suite Performance</div>\n      <div style={{ fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--text-muted)', marginTop: 4 }}>Health by category</div>\n      <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 18, marginTop: 22 }}>\n        {rows.map((r) => (\n          <div key={r.name}>\n            <div style={{ display: key: 'status', header: 'Status', width: 130, render: (v) => <StatusBadge status={v} variant=\"soft\" /> },\n    { key: 'anomalies', header: 'Anomalies', align: 'right', render: (v) => <span style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', padding: '18px 20px 14px' }}>\n        <span style={{ fontSize: 18, fontWeight: 700, color: 'var(--text-primary)' }}>Recent Runs</span>\n        <IconButton ariaLabel=\"Refresh\"><window.Icon.refresh size={16} /></IconButton>\n      </div>\n      <DataTable columns={cols} rows={rows} onRowClick={(r) => onOpen && onOpen(r)} 'Jun 13, 06:00', dur: '3m 08s' },\n  { suite: 'Warehouse Reconciliation', status: 'cancelled', by: 'manual', started: 'Jun 12, 22:14', dur: '—' },\n];\n\nconst PIPELINES = [\n  { provider: 'adf', dag: 'adf-daily-ingest', env: 'PROD', status: 'succeeded', started: 'Jun 13, 09:00', reason: '—' },\n  { provider: 'airflow', dag: 'cust_ingestion_dag', env: 'PROD', status: 'failed', started: 'Jun 13, 08:25', reason: 'Task retry limit exceeded' },\n  { provider: 'adf', dag: 'adf-uc-sync', env: 'UAT', status: 'running', started: 'Jun 13, 10:00', reason: '—' },\n  { provider: 'airflow', dag: 'feature_store_dag', env: 'QA', status: 'succeeded', started: 'Jun 13, 02:00', reason: '—' },\n];\n\nfunction RunsTab({ onOpen }) {\n  const { DataTable, StatusBadge } = DQ;\n  const cols = [\n    { key: 'suite', header: 'Suite', render: (v) => <strong style={{ color: 'var(--text-primary)', fontWeight: 600 }}>{v}</strong> },\n    { key: 'status', header: 'Status', width: 130, render: (v) => <StatusBadge status={v} variant=\"soft\" /> },\n    { key: 'by', header: 'Triggered by', mono: true, render: (v) => <span style={{ color: 'var(--text-accent)' }}>{v}</span> },\n    { key: 'started', header: 'Started', nowrap: true },\n    { key: 'dur', header: 'Duration', align: 'right', nowrap: true, render: (v) => <span style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>{v}</span> },\n  ];\n  return <DQ.Card padding={0} style={{ overflow: 'hidden' }}><DataTable columns={cols} rows={RUNS} onRowClick={(r) => onOpen(r)} /></DQ.Card>;\n}\n\nconst CHECK_RESULTS = [\n  { check: 'revenue not null', exp: 'expect_column_values_to_not_be_null', status: 'pass', metric: '0.0', observed: '0 unexpected' },\n  { check: 'amount positive', exp: 'expect_column_values_to_be_between', status: 'pass', metric: '0.0', observed: 'min 0.01' },\n  { check: 'email format', exp: 'expect_column_values_to_match_regex', status: 'fail', metric: '4.2', observed: '412 / 9842' },\n  { check: 'customer id unique', exp: 'expect_column_values_to_be_unique', status: 'critical', metric: '11.8', observed: '1,160 dupes' },\n  { check: 'row count stable', exp: 'expect_table_row_count_to_be_between', status: 'warn', metric: '1.0', observed: '9,842 rows' },\n];\n\nfunction Meta({ label, children }) {\n  return (\n    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 3 }}>\n      <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>{label}</span>\n      <span style={{ fontSize: 14, color: 'var(--text-primary)', fontWeight: 500 }}>{children}</span>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction DownloadMenu({ run }) {\n  const { Button } = DQ;\n  const [open, setOpen] = React.useState(false);\n  const ref = React.useRef(null);\n\n  React.useEffect(() => {\n    if (!open) return undefined;\n    const onDoc = (e) => { if (ref.current && !ref.current.contains(e.target)) setOpen(false); };\n    document.addEventListener('mousedown', onDoc);\n    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', onDoc);\n  }, [open]);\n\n  const slug = (run.suite || 'run').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '_').replace(/^_|_$/g, '');\n\n  const triggerDownload = (filename, mime, text) => {\n    const blob = new Blob([text], { type: mime });\n    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);\n    const a = document.createElement('a');\n    a.href = url; a.download = filename;\n    document.body.appendChild(a); a.click();\n    document.body.removeChild(a);\n    setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 0);\n    setOpen(false);\n  };\n\n  const downloadCsv = () => {\n    const cols = ['check', 'exp', 'status', 'metric', 'observed'];\n    const head = ['Check', 'Expectation', 'Status', 'Metric', 'Observed'];\n    const esc = (v) => { const s = String(v == null ? '' : v); return /[\",\\n]/.test(s) ? '\"' + s.replace(/\"/g, '\"\"') + '\"' : s; };\n    const lines = [head.join(',')].concat(CHECK_RESULTS.map((r) => cols.map((c) => esc(r[c])).join(',')));\n    triggerDownload(`${slug}_results.csv`, 'text/csv;charset=utf-8', lines.join('\\n'));\n  };\n\n  const downloadJson = () => {\n    const payload = {\n      suite: run.suite, triggeredBy: run.by, status: run.status,\n      started: run.started, duration: run.dur,\n      checks: CHECK_RESULTS.map((r) => ({ name: r.check, expectation: r.exp, status: r.status, metric: r.metric, observed: r.observed })),\n    };\n    triggerDownload(`${slug}_results.json`, 'application/json', JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2));\n  };\n\n  const Item = ({ icon, label, onClick }) => {\n    const [hover, setHover] = React.useState(false);\n    return (\n      <button onMouseDown={onClick}\n        onMouseEnter={() => setHover(true)} onMouseLeave={() => setHover(false)}\n        style={{\n          display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10, width: '100%', textAlign: 'left',\n          padding: '8px 12px', border: 'none', borderRadius: 'var(--radius)', cursor: 'pointer',\n          background: hover ? 'var(--bg-hover)' : 'transparent',\n          fontFamily: 'var(--font-sans)', fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 500, color: 'var(--text-body)',\n        }}>\n        <span style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)', display: 'inline-flex' }}>{icon}</span>{label}\n      </button>\n    );\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div ref={ref} style={{ position: 'relative' }}>\n      <Button variant=\"secondary\" size=\"sm\"\n        iconLeft={<window.Icon.download size={14} />}\n        iconRight={<window.Icon.down size={11} style={{ marginLeft: -2 }} />}\n        onClick={() => setOpen((o) => !o)}>Download</Button>\n      {open && (\n        <div style={{\n          position: 'absolute', right: 0, top: 'calc(100% + 6px)', width: 196, zIndex: 30,\n          background: 'var(--bg-card)', border: '1px solid var(--border)', borderRadius: 'var(--radius-lg)',\n          boxShadow: 'var(--shadow-overlay)', padding: 6,\n        }}>\n          <Item icon={<window.Icon.docs size={15} />} label=\"Download as CSV\" onClick={downloadCsv} />\n          <Item icon={<window.Icon.docs size={15} />} label=\"Download as JSON\" onClick={downloadJson} />\n        </div>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction RunDetailScreen({ go, params }) {\n  const { Card, MetricCard, StatusBadge, DataTable, Button } = DQ;\n  const { PageHeader } = window;\n  const run = (params && params.run) || RUNS[0];\n  const started = run.started || '—';\n  const startParts = started.split(', ');\n  const cols = [\n    { key: 'check', header: 'Check', render: (v) => <strong style={{ color: 'var(--text-primary)', fontWeight: 600 }}>{v}</strong> },\n    { key: 'exp', header: 'Expectation', mono: true, render: (v) => <span style={{ color: 'var(--text-secondary)', fontSize: 12 }}>{v}</span> },\n    { key: 'status', header: 'Status', width: 96, render: (v) => <StatusBadge status={v} variant=\"soft\" /> },\n    { key: 'metric', header: 'Metric', align: 'right', width: 70, mono: true },\n    { key: 'observed', header: 'Observed', mono: true, render: (v) => <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--text-body)' }}>{v}</span> },\n  ];\n  const passed = CHECK_RESULTS.filter((c) => c.status === 'pass').length;\n  return (\n    <div style={{ maxWidth: 1000, margin: '0 auto' }}>\n      <PageHeader backLabel=\"Results\" onBack={() => go('results')}\n        title={run.suite} subtitle={<span style={{ fontFamily: 'var(--font-mono)' }}>{run.by || 'manual'}</span>}\n        actions={<React.Fragment>\n          <DownloadMenu run={run} />\n          <Button variant=\"secondary\" size=\"sm\" iconLeft={<window.Icon.refresh size={14} />}>Re-run</Button>\n        </React.Fragment>} />\n\n      <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(4, 1fr)', gap: 16, marginBottom: 20 }}>\n        <Card padding={18}>\n          <div style={{ fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--text-secondary)', marginBottom: 8 }}>Status</div>\n          <StatusBadge status={run.status} variant=\"soft\" />\n        </Card>\n        <MetricCard label=\"Checks passed\" value={`${passed}/${CHECK_RESULTS.length}`} progress={(passed / CHECK_RESULTS.length) * 100} progressTone={passed === CHECK_RESULTS.length ? 'pass' : 'warning'} />\n        <MetricCard label=\"Started\" value={startParts[1] || startParts[0]} footnote={startParts[1] ? startParts[0] : null} />\n        <MetricCard label=\"Duration\" value={run.dur || '—'} />\n      </div>\n\n      <div style={{ fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--text-primary)', marginBottom: 12 }}>Check results</div>\n      <Card padding={0} style={{ overflow: 'hidden' }}><DataTable columns={cols} rows={CHECK_RESULTS} /></Card>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction PipelinesTab() {\n  const { DataTable, StatusBadge, Tag } = DQ;\n  const cols = [\n    { key: 'provider', header: 'Provider', width: 110, render: (v) => <Tag tone=\"outline\">{v}</Tag> },\n    { key: 'dag', header: 'Pipeline / DAG', mono: true, render: (v) => <span style={{ color: 'var(--text-primary)' }}>{v}</span> },\n    { key: 'env', header: 'Env', width: 80, render: (v) => <Tag>{v}</Tag> },\n    { key: 'status', header: 'Status', width: 130, render: (v) => <StatusBadge status={v} variant=\"soft\" /> },\n    { key: 'started', header: 'Started', nowrap: true },\n    { key: 'reason', header: 'Failure reason', render: (v) => <span style={{ color: v === '—' ? 'var(--text-muted)' : 'var(--status-fail)' }}>{v}</span> },\n  ];\n  return <DQ.Card padding={0} style={{ overflow: 'hidden' }}><DataTable columns={cols} rows={PIPELINES} /></DQ.Card>;\n}\n\nfunction ResultsScreen({ go }) {\n  const { MetricCard } = DQ;\n  const I = window.Icon;\n  const [tab, setTab] = React.useState('runs');\n  return (\n    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 20, maxWidth: 1200, margin: '0 auto' }}>\n      <h1 style={{ margin: 0, fontFamily: 'var(--font-sans)', fontSize: 24, fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--text-primary)', letterSpacing: '-0.005em' }}>Results</h1>\n      <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(4, 1fr)', gap: 16 }}>\n        <MetricCard label=\"Pass Rate (7d)\" value=\"94.2%\" delta=\"+1.2%\" trend=\"up\" icon={<I.shield size={18} />} progress={94.2} />\n        <MetricCard label=\"Runs (7d)\" value=\"1,204\" icon={<I.refresh size={18} />} footnote=\"Across 42 suites\" />\n        <MetricCard label=\"Open Anomalies\" value=\"128\" delta=\"-14%\" trend=\"down\" icon={<I.alert size={18} />} footnote=\"warn + fail + critical\" />\n        <MetricCard label=\"Avg. Duration\" value=\"2.4h\" delta=\"-20m\" trend=\"down\" icon={<I.clock size={18} />} />\n      </div>\n      <div>\n        <Tabs value={tab} onChange={setTab} items={[{ key: 'runs', label: 'Runs' }, { key: 'pipelines', label: 'Pipeline runs' }]} />\n        <div style={{ marginTop: 16 }}>{tab === 'runs' ? <RunsTab onOpen={(run) => go('run-detail', { run })} /> : <PipelinesTab />}</div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nObject.assign(window, { ResultsScreen, RunDetailScreen });\n","contentType":"application/javascript","isBase64":false,"truncated":false}

Building a standalone table on `/results` now and rebuilding it on `/dashboard` in W6 is throwaway work; the dashboard also gates on the chart-library decision first. Deferred to build it **once, in its final home** — see Week 6.
239:- [x] ✅ Run now panel — suite picker, env / datasource, notification target — `RunNowPanel` (`components/runs/`): a **cross-suite** run launcher (modal off a **Run now** button in the Results header), distinct from the suite-detail **Run** button (#245) which runs one suite in context. A searchable suite `Select` filtered to the `owner`/`admin`/`edit` ladder the backend `POST /suites/{id}/run` enforces (view-only suites never appear); a read-only `Descriptions` readout of the picked suite's **env** (tag) + **datasource** (connection name + type) + **target** (one-line `catalog.schema.table` / flat-file `path`, mirroring `suiteTarget.ts`). A targetless suite blocks **Run** with an inline reason rather than a backend 422. On trigger it hands off to the shared `LiveRunProgress` drawer (modal closes; run watched + cancellable). **Notification target ships as a clearly-labelled disabled placeholder** ("coming in Week 6") — no notification backend exists yet (Teams/`ResultPublisher` alert routing is Week 6), so it doesn't jump the gun. Self-contained (owns its data fetch + progress drawer) so the Week-6 UI rework can drop it onto the dedicated Execution page unchanged. 3 component tests (no-runnable empty state, env/datasource/target render + trigger, targetless-suite block).
242:- [ ] 🔵 Recent runs audit table with drill-down link to results — **moved to Week 6** (counted there), folded into the Enhanced Monitoring Dashboard (`/dashboard`) where the prototype homes the recent-runs widget. Rationale in the group note above + the Week-6 task.

249	## Week 6 — Results dashboard + alerting
250	
251	**Exit gate:** Full results dashboard live across all source types; alerts firing with suppression.
252	
253	### Results dashboard (11 tasks — 2/11 ✅)
254	> **Build as an in-app React page** (the GX-Cloud-style redesign Phase C), **not Grafana** — reading Postgres directly would bypass DataQ's per-suite sharing + PII redaction; Grafana is deferred to an optional post-v1 ops add-on ([ADR 0018](adr/0018-results-surface-and-grafana-deferral.md), accepted). The Week-5 run-enablement backend it gates on is **done**: `runs.py` read endpoints (`GET /runs`, `GET /runs/{id}`, `GET /pipeline_runs`) + `POST /suites/{id}/run` (PR-C0b); [#215](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/215) target (PR-C0a). The `results.metric_value`/`duration_ms` columns were seam-built for these trend charts (ADR 0012).
255	- [x] ✅ **Results page scaffold** (PR-C1) — the in-app `/results` page + sidebar nav (Connections · Suites · **Results** · Profile). **Runs tab**: a runs table (suite name, status, triggered-by, started, duration) with a status filter, each row opening a **run-detail drawer** that drills into the per-check results (check name + expectation, severity tag, `metric_value`, observed value). **Pipeline runs tab**: the orchestration monitoring feed (`pipeline_runs`) with a provider filter. Pure presentation helpers (`resultsFormat.ts` — duration/timestamp formatters, status→colour maps) are unit-tested; `api/runs.ts` mirrors the C0b schemas. Demo seed (`demo_data.py`) now lands runs + results (pass/pass/warn/fail spread) + two pipeline-runs + suite targets so the page has real content. 10 vitest + 2 Playwright (`results.spec.ts`). _The rich widgets below build on this scaffold._
256	- [ ] ⬜ Health score stat cards + 7-day trend chart
257	- [ ] ⬜ Per-suite pass / fail progress bars — warn / fail / critical breakdown
258	- [ ] 🟡 Results filter bar — env, datasource type, suite, date range, status — _run **status** filter + pipeline **provider** filter shipped (PR-C1); env/datasource/suite/date-range pending_
259	- [ ] 🟡 Failed check drill-down — sample failing rows from GX result — _per-check drill-down shipped (PR-C1); **sample rows withheld** pending row-level PII redaction ([#226](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/226), ADR 0018)_
260	- [ ] ⬜ Per-check historical trend chart

262	- [ ] ⬜ Datasource type filter — Snowflake / flat file / Unity Catalog toggle

I've pulled the prototype and mapped it against our codebase. Here's where we stand.

## The prototype (claude.ai/design → "DataQ Design System")

Last updated 2026-06-18, `projectId 317fec67…`. The two screens that matter for Week 6:

- **`DashboardScreen.jsx`** — the *Enhanced Monitoring Dashboard* landing page we haven't built yet: a 4-up KPI row (Data Integrity Score / Total Anomalies / Active Connections / Avg. Time to Resolution), a dark **Quality Trends** bar chart (pass vs fail, 14d), a **Suite Performance** panel (per-suite health progress bars), and a **Recent Runs** table with row→run-detail drill-down.
- **`ResultsScreen.jsx`** — mirrors our shipped `/results`: 4 MetricCards + Runs / Pipeline-runs tabs, **plus** a routed **`RunDetailScreen`** (per-check results table, status/checks-passed/started/duration cards, and a **CSV/JSON download** menu).

One trap worth flagging: `uploads/DESIGN.md` is the **superseded teal** brief — progress.md explicitly says ignore it for palette. The real spec is `readme.md`, and the actual `.jsx` uses indigo (`--indigo-500`), matching our shipped `theme.ts`. So this is a **structure adoption, not a re-skin**.

## Where we actually stand — Week 6 is 2/23

What's shipped (PR-C1):
- ✅ [Results.tsx](frontend/src/pages/Results.tsx) scaffold — Runs + Pipeline-runs tabs, run-detail **drawer**, status/provider filters, severity badge colours in [resultsFormat.ts](frontend/src/components/results/resultsFormat.ts)
- 🟡 Partial: status/provider filters, per-check drill-down (sample rows withheld pending PII work [#226]), pipeline-runs feed

What the prototype shows but we **don't have**:

| Track | Gap |
|---|---|
| **Dashboard** | The entire `/dashboard` landing page — KPI cards, Quality Trends chart, Suite Performance bars, recent-runs table |
| **Results** | Run-detail is a **drawer** ([Results.tsx:54](frontend/src/pages/Results.tsx#L54)) — prototype wants a routed `/results/:runId` page ([#268]) with CSV/JSON download |
| **Nav/shell** | No Dashboard entry; login lands on `/connections` not `/dashboard` |
| **Edit flows** | Connection/Suite/Check edit are still drawers — prototype wants dedicated pages |
| **Error pages** | No `ErrorState`-based 4xx/5xx pages |
| **Alerting** | 0/6 — `ResultPublisher` seam, Teams config, suppression/dedup/routing — nothing started |

## The one real blocker

The **Enhanced Monitoring Dashboard is gated on a chart-library decision** (antd ships no charts → `@ant-design/charts` vs `recharts`, needs to clear `pnpm audit` + bundle-size and get an ADR-light note). That decision should come first since both the Quality Trends chart and the per-check historical trend depend on it.

Want me to start by making the chart-library call (ADR-light), or would you rather kick off with something that *isn't* chart-gated — the **nav/shell restructure** + **`/results/:runId` route** (promoting the drawer to a page with CSV/JSON download), which together unblock the recent-runs drill-down target?

{"method":"get_file","path":"readme.md","content":"# DataQ Design System\n\nA design system for **DataQ** — a single-tenant **data quality monitoring platform** built around Great Expectations (the v1). DataQ runs DQ checks across four datasources (Snowflake, ADLS Gen2, AWS S3, Unity Catalog/Databricks) and observes two orchestration providers (Azure Data Factory, Apache Airflow), surfacing health, runs and anomalies through an in-app React console.\n\nThis system captures the **shipped product's** visual language: an **Ant Design** foundation with an **indigo** primary on a soft-gray canvas, white surfaces, hairline borders, and a status model that mirrors DataQ's real severity tiers.\n\n> **Personality:** the \"Silent Expert\" — authoritative, precise, unobtrusive. Beauty comes from alignment, crisp hairlines and the rhythmic regularity of the grid, not decoration.\n\n---\n\n## Sources\n\nThis system was reconstructed from the real product, not invented. Explore these to go deeper:\n\n- **Codebase (source of truth):** [github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ) — React + Vite + Ant Design frontend (`frontend/src/`). The brand palette and shell live in `frontend/src/theme.ts` + `App.tsx`; the status/severity model in `frontend/src/components/results/resultsFormat.ts`; per-datasource visuals in `frontend/src/components/connections/connectionVisuals.tsx`. `CLAUDE.md` is the full project guide; `context/DataQ_platform_roadmap.md` the 8-week roadmap.\n- **Figma file:** the attached `DataQ.fig` (14 frames — landing page + console screens). Useful for layout reference. **Note:** the Figma explores a teal \"Enterprise Monitor\" direction that was *not* shipped — this system follows the **indigo Ant Design** app in code, per the project's actual theme.\n- **Uploaded spec:** `uploads/DESIGN.md` — an earlier design brief (teal-oriented); superseded by the shipped indigo theme but useful for spacing/typography intent.\n\nYou'll build more accurate DataQ designs by reading the repo above — particularly `theme.ts`, `resultsFormat.ts` and the page components under `frontend/src/pages/`.\n\n---\n\n## Tech stack (match it when writing production code)\n\n| Layer | Tech |\n| --- | --- |\n| Frontend | **React + Vite + Ant Design** (antd) + Monaco editor |\n| Auth | Azure AD (MSAL) |\n| Backend | FastAPI · Celery · Great Expectations · PostgreSQL · Redis |\n| DQ engine | Great Expectations (GX Core) v1 — every check is a GX *expectation* |\n\nWhen producing production code for DataQ, use **Ant Design components** and read tokens from `theme.ts` (don't hardcode hexes). The React components in this system are framework-free recreations for *prototypes and mocks* — they mirror the antd look so throwaway artifacts match the product, but production work should use real antd.\n\n---\n\n## Content fundamentals\n\n**Voice — the Silent Expert.** Authoritative, terse, technical. State facts and outcomes; never hype.\n\n- **Casing:** Title Case for page/section titles (\"Results\", \"Pipeline runs\", \"New Connection\"). Sentence case for descriptions and helper text. Status values are **lowercase** to match the data model and antd Tags (`pass`, `warn`, `fail`, `critical`, `succeeded`, `failed`).\n- **Person:** address the user as **you** (\"where DataQ sends your alerts\"); the product is **DataQ** in the third person. Avoid \"we\".\n- **Numbers & precision:** lead with the metric. \"94.2% pass rate\", \"128 anomalies\", \"2.4h avg. time to resolution\". Mono-format raw values, ids, schema paths and expectation names (`expect_column_values_to_not_be_null`, `Snowflake_Prod_01`, `prod.analytics.fct_revenue`).\n- **No emoji**, no exclamation marks, no cute error copy. A failure says `Failed (Err 504)` or `Task retry limit exceeded`, not \"Oops!\".\n- **Examples:**\n  - ✅ \"Identify anomalies before they propagate downstream.\"\n  - ✅ \"42 anomalies detected. View details.\"\n  - ❌ \"Supercharge your data with revolutionary AI! 🚀\"\n  - ❌ \"Looks like something went a little wonky.\"\n\n---\n\n## Visual foundations\n\n**Color.** Indigo (`#4f46e5`, indigo-600) is the single brand accent — buttons, active nav, links, selected state. Everything else is a **soft-gray neutral system**: a `#f4f5f7` canvas behind white (`#ffffff`) surfaces, hairline borders at `#e6e8eb`, ink text at `#1f2430`. The \"selected\" tint is one pale indigo (`#eef0fe`) used everywhere (nav, rows). Restraint is the point — color earns attention, so it's spent only on the accent and on status.\n\n**Status / severity.** DataQ's result-severity model (ADR 0005/0016) maps to Ant Design Tag hues: `pass` → green, `warn` → gold, `fail` → red, `critical` → **magenta** (the top tier), `skip` → gray, `error` → volcano. Run/pipeline execution uses `queued` (gray), `running` (blue), `succeeded` (green), `failed` (red), `cancelled` (gold). Always pair color with a label or dot — never color alone (color-blind safe). Datasource cards carry per-source brand accents (Snowflake `#29b5e8`, ADLS/ADF `#0078d4`, S3 `#ff9900`, Unity Catalog `#ff3621`, Airflow `#017cee`).\n\n**Typography.** **Inter** for all UI and prose, **14px base** (Ant Design). Headlines use Ant's Title scale; page titles are level-3 (24px, semibold). **JetBrains Mono** for anything machine: SQL, schema paths, connection ids, expectation types, ids and metric values. Headings get slightly tight tracking; body stays neutral.\n\n**Spacing & layout.** 8pt rhythm with 4px micro-increments. Content padding 24px; card padding 20–24px; gaps of 16/24. A light fixed sider (220px) + white header (56px) frame a scrolling content canvas. Max content width \\~1200px, centered.\n\n**Shape & elevation.** Soft, Ant-aligned radii: **8px** on controls (buttons, inputs, menu items), **12px** on cards and drawers, full pills for status. Depth is **flat** — 1px hairline borders define cards at rest; soft, neutral shadows appear only on hover (cards lift \\~1px with a diffused shadow) and on overlays/drawers. No heavy drop shadows, no gradients.\n\n**Surfaces & cards.** White, 1px `#e6e8eb` border, 12px radius, no shadow by default. Interactive cards gain a soft ambient shadow + 1px lift on hover. Tables are the workhorse: tinted header row (`#fafbfc`), horizontal dividers only (no vertical borders), subtle `#fafbfc` row hover.\n\n**Motion.** Quiet and quick — 120–180ms ease on background, color, border and shadow. Hover = subtle background/shadow shift; press = 0.5px nudge. No bounces, no infinite decorative loops. Live/running states may pulse a dot or animate an indeterminate progress stripe.\n\n**Focus.** Keyboard focus = a 2px indigo ring (with offset) or a soft indigo halo on inputs — high-visibility, never removed.\n\n**Dark mode (planned post-v1).** v1 ships **light only**, but the system is built dark-ready. The foundation lives in `tokens/dark.css`: a `[data-theme=\"dark\"]` scope that remaps the **semantic layer** (surfaces, text, borders, status tints, elevation) to a deep-slate palette — set `<html data-theme=\"dark\">` (or scope it to any subtree) and the whole UI flips with **zero component changes**. This works because of one contract every component already follows: **reference semantic tokens** (`--bg-card`, `--text-primary`, `--border`, `--surface-*`, `--status-*-bg`, …), never raw scale hexes. The only hardcoded `#fff` allowed is text/dots/knobs sitting *on top of* a brand or status fill (white on the indigo button, white on a solid status pill) — those stay correct in both themes. Dark surfaces are deep slate (`#0d1117` canvas, `#161b22` cards), not pure black; status hues lift for AA contrast (pass `#3fb950`, fail `#f85149`, critical `#db61a2`) over darkened tint backgrounds; depth shifts from hairline borders to deeper shadows. An optional `prefers-color-scheme` auto-switch is included (commented out) for when the team opts in. When you build new components, keep using the semantic tokens and they inherit dark mode for free.\n\n**Imagery.** The product is chrome-and-data; it leans on its own UI (dashboards, tables, charts) rather than photography. A faint yin-yang watermark (5% opacity) sits bottom-right of the content area.\n\n---\n\n## Iconography\n\nDataQ uses **line icons** from **Ant Design Icons**. The shipped app uses **Ant Design Icons** (`@ant-design/icons` — `CloudOutlined`, `DeploymentUnitOutlined`, `NodeIndexOutlined`, `TableOutlined`, `FolderOpenOutlined`, `InboxOutlined`, etc.), tinted with each datasource's brand color on a soft 12%-alpha tile (see `connectionVisuals.tsx`).\n\n- **In this system's recreations**, icons are the real **Ant Design Icons** (outlined set, MIT-licensed, from `@ant-design/icons` / `ant-design/ant-design-icons`) — the exact glyphs the shipped DataQ app renders. They're inlined (1024 viewBox, filled `currentColor` paths) in `templates/app/Icons.jsx` so screens stay self-contained, exposed as `window.Icon.<name>`. Datasource tiles tint each icon with the source's brand color (see `connectionVisuals.tsx`).\n- **No emoji** as UI icons. No multicolor/filled icon systems. Unicode glyphs appear only as incidental marks (e.g. the datasource specimen card).\n- The **brand mark** is the two-tone indigo **yin-yang** glyph (`assets/logo/dataq-mark.svg`) — it nods to DataQ's pass/fail, expected/observed duality. Use the lockup (`dataq-logo.svg`) in headers, the standalone mark at small sizes, the reversed lockup on dark backgrounds.\n\n---\n\n## What's in here (manifest)\n\n**Foundations**\n\n- `styles.css` — global entry point (import this one file). It `@import`s everything below.\n- `tokens/colors.css` · `typography.css` · `spacing.css` · `elevation.css` · `fonts.css` — CSS custom properties + webfonts. **Inter** and **JetBrains Mono** are self-hosted as variable TTFs in `assets/fonts/` (SIL OFL 1.1) via `@font-face`.\n- `guidelines/*.card.html` — foundation specimen cards (Colors, Type, Spacing, Brand) shown in the Design System tab.\n\n**Components** (`components/<group>/` — React, framework-free, styled via tokens)\n\n- `buttons/` — **Button**, **IconButton**\n- `forms/` — **Input**, **Select**, **Checkbox**, **Switch**\n- `feedback/` — **StatusBadge** (severity + run status), **Tag**, **ProgressBar**, **Avatar**, **ErrorState** (4xx/5xx)\n- `data/` — **Card**, **MetricCard**, **DataTable**\n- `navigation/` — **SegmentedControl**\n- `overlay/` — **Drawer** (right-side panel for forms & detail)\n\n**Templates** (`templates/<slug>/` — copy-and-go starting points; shown in the Templates picker)\n\n- `templates/app/` — interactive DataQ console: a light Ant Design shell (white header + light sider + gray canvas). **Login lands on the Dashboard** (Enhanced Monitoring — KPIs, Quality Trends, Suite Performance, Recent Runs). Sidebar: **Dashboard · Connections · Suites · Results · Profile**; footer: **Admin · Settings · Documentation** (placeholder). A demo \"View as\" role switch (user menu) gates **Admin** behind a **403** for non-admins.\n  - **Full-page flows** (sub-routed like the Figma frames, with back-nav): New Connection → *Select Source* (categorized — **Orchestration** first, then Warehouses/Lakehouses/Cloud Storage) → *Configuration* (spec-driven, `ConnectionFlow.jsx`); New Suite → *Details/Source* (`SuiteFlow.jsx`) → *Define Rules* (`CheckFlow.jsx`); Results → click a run → *Run detail* with per-check results + CSV/JSON download (`ResultsScreen.jsx`). Suites also have a **Share** drawer (`ShareFlow.jsx` — invite by email with view/edit permission). Forms driven by the real `connectionFormSpec.ts` / `expectationCatalog.ts`.\n- `templates/marketing/` — the DataQ landing page: sticky nav, hero (with a live product preview built from real components), a features bento, a social-proof strip, a dark CTA closer, and a footer. Sections in `MarketingNav.jsx`, `Hero.jsx`, `Features.jsx`, `MarketingClose.jsx`.\n- `templates/error/` — standard **4xx & 5xx** HTTP error pages in the app shell, switchable across 400/401/403/404/429/500/502/503/504, built on the `ErrorState` component.\n- Each template loads the system through a sibling **`ds-base.js`** — a consuming project repoints one `base` line at the bound `_ds/<folder>` tree.\n\n**Assets**\n\n- `assets/logo/` — yin-yang mark, primary lockup, reversed lockup, app mark.\n\n**Skill**\n\n- `SKILL.md` — makes this folder usable as an Agent Skill in Claude Code.\n\n---\n\n## Using it\n\nLink the one stylesheet and read components off the compiled bundle:\n\n```html\n<link rel=\"stylesheet\" href=\"styles.css\">\n<script src=\"_ds_bundle.js\"></script>\n<script>\n  const { Button, StatusBadge, DataTable } = window.DataQDesignSystem_317fec;\n</script>\n```\n\nFor **mocks and prototypes**, copy assets out and build static HTML using these components. For **production DataQ work**, use real Ant Design + read `theme.ts`, and treat this system as the reference for palette, status semantics, voice and layout.\n","contentType":"text/markdown","isBase64":false,"truncated":false}
```

{"method":"get_file","path":"templates/app/index.html","content":"<!-- @template name=\"DataQ Monitoring Console\" description=\"The DataQ product shell — light Ant Design layout with a Dashboard landing, Connections, Suites, Results, Profile, Admin (403-gated) & Settings, plus full-page New-Connection, New-Suite, Add-Check and Run-detail flows.\" -->\n<!doctype html>\n<html lang=\"en\">\n<head>\n<meta charset=\"utf-8\">\n<meta name=\"viewport\" content=\"width=device-width, initial-scale=1\">\n<title>DataQ — Quality Monitoring</title>\n<style>\n  * { box-sizing: border-box; }\n  html, body { margin: 0; height: 100%; font-family: var(--font-sans); color: var(--text-body); background: var(--bg-app); }\n  #root { height: 100%; }\n</style>\n<script src=\"https://unpkg.com/react@18.3.1/umd/react.development.js\" integrity=\"sha384-hD6/REDACTED/6onKUg4lLsHAs9EBPT82L\" crossorigin=\"anonymous\"></script>\n<script src=\"https://unpkg.com/react-dom@18.3.1/umd/react-dom.development.js\" integrity=\"REDACTED\" crossorigin=\"anonymous\"></script>\n<script src=\"https://unpkg.com/@babel/standalone@7.29.0/babel.min.js\" integrity=\"sha384-m08KidiNqLdpJqLq95G/LEi8Qvjl/REDACTED\" crossorigin=\"anonymous\"></script>\n<script src=\"./ds-base.js\"></script>\n<script type=\"text/babel\" src=\"Icons.jsx\"></script>\n<script type=\"text/babel\" src=\"AppShell.jsx\"></script>\n<script type=\"text/babel\" src=\"ConnectionFlow.jsx\"></script>\n<script type=\"text/babel\" src=\"CheckFlow.jsx\"></script>\n<script type=\"text/babel\" src=\"SuiteFlow.jsx\"></script>\n<script type=\"text/babel\" src=\"ShareFlow.jsx\"></script>\n<script type=\"text/babel\" src=\"DashboardScreen.jsx\"></script>\n<script type=\"text/babel\" src=\"ResultsScreen.jsx\"></script>\n<script type=\"text/babel\" src=\"ConnectionsScreen.jsx\"></script>\n<script type=\"text/babel\" src=\"SuitesScreen.jsx\"></script>\n<script type=\"text/babel\" src=\"ProfileScreen.jsx\"></script>\n<script type=\"text/babel\" src=\"AdminScreen.jsx\"></script>\n<script type=\"text/babel\" src=\"SettingsScreen.jsx\"></script>\n</head>\n<body>\n<div id=\"root\"></div>\n<script type=\"text/babel\" data-presets=\"react\">\n  function App() {\n    const W = window;\n    const { AppShell } = W;\n    const { ErrorState } = W.DataQDesignSystem_317fec;\n    // nav = { name, params }. go(name, params) navigates; back links call go(parent).\n    const [nav, setNav] = React.useState({ name: 'dashboard', params: {} });\n    const [role, setRole] = React.useState('Admin');\n    const go = React.useCallback((name, params = {}) => {\n      setNav({ name, params });\n      const main = document.querySelector('main');\n      if (main) main.scrollTop = 0;\n    }, []);\n\n    const SCREENS = {\n      dashboard: W.DashboardScreen,\n      connections: W.ConnectionsScreen,\n      'connection-source': W.ConnectionSourceScreen,\n      'connection-config': W.ConnectionConfigScreen,\n      suites: W.SuitesScreen,\n      'suite-new': W.NewSuiteScreen,\n      'suite-check': W.CheckScreen,\n      results: W.ResultsScreen,\n      'run-detail': W.RunDetailScreen,\n      profile: W.ProfileScreen,\n      admin: W.AdminScreen,\n      settings: W.SettingsScreen,\n    };\n\n    let content;\n    if (nav.name === 'admin' && role !== 'Admin') {\n      // Admin Control Center is gated — non-admins get a 403.\n      content = (\n        <ErrorState tone=\"client\" code=\"403\"\n          title=\"Access denied\"\n          message=\"The Admin Control Center is restricted to workspace admins. Ask an admin to grant you the role, or head back to your dashboard.\"\n          icon={<W.Icon.lock size={30} />}\n          primaryAction={{ label: 'Back to Dashboard', onClick: () => go('dashboard') }}\n          secondaryAction={{ label: 'View your Profile', onClick: () => go('profile') }} />\n      );\n    } else {\n      const Screen = SCREENS[nav.name] || W.DashboardScreen;\n      content = <Screen go={go} params={nav.params} />;\n    }\n\n    return (\n      <AppShell route={nav.name} go={go} role={role} setRole={setRole}>\n        {content}\n      </AppShell>\n    );\n  }\n  ReactDOM.createRoot(document.getElementById('root')).render(<App />);\n</script>\n</body>\n</html>\n","contentType":"text/html","isBase64":false,"truncated":false}

rounded inset\n// menu items, soft-gray content canvas, faint yin-yang watermark.\nconst { Avatar } = window.DataQDesignSystem_317fec;\n\nconst NAV = [\n  { key: 'dashboard', label: 'Dashboard', icon: { key: 'profile', label: 'Profile', icon: 'user' },\n];\n\n// Sub-pages highlight their parent section in the sidebar.\nconst SECTION = {\n  dashboard: 'dashboard',\n  connections: 'connections', 'profile', admin: 'admin', settings: 'settings',\n};\n\n// Shared full-page header: optional back link (breadcrumb), title, subtitle, actions.\nfunction PageHeader({ backLabel, onBack, title, gap: 6, background: 'none', border: 'none', cursor: 'pointer',\n          padding: '2px 2px 8px', color: 'var(--text-secondary)', fontFamily: 'var(--font-sans)', fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 500,\n        }}>\n          <window.Icon.chevronLeft size={15} /> {backLabel}\n        </button>\n      )}\n      <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'flex-start', gap: 16 }}>\n        <div>\n          <h1 style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: 24, fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--text-primary)', letterSpacing: '-0.005em' }}>{title}</h1>\n          {subtitle && <div style={{ fontSize: 14, color: 'var(--text-secondary)', marginTop: 4 }}>{subtitle}</div>}\n        </div>\n        {actions && <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8, flex: 'none' }}>{actions}</div>}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction NavItem({ item, active, onClick }) {\n  const [hover, setHover] = React.useState(false);\n  const I = window.Icon[item.icon];\n  return (\n    <button\n      onClick={onClick}\n      onMouseEnter={() => setHover(true)}\n      onMouseLeave={() => setHover(false)}\n      style={{\n        display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12, width: '100%',\n        height: 'var(--menu-item-height)', padding: '0 12px', margin: 0,\n        border: 'none', borderRadius: 'var(--radius)', cursor: 'pointer', textAlign: 'left',\n        fontFamily: 'var(--font-sans)', fontSize: 14, fontWeight: active ? 600 : 500,\n        color: active ? 'var(--indigo-600)' : 'var(--text-body)',\n        background: active ? 'var(--bg-selected)' : hover ? 'var(--bg-hover)' : 'transparent',\n        transition: 'background .15s, color .15s',\n      }}\n    >\n      <span style={{ color: active ? 'var(--indigo-600)' : 'var(--gray-500)', display: 'inline-flex' }}><I size={18} /></span>\n      {item.label}\n    </button>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction Sidebar({ route, go }) {\n  const section = SECTION[route] || route;\n  return (\n    <aside style={{\n      width: 'var(--sidebar-width)', flex: 'none', background: 'var(--bg-sider)',\n      borderRight: '1px solid var(--border)', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column',\n      height: '100%', boxSizing: 'border-box', paddingTop: 8,\n    }}>\n      <nav style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 4, padding: '8px' }}>\n        {NAV.map((n) => <NavItem key={n.key} item={n} active={section === n.key} onClick={() => go(n.key)} />)}\n      </nav>\n      <div style={{ flex: 1 }} />\n      <div style={{ padding: '8px', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 4, borderTop: '1px solid var(--divider)' }}>\n        <NavItem item={{ label: 'Admin', icon: 'shield' }} active={section === 'admin'} onClick={() => go('admin')} />\n        <NavItem item={{ label: 'Settings', icon: 'settings' }} active={section === 'settings'} onClick={() => go('settings')} />\n        <NavItem item={{ label: 'Documentation', icon: 'docs' }} onClick={() => {}} />\n      </div>\n    </aside>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction UserMenu({ role, setRole }) {\n  const [open, setOpen] = React.useState(false);\n  const roles = ['Admin', 'Viewer'];\n  return (\n    <div style={{ position: 'relative' }}>\n      <button\n        onClick={() => setOpen((o) => !o)}\n        style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8, background: 'none', border: 'none', cursor: 'pointer', padding: '4px 6px', borderRadius: 'var(--radius)' }}\n      >\n        <Avatar name=\"Jane Doe\" size={28} />\n        <span style={{ fontSize: 14, color: 'var(--text-primary)' }}>Jane Doe</span>\n        <span style={{ color: 'var(--gray-500)', display: 'inline-flex' }}><window.Icon.chevronRight size={12} style={{ transform: 'rotate(90deg)' }} /></span>\n      </button>\n      {open && (\n        <div style={{\n          position: 'absolute', right: 0, top: 'calc(100% + 6px)', width: 232, zIndex: 20,\n          background: 'var(--bg-card)', borderRadius: 'var(--radius-lg)', boxShadow: 'var(--shadow-overlay)',\n          border: '1px solid var(--border)', padding: 6,\n        }}>\n          <div style={{ padding: '8px 10px' }}>\n            <div style={{ fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--text-primary)' }}>Jane Doe</div>\n            <div style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>jane.doe@acme.com</div>\n          </div>\n          <div style={{ height: 1, background: 'var(--divider)', margin: '4px 0' }} />\n          <div style={{ padding: '8px 10px' }}>\n            <div style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 600, letterSpacing: '.05em', textTransform: 'uppercase', color: 'var(--text-muted)', marginBottom: 8 }}>View as (demo)</div>\n            <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 4 }}>\n              {roles.map((r) => (\n                <button key={r} onClick={() => { setRole && setRole(r); }} style={{\n                  flex: 1, height: 28, border: '1px solid var(--border)', borderRadius: 'var(--radius)', cursor: 'pointer',\n                  fontFamily: 'var(--font-sans)', fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600,\n                  background: role === r ? 'var(--bg-selected)' : 'transparent',\n                  color: role === r ? 'var(--indigo-600)' : 'var(--text-body)',\n                  borderColor: role === r ? 'var(--indigo-200)' : 'var(--border)',\n                }}>{r}</button>\n              ))}\n            </div>\n          </div>\n          <div style={{ height: 1, background: 'var(--divider)', 14, color: 'var(--text-muted)', textAlign: 'left' }}>Sign out</button>\n        </div>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction Header({ role, setRole }) {\n  return (\n    <header style={{\n      display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 16, padding: '0 24px',\n      height: 'var(--header-height)', flex: 'none',\n      background: 'var(--bg-header)', borderBottom: '1px solid var(--border)',\n    }}>\n      <div style={{ flex: 1, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10 }}>\n        <img src=\"../../assets/logo/dataq-mark.svg\" width=\"28\" height=\"28\" alt=\"\" />\n        <span style={{ fontFamily: 'var(--font-sans)', fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 17, color: 'var(--ink)', letterSpacing: '-0.01em' }}>DataQ</span>\n      </div>\n      <UserMenu role={role} setRole={setRole} />\n    </header>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction Watermark() {\n  return (\n    <div aria-hidden style={{ position: 'absolute', inset: 0, overflow: 'hidden', pointerEvents: 'none' }}>\n      <img src=\"../../assets/logo/dataq-mark.svg\" style={{ position: 'absolute', right: -90, bottom: -90, width: 360, opacity: 0.05 }} alt=\"\" />\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction AppShell({ route, go, );\n}\n\nObject.assign(window, { AppShell, PageHeader });\n","contentType":"application/javascript","isBase64":false,"truncated":false}

unity_catalog: { icon: 'layers',  accent: 'var(--src-unity-catalog)' },\n  adf:           { icon: 'orchestration', accent: 'var(--src-adf)' },\n  airflow:       { icon: 'airflow', accent: 'var(--src-airflow)' },\n};\n\nconst KIND = { adf: 'Orchestration', airflow: 'Orchestration' };\n\nconst CONNECTIONS = [\n  { name: 'ADF_Prod_Orchestrator', type: 'adf', label: 'Azure Data Factory', env: 'PROD', status: 'healthy', sync: '1m ago' },\n  { name: 'Airflow_Analytics', type: 'airflow', label: 'Apache Airflow', env: 'PROD', status: 'healthy', sync: '4m ago' },\n  { name: 'Snowflake_Prod_01', type: 'snowflake', label: 'Snowflake', env: 'PROD', status: 'healthy', sync: '2m ago' },\n  { name: 'AWS_S3_Landing', type: 's3', label: 'AWS S3', env: 'PROD', status: 'healthy', sync: '5m ago' },\n  { name: 'Databricks_UC_Main', type: 'unity_catalog', label: 'Unity Catalog', env: 'UAT', status: 'warn', sync: '18m ago' },\n  { name: 'ADLS_Gen2_Archive', type: 'adls_gen2', label: 'ADLS Gen2', env: 'DEV', status: 'failed', sync: '3h ago' },\n];\n\nfunction ConnectionCard({ c }) {\n  const { Card, StatusBadge, Tag } = DQc;\n  const src = SOURCES[c.type];\n  const I = window.Icon[src.icon];\n  return (\n    <Card interactive padding={20}>\n      <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'flex-start', justifyContent: 'space-between' }}>\n        <div style={{\n          width: 44, height: 44, borderRadius: 10, flex: 'none',\n          background: `color-mix(in srgb, ${src.accent} 12%, #fff)`, color: src.accent,\n          display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',\n        }}><I size={22} /></div>\n        <StatusBadge status={c.status} variant=\"soft\" />\n      </div>\n      <div style={{ marginTop: 16, fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--text-primary)', fontFamily: 'var(--font-mono)' }}>{c.name}</div>\n      <div style={{ marginTop: 4, fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>{c.label}{KIND[c.type] ? ` · ${KIND[c.type]}` : ''}</div>\n      <div style={{ marginTop: 16, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between' }}>\n        <Tag tone={c.env === 'PROD' ? 'indigo' : 'neutral'}>{c.env}</Tag>\n        <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>Synced {c.sync}</span>\n      </div>\n    </Card>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction ConnectionsScreen({ go }) {\n  const { Button } = DQc;\n  return (\n    <div style={{ maxWidth: 1200, margin: '0 auto' }}>\n      <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: 20 }}>\n        <div>\n          <h1 style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: 24, fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--text-primary)' }}>Connections</h1>\n          <div style={{ fontSize: 14, color: 'var(--text-secondary)', marginTop: 4 }}>6 connections · <span style={{ color: 'var(--status-pass)', fontWeight: 600 }}>4 healthy</span> · 2 orchestration</div>\n        </div>\n        <Button variant=\"primary\" iconLeft={<window.Icon.plus size={15} />} onClick={() => go('connection-source')}>New Connection</Button>\n      </div>\n      <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(3, 1fr)', gap: 16 }}>\n        {CONNECTIONS.map((c) => <ConnectionCard key={c.name} c={c} />)}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nObject.assign(window, { ConnectionsScreen });\n","contentType":"application/javascript","isBase64":false,"truncated":false}

name: 'subscription_id', label: 'Subscription ID' }, { name: 'resource_group', label: 'Resource group' },\n      { name: 'factory_name', label: 'Factory name' }, { name: 'tenant_id', label: 'Tenant }, { name: 'username', label: 'Username' }],\n    auth: [\n      { value: 'password', label: 'Password', secretLabel: 'Password' },\n      { value: 'api_token', label: 'API token', secretLabel: 'Bearer token' },\n    ],\n  },\n};\n\nfunction SourceCard({ type, onPick }) {\n  const { Card } = DQconn;\n  const m = SOURCE_META[type];\n  const I = window.Icon[m.icon];\n  return (\n    <Card interactive padding={20} onClick={() => onPick(type)}>\n      <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 14 }}>\n        <div style={{ width: 48, height: 48, borderRadius: 12, flex: 'none', background: `color-mix(in srgb, ${m.accent} 12%, #fff)`, color: m.accent, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}><I size={24} /></div>\n        <div style={{ flex: 1 }}>\n          <div style={{ fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--text-primary)' }}>{m.label}</div>\n          <div style={{ fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>{m.blurb}</div>\n        </div>\n        <span style={{ color: 'var(--gray-400)', display: 'inline-flex' }}><window.Icon.chevronRight size={18} /></span>\n      </div>\n    </Card>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction ConnectionSourceScreen({ go }) {\n  const { PageHeader } = window;\n  const byCat = CATEGORY_ORDER.map((cat) => ({ cat, types: Object.keys(SOURCE_META).filter((t) => SOURCE_META[t].cat === cat) }));\n  return (\n    <div style={{ maxWidth: 880, margin: '0 auto' }}>\n      <PageHeader backLabel=\"Connections\" onBack={() => go('connections')}\n        title=\"New Connection\" subtitle=\"Select a source or orchestration provider to connect\" />\n      <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 28 }}>\n        {byCat.map(({ cat, types }) => (\n          <div key={cat}>\n            <div style={{ fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 600, letterSpacing: '.05em', textTransform: 'uppercase', color: 'var(--text-muted)', marginBottom: CATEGORY_NOTE[cat] ? 6 : 12 }}>{cat}</div>\n            {CATEGORY_NOTE[cat] && <div style={{ fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--text-secondary)', marginBottom: 12, maxWidth: 560 }}>{CATEGORY_NOTE[cat]}</div>}\n            <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: 14 }}>\n              {types.map((t) => <SourceCard key={t} type={t} onPick={(type) => go('connection-config', { type })} />)}\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        ))}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction ConnectionConfigScreen({ go, params }) {\n  const { Card, Input, Select, Button } = DQconn;\n  const { PageHeader } = window;\n  const type = (params && params.type) || 'snowflake';\n  const spec = FORM_SPECS[type];\n  const m = SOURCE_META[type];\n  const [authType, setAuthType] = React.useState(spec.auth ? spec.auth[0].value : null);\n  const [testState, setTestState] = React.useState(null);\n  const auth = spec.auth ? spec.auth.find((a) => a.value === authType) : null;\n  const secretLabel = auth ? auth.secretLabel : spec.secretLabel;\n\n  return (\n    <div style={{ maxWidth: 720, margin: '0 auto' }}>\n      <PageHeader backLabel=\"Select source\" onBack={() => go('connection-source')}\n        title=\"Configure Connection\" subtitle={`${m.label} · ${m.cat}`} />\n      <Card padding={24}>\n        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12, padding: '12px 14px', background: 'var(--bg-subtle)', borderRadius: 'var(--radius)', border: '1px solid var(--border)', marginBottom: 20 }}>\n          <div style={{ width: 34, height: 34, borderRadius: 8, background: `color-mix(in srgb, ${m.accent} 12%, #fff)`, color: m.accent, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}>{React.createElement(window.Icon[m.icon], { size: 18 })}</div>\n          <span style={{ fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--text-primary)' }}>{m.label}</span>\n        </div>\n\n        <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: 16 }}>\n          <Input label=\"Connection name\" placeholder={`${m.label}_Prod_01`} mono style={{ gridColumn: '1 / -1' }} />\n          {spec.textFields.map((f) => (\n            <Input key={f.name} label={f.label + (f.optional ? ' (optional)' : '')} placeholder={f.name} mono />\n          ))}\n          {spec.auth && (\n            <Select label=\"Authentication\" options={spec.auth.map((a) => ({ value: a.value, label: a.label }))} value={authType} onChange={(e) => setAuthType(e.target.value)} />\n          )}\n          <Input label={secretLabel} type=\"password\" placeholder=\"••••••••\"\n            hint={auth && auth.multilineSecret ? 'Paste the full PEM block.' : 'Stored encrypted in Azure Key Vault.'}\n            style={{ gridColumn: spec.auth ? 'auto' : '1 / -1' }} />\n        </div>\n\n        {testState && (\n          <div style={{ marginTop: 16, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 500, color: testState === 'ok' ? 'var(--status-pass)' : 'var(--text-muted)' }}>\n            {testState === 'ok' ? '✓ Connection succeeded · 12 schemas reachable' : 'Testing connection…'}\n          </div>\n        )}\n\n        <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'flex-end', gap: 10, marginTop: 24, paddingTop: 20, borderTop: '1px solid var(--divider)' }}>\n          <Button variant=\"secondary\" onClick={() => { setTestState('testing'); setTimeout(() => setTestState('ok'), 700); }}>Test Connection</Button>\n          <Button variant=\"primary\" onClick={() => go('connections')}>Save Connection</Button>\n        </div>\n      </Card>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nObject.assign(window, { ConnectionSourceScreen, ConnectionConfigScreen });\n","contentType":"application/javascript","isBase64":false,"truncated":false}

'var(--indigo-700)' : 'var(--text-primary)' }}>{s.name}</span>\n        <StatusBadge status={s.last} variant=\"soft\" />\n      </div>\n      <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--text-muted)', fontFamily: 'var(--font-mono)' }}>{s.target}</span>\n    </button>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction SuiteDetail({ s, go }) {\n  const { Card, DataTable, StatusBadge, Tag, Button } = DQs;\n  const [shareOpen, },\n    { key: 'kind', header: 'Kind', width: 110, render: (v) => <Tag tone=\"outline\">{v}</Tag> },\n    { key: 'status', header: 'Last', width: 110, render: (v) => <StatusBadge status={v} <Button variant=\"ghost\" size=\"sm\" iconLeft={<window.Icon.share size={13} />} onClick={() => setShareOpen(true)}>Share</Button>\n          <Button variant=\"secondary\" size=\"sm\" onClick={() => s={s} active={s.id === sel} onClick={() => setSel(s.id)} />)}\n          </div>\n        </DQs.Card>\n        <SuiteDetail s={suite} go={go} />\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nObject.assign(window, { SuitesScreen });\n","contentType":"application/javascript","isBase64":false,"truncated":false}

Mirrors the Figma \"new suite details /\n// source\" frame: name + description, datasource connection, target dataset,\n// schedule. On create, continues to Define Rules (Add Check). Exports\n// window.NewSuiteScreen.\nconst DQsuite = window.DataQDesignSystem_317fec;\n\nconst CONNECTIONS = [\n  'Snowflake_Prod_01', 'AWS_S3_Landing', 'Databricks_UC_Main',\n  'Postgres_EU_West', 'ADLS_Gen2_Archive',\n];\n\nfunction NewSuiteScreen({ go }) {\n  const { Card, Button, Input, Select } = DQsuite;\n  const { PageHeader } = window;\n  const [conn, setConn] = React.useState(CONNECTIONS[0]);\n  const [schedule, setSchedule] = React.useState('Hourly');\n  const [name, setName] = React.useState('');\n\n  return (\n    <div style={{ maxWidth: 720, margin: '0 auto' }}>\n      <PageHeader backLabel=\"Suites\" onBack={() => go('suites')}\n        title=\"New Suite\" subtitle=\"Define a validation suite, then add checks\" />\n      <Card padding={24}>\n        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 16 }}>\n          <Input label=\"Suite name\" placeholder=\"Daily Revenue Audit\" value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} />\n          <Input label=\"Description (optional)\" placeholder=\"What this suite validates and why.\" />\n\n          <div style={{ height: 1, background: 'var(--divider)', margin: '2px 0' }} />\n          <div style={{ fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 600, letterSpacing: '.04em', textTransform: 'uppercase', color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>Source</div>\n\n          <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: 16 }}>\n            <Select label=\"Connection\" options={CONNECTIONS} value={conn} onChange={(e) => setConn(e.target.value)}\n              hint=\"Pick from your configured datasources.\" />\n            <Select label=\"Schedule\" value={schedule} onChange={(e) => setSchedule(e.target.value)}\n              options={['On data arrival', 'Hourly', 'Daily 02:00', 'Weekly (Mon)', 'Manual only']}\n              hint=\"Or trigger from an ADF / Airflow run.\" />\n          </div>\n          <Input label=\"Dataset / table\" placeholder=\"prod.analytics.fct_revenue\" mono\n            hint=\"Fully-qualified table, file glob, or view the suite runs against.\" />\n        </div>\n\n        <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10, marginTop: 24, paddingTop: 20, borderTop: '1px solid var(--divider)' }}>\n          <span style={{ fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>Next: define the suite's checks.</span>\n          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 10 }}>\n            <Button variant=\"secondary\" onClick={() => go('suites')}>Cancel</Button>\n            <Button variant=\"primary\" onClick={() => go('suite-check', { suiteName: name || 'New Suite' })}>Create &amp; Add Checks</Button>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      </Card>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nObject.assign(window, { NewSuiteScreen });\n","contentType":"application/javascript","isBase64":false,"truncated":false}

{"method":"get_file","path":"templates/app/CheckFlow.jsx","content":"// DataQ — Add Check / Define Rules (full-page). Mirrors the Figma \"new suite\n// define rules\" frame: pick a GX expectation, fill spec-driven config, set a\n// severity tier, dry-run a preview. From expectationCatalog.ts. Exports\n// window.CheckScreen.\nconst DQchk = window.DataQDesignSystem_317fec;\n\nconst CATALOG = [\n  { type: 'expect_column_values_to_not_be_null', label: 'Column values not null', category: 'Column values', desc: 'Every value in the column is non-null.', fields: [{ name: 'column', label: 'Column', type: 'string' }] },\n  { type: 'expect_column_values_to_be_unique', label: 'Column values unique', category: 'Column values', desc: 'Values in the column are distinct.', fields: [{ name: 'column', label: 'Column', type: 'string' }] },\n  { type: 'expect_column_values_to_be_between', label: 'Column values in range', category: 'Column values', desc: 'Numeric values fall within [min, max].', fields: [{ name: 'column', label: 'Column', type: 'string' }, { name: 'min_value', label: 'Minimum', type: 'number', optional: true }, { name: 'max_value', label: 'Maximum', type: 'number', optional: true }] },\n  { type: 'expect_column_values_to_be_in_set', label: 'Column values in set', category: 'Column values', desc: 'Every value is one of an allowed set.', fields: [{ name: 'column', label: 'Column', type: 'string' }, { name: 'value_set', label: 'Allowed values', type: 'list', help: 'Comma-separated list of permitted values.' }] },\n  { type: 'expect_column_values_to_match_regex', label: 'Column values match regex', category: 'Column values', desc: 'Every value matches the regex.', fields: [{ name: 'column', label: 'Column', type: 'string' }, { name: 'regex', label: 'Regex', type: 'string' }] },\n  { type: 'expect_table_row_count_to_be_between', label: 'Table row count in range', category: 'Table shape', desc: 'The table row count falls within [min, max].', fields: [{ name: 'min_value', label: 'Minimum rows', type: 'number', optional: true }, { name: 'max_value', label: 'Maximum rows', type: 'number', optional: true }] },\n];\nconst CATEGORIES = ['Column values', 'Table shape'];\n\nfunction CheckScreen({ go, params }) {\n  const { Card, Button, Input, Select, Tag, StatusBadge } = DQchk;\n  const { PageHeader } = window;\n  const suiteName = (params && params.suiteName) || 'suite';\n  const [expType, setExpType] = React.useState(CATALOG[0].type);\n  const [severity, setSeverity] = React.useState('fail');\n  const [dry, setDry] = React.useState(null);\n  const spec = CATALOG.find((e) => e.type === expType);\n  const grouped = CATEGORIES.map((c) => ({ label: c, options: CATALOG.filter((e) => e.category === c).map((e) => ({ value: e.type, label: e.label })) }));\n\n  return (\n    <div style={{ maxWidth: 720, margin: '0 auto' }}>\n      <PageHeader backLabel=\"Suites\" onBack={() => go('suites')}\n        title=\"Add Check\" subtitle={`Define a rule for ${suiteName}`} />\n      <Card padding={24}>\n        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 16 }}>\n          <Input label=\"Check name\" placeholder=\"revenue not null\" />\n\n          <label style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 6 }}>\n            <span style={{ fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--text-primary)' }}>Expectation</span>\n            <span style={{ position: 'relative' }}>\n              <select value={expType} onChange={(e) => setExpType(e.target.value)} style={{\n                width: '100%', height: 40, padding: '0 36px 0 12px', boxSizing: 'border-box', appearance: 'none', WebkitAppearance: 'none',\n                background: 'var(--bg-card)', border: '1px solid var(--border-input)', borderRadius: 'var(--radius)',\n                fontFamily: 'var(--font-mono)', fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--text-primary)', cursor: 'pointer',\n              }}>\n                {grouped.map((g) => (\n                  <optgroup key={g.label} label={g.label}>\n                    {g.options.map((o) => <option key={o.value} value={o.value}>{o.label}</option>)}\n                  </optgroup>\n                ))}\n              </select>\n              <span style={{ position: 'absolute', right: 12, top: '50%', transform: 'translateY(-50%)', pointerEvents: 'none', color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>\n                <svg width=\"14\" height=\"14\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" fill=\"none\" stroke=\"currentColor\" strokeWidth=\"2.2\" strokeLinecap=\"round\"><polyline points=\"6 9 12 15 18 9\"/></svg>\n              </span>\n            </span>\n            <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>{spec.desc}</span>\n          </label>\n\n          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8 }}>\n            <Tag mono tone=\"outline\">{spec.type}</Tag>\n            <Tag tone=\"neutral\">expectation</Tag>\n          </div>\n\n          <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: 16 }}>\n            {spec.fields.map((f, i) => (\n              <Input key={f.name} label={f.label + (f.optional ? ' (optional)' : '')} mono={f.name === 'column'}\n                placeholder={f.type === 'number' ? '0' : f.type === 'list' ? 'USD, EUR, GBP' : f.name}\n                hint={f.help} type={f.type === 'number' ? 'number' : 'text'}\n                style={{ gridColumn: (f.type === 'list' || spec.fields.length === 1) ? '1 / -1' : 'auto' }} />\n            ))}\n          </div>\n\n          <Select label=\"Severity tier\" value={severity} onChange={(e) => setSeverity(e.target.value)}\n            hint=\"On breach, the result is banded to this tier (ADR 0005).\"\n            options={[{ value: 'warn', label: 'warn — weight 0.5' }, { value: 'fail', label: 'fail — weight 1.0' }, { value: 'critical', label: 'critical — weight 2.0' }]} />\n\n          {dry && (\n            <div style={{ padding: '12px 14px', background: 'var(--bg-subtle)', border: '1px solid var(--border)', borderRadius: 'var(--radius)' }}>\n              <div style={{ fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 600, letterSpacing: '.04em', textTransform: 'uppercase', color: 'var(--text-muted)', marginBottom: 8 }}>Dry-run preview</div>\n              {dry === 'running' ? <span style={{ fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>Sampling 10,000 rows…</span> : (\n                <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12 }}>\n                  <StatusBadge status=\"pass\" variant=\"soft\" />\n                  <span style={{ fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--text-body)', fontFamily: 'var(--font-mono)' }}>unexpected: 0 / 9,842 · observed 100%</span>\n                </div>\n              )}\n            </div>\n          )}\n        </div>\n\n        <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'flex-end', gap: 10, marginTop: 24, paddingTop: 20, borderTop: '1px solid var(--divider)' }}>\n          <Button variant=\"secondary\" onClick={() => { setDry('running'); setTimeout(() => setDry('pass'), 700); }}>Dry run</Button>\n          <Button variant=\"primary\" onClick={() => go('suites')}>Add Check</Button>\n        </div>\n      </Card>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nObject.assign(window, { CheckScreen });\n","contentType":"application/javascript","isBase64":false,"truncated":false}

  <h2 style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--text-primary)' }}>Members &amp; access</h2>\n          <Button variant=\"secondary\" size=\"sm\" iconLeft={<I.plus size={14} Composes Card, Input, Select, Switch, Tag.\nconst DQset = window.DataQDesignSystem_317fec;\n\nfunction SettingsTabs({ value, onChange, items }) {\n  return (\n    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 2 }}>\n      {items.map((it) => {\n        const active = it === value;\n        return (\n          <button key={it} onClick={() => onChange(it)} style={{\n            textAlign: 'left', padding: '9px 12px', border: 'none', borderRadius: 'var(--radius)', cursor: 'pointer',\n            fontFamily: 'var(--font-sans)', fontSize: 14, fontWeight: active ? 600 : 500,\n            color: active ? 'var(--indigo-600)' : 'var(--text-body)',\n            background: active ? 'var(--bg-selected)' : 'transparent',\n          }}>{it}</button>\n        );\n      })}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction Row({ label, hint, children }) {\n  return (\n    <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', gap: 24, padding: '16px 0', borderBottom: '1px solid var(--divider)' }}>\n      <div style={{ maxWidth: 320 }}>\n        <div style={{ fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 500, color: 'var(--text-primary)' }}>{label}</div>\n        {hint && <div style={{ fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--text-muted)', marginTop: 2 }}>{hint}</div>}\n      </div>\n      <div style={{ flex: 'none' }}>{children}</div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction GeneralTab() {\n  const { Input, Select } = DQset;\n  return (\n    <div style={{ maxWidth: 460, display: 'flex', value=\"PROD\" onChange={() => {}} />\n      <Select label=\"Azure region\" options={['East US 2', 'West Europe', 'Australia East']} value=\"East US 2\" onChange={() => {}} />\n      <Input label=\"Data Integrity SLA target\" defaultValue=\"99.5\" hint=\"Health score below this raises a workspace alert.\" />\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction SecretsTab() {\n  const { Card, Tag, Button } = DQset;\n  const secrets = [\n    { name: 'snowflake-prod-01-password', type: 'Password', rotated: '12d ago' },\n    { name: 'aws-s3-landing-secret-key', type: 'Secret key', rotated: '3d ago' },\n    { name: 'airflow-hmac-signing-key', type: 'HMAC key', rotated: '28d ago' },\n  ];\n  return (\n    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 16 }}>\n      <Card style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12 }}>\n        <div style={{ width: 38, height: 38, borderRadius: 8, background: 'var(--indigo-50)', color: 'var(--indigo-600)', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}>{React.createElement(window.Icon.shield, { size: 20 })}</div>\n        <div style={{ flex: 1 }}>\n          <div style={{ fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--text-primary)' }}>Azure Key Vault</div>\n          <div style={{ fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>acme-dataq-kv · all secrets encrypted at rest</div>\n        </div>\n        <Tag tone=\"indigo\">Connected</Tag>\n      </Card>\n      <Card padding={0} style={{ overflow: 'hidden' }}>\n        {secrets.map((s, i) => (\n          <div key={s.name} style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12, padding: '14px 18px', borderTop: i ? '1px solid var(--divider)' : 'none' }}>\n            <span style={{ flex: 1, fontFamily: 'var(--font-mono)', fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--text-primary)' }}>{s.name}</span>\n            <Tag tone=\"outline\">{s.type}</Tag>\n            <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--text-muted)', width: 90, textAlign: 'right' }}>Rotated {s.rotated}</span>\n            <Button variant=\"ghost\" [b, setB] = React.useState(true), [c, setC] = React.useState(false);\n  return (\n    <div style={{ maxWidth: 520 }}>\n      <Row label=\"Microsoft Teams\" hint=\"Post failures and critical results to a channel webhook.\"><Switch checked={a} onChange={setA} /></Row>\n      <Row label=\"Email digest\" hint=\"Daily summary of suite health to all members.\"><Switch checked={b} onChange={setB} /></Row>\n      <Row label=\"PagerDuty\" hint=\"Page on-call for critical-severity breaches only.\"><Switch checked={c} onChange={setC} /></Row>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction DangerTab() {\n  const { Button } = DQset;\n  return (\n    <div style={{ maxWidth: 560, border: '1px solid var(--red-border)', borderRadius: 'var(--radius-lg)', background: 'var(--red-bg)', padding: 20 }}>\n      <div trigger.\"><Button variant=\"secondary\" size=\"sm\">Pause</Button></Row>\n      <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', gap: 24, padding: '16px 0 0' }}>\n        <div style={{ maxWidth: 320 }}>\n          <div style={{ fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 500, color: 'var(--text-primary)' }}>Delete workspace</div>\n          <div style={{ fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--text-muted)', marginTop: 2 }}>Permanently removes all suites, runs and results.</div>\n        </div>\n        <Button variant=\"danger\" size=\"sm\">Delete</Button>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction SettingsScreen() {\n  const TABS = ['General', 'Secrets', 'Notifications', 'Danger zone'];\n  const [tab, setTab] = React.useState('General');\n  return (\n    <div style={{ maxWidth: 1000, margin: '0 auto' }}>\n      <h1 style={{ margin: '0 0 20px', fontSize: 24, fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--text-primary)' }}>Workspace Settings</h1>\n      <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '200px 1fr', gap: 28, alignItems: 'start' }}>\n        <SettingsTabs value={tab} onChange={setTab} items={TABS} />\n        <div>\n          {tab === 'General' && <GeneralTab />}\n          {tab === 'Secrets' && <SecretsTab />}\n          {tab === 'Notifications' && <NotificationsTab />}\n          {tab === 'Danger zone' && <DangerTab />}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nObject.assign(window, { SettingsScreen });\n","contentType":"application/javascript","isBase64":false,"truncated":false}
