```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/arijit/Coding/Python/DataQ/backend/app/services/dashboard_service.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Dashboard aggregates — the read model behind the Enhanced Monitoring\nDashboard (Week 6, ADR 0022).\n\nEvery aggregate is **suite-scoped** through the same owned-or-shared filter the\nruns read model uses (`suite_service.accessible_suite_ids`), so the dashboard can\nnever surface a run/result from a suite the caller can't see — the same reason\nreading Postgres directly (a Grafana panel) is rejected as the product surface\n(ADR 0018). Aggregation is done in SQL over the persisted `status` column\n(ADR 0005 health score) — never by reducing JSONB in Python (ADR 0012).\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport uuid\nfrom collections.abc import Mapping\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom datetime import date, datetime, timedelta, timezone\n\nfrom sqlalchemy import func, select\nfrom sqlalchemy.orm import Session\n\nfrom backend.app.db.models import Result, Run, Suite\nfrom backend.app.services import suite_service\n\n# ── health score (ADR 0005) ──────────────────────────────────────────────────\n# Fixed penalty weights; W_MAX (the critical weight) normalises into [0, 100] so\n# all-fail scores 50, not the floor — critical stays meaningfully worse than fail.\n_PENALTY: Mapping[str, float] = {\"pass\": 0.0, \"warn\": 0.5, \"fail\": 1.0, \"critical\": 2.0}\n_W_MAX = 2.0\n# Only the four severity tiers count toward the score / pass-rate. `skip` and\n# `error` did not evaluate a severity, so they are excluded from N rather than\n# treated as a pass (ADR 0005 covers the four tiers only).\n_SEVERITY_STATUSES: tuple[str, ...] = tuple(_PENALTY)\n\n# Health-score bands for the per-suite performance state label.\n_OPTIMAL_MIN = 90.0\n_STABLE_MIN = 60.0\n\n\ndef health_score(counts: Mapping[str, int]) -> float | None:\n    \"\"\"ADR-0005 health score from a status histogram, or ``None`` when no\n    severity results are in scope.\n\n    ``100 × (1 − Σ penalty / (N × 2.0))`` over the four tiers. 100 = all pass,\n    0 = all critical, 50 = all fail, 75 = all warn; ``{fail, fail, pass, pass}``\n    → 75.0. Rounded to 1 dp for display stability.\n    \"\"\"\n    n = sum(counts.get(s, 0) for s in _SEVERITY_STATUSES)\n    if n == 0:\n        return None\n    penalty = sum(_PENALTY[s] * counts.get(s, 0) for s in _SEVERITY_STATUSES)\n    return round(100.0 * (1.0 - penalty / (n * _W_MAX)), 1)\n\n\ndef pass_rate(counts: Mapping[str, int]) -> float | None:\n    \"\"\"Share of evaluated (severity) results that passed, 0-100, or ``None`` when\n    nothing evaluated. Excludes `skip`/`error` from the denominator (as the score).\"\"\"\n    n = sum(counts.get(s, 0) for s in _SEVERITY_STATUSES)\n    if n == 0:\n        return None\n    return round(100.0 * counts.get(\"pass\", 0) / n, 1)\n\n\ndef performance_state(score: float | None) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Coarse state label for a suite's health score (prototype Suite Performance).\"\"\"\n    if score is None:\n        return \"unknown\"\n    if score >= _OPTIMAL_MIN:\n        return \"optimal\"\n    if score >= _STABLE_MIN:\n        return \"stable\"\n    return \"critical\"\n\n\n# ── summary shape ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass Kpis:\n    health_score: float | None\n    pass_rate: float | None\n    total_runs: int\n    active_connections: int\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass TrendPoint:\n    day: date\n    succeeded: int\n    failed: int\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass SuitePerformance:\n    suite_id: uuid.UUID\n    name: str\n    score: float | None\n    state: str\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass DashboardSummary:\n    window_days: int\n    kpis: Kpis\n    trend: list[TrendPoint]\n    suite_performance: list[SuitePerformance]\n\n\ndef _window_start(window_days: int) -> datetime:\n    return datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(days=window_days)\n\n\ndef _status_counts(session: Session, accessible: object, since: datetime) -> dict[str, int]:\n    \"\"\"Histogram of result statuses across accessible suites since ``since``.\"\"\"\n    stmt = (\n        select(Result.status, func.count())\n        .join(Run, Result.run_id == Run.id)\n        .where(Run.suite_id.in_(accessible), Result.created_at >= since)\n        .group_by(Result.status)\n    )\n    return {status: count for status, count in session.execute(stmt)}\n\n\ndef _run_trend(session: Session, accessible: object, since: datetime) -> list[TrendPoint]:\n    \"\"\"Per-day succeeded/failed run counts, zero-filled across the window so the\n    chart has a contiguous x-axis even on quiet days.\"\"\"\n    day = func.date(Run.created_at)\n    stmt = (\n        select(day, Run.status, func.count())\n        .where(Run.suite_id.in_(accessible), Run.created_at >= since)\n        .group_by(day, Run.status)\n    )\n    by_day: dict[date, dict[str, int]] = {}\n    for d, status, count in session.execute(stmt):\n        # func.date returns a date on psycopg2; normalise just in case a driver\n        # hands back a datetime.\n        key = d.date() if isinstance(d, datetime) else d\n        by_day.setdefault(key, {})[status] = count\n\n    start = since.date()\n    today = datetime.now(timezone.utc).date()\n    out: list[TrendPoint] = []\n    cursor = start\n    while cursor <= today:\n        counts = by_day.get(cursor, {})\n        out.append(\n            TrendPoint(\n                day=cursor,\n                succeeded=counts.get(\"succeeded\", 0),\n                failed=counts.get(\"failed\", 0),\n            )\n        )\n        cursor += timedelta(days=1)\n    return out\n\n\ndef _suite_performance(session: Session, accessible: object) -> list[SuitePerformance]:\n    \"\"\"Per-suite health from each suite's **latest** run, worst (lowest) first.\n\n    A suite with no run, or whose latest run wrote no results (a hard-failed run\n    rolls back its results), is omitted — there is no health to show.\n    \"\"\"\n    # DISTINCT ON (suite_id) ordered by created_at desc → the latest run per suite.\n    latest = (\n        select(Run.id, Run.suite_id)\n        .where(Run.suite_id.in_(accessible))\n        .order_by(Run.suite_id, Run.created_at.desc())\n        .distinct(Run.suite_id)\n        .subquery()\n    )\n    stmt = (\n        select(Suite.id, Suite.name, Result.status, func.count())\n        .select_from(latest)\n        .join(Suite, Suite.id == latest.c.suite_id)\n        .join(Result, Result.run_id == latest.c.id)\n        .group_by(Suite.id, Suite.name, Result.status)\n    )\n    counts: dict[uuid.UUID, dict[str, int]] = {}\n    names: dict[uuid.UUID, str] = {}\n    for sid, name, status, count in session.execute(stmt):\n        counts.setdefault(sid, {})[status] = count\n        names[sid] = name\n\n    out = [\n        SuitePerformance(\n            suite_id=sid,\n            name=names[sid],\n            score=health_score(c),\n            state=performance_state(health_score(c)),\n        )\n        for sid, c in counts.items()\n    ]\n    # Worst first (lowest score), suites with no severity result (score None) last.\n    out.sort(key=lambda s: (s.score is None, s.score if s.score is not None else 0.0))\n    return out\n\n\ndef _active_connections(session: Session, accessible: object) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Distinct connections referenced by the caller's accessible suites.\"\"\"\n    stmt = select(func.count(func.distinct(Suite.connection_id))).where(Suite.id.in_(accessible))\n    return session.scalar(stmt) or 0\n\n\ndef _total_runs(session: Session, accessible: object, since: datetime) -> int:\n    stmt = (\n        select(func.count())\n        .select_from(Run)\n        .where(Run.suite_id.in_(accessible), Run.created_at >= since)\n    )\n    return session.scalar(stmt) or 0\n\n\ndef dashboard_summary(\n    session: Session, *, user_id: uuid.UUID, window_days: int\n) -> DashboardSummary:\n    \"\"\"KPIs + run trend + per-suite performance for the caller's accessible suites\n    over the trailing ``window_days``.\"\"\"\n    accessible = suite_service.accessible_suite_ids(user_id)\n    since = _window_start(window_days)\n\n    counts = _status_counts(session, accessible, since)\n    kpis = Kpis(\n        health_score=health_score(counts),\n        pass_rate=pass_rate(counts),\n        total_runs=_total_runs(session, accessible, since),\n        active_connections=_active_connections(session, accessible),\n    )\n    return DashboardSummary(\n        window_days=window_days,\n        kpis=kpis,\n        trend=_run_trend(session, accessible, since),\n        suite_performance=_suite_performance(session, accessible),\n    )\n"
}
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/arijit/Coding/Python/DataQ/backend/app/api/v1/dashboard.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Dashboard read API — the Enhanced Monitoring Dashboard summary (Week 6, ADR 0022).\n\nA single suite-scoped aggregate: KPIs (health score, pass rate, run count, active\nconnections), a per-day run trend, and per-suite performance. All scoping is done\nin the service via the owned-or-shared accessible-suite filter, so this endpoint\nis gated on authentication only (the data it returns is already scoped to the\ncaller). Read-only; no JSONB is reduced in Python (ADR 0005 / 0012).\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport uuid\nfrom datetime import date\nfrom typing import Annotated\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Query\nfrom pydantic import BaseModel\nfrom sqlalchemy.orm import Session\n\nfrom backend.app.core.auth import get_current_user\nfrom backend.app.db.models import User\nfrom backend.app.db.session import get_db\nfrom backend.app.services import dashboard_service as svc\n\nrouter = APIRouter(tags=[\"dashboard\"])\n\n_WINDOW_DEFAULT = 7\n_WINDOW_MAX = 90\n\n\nclass KpisRead(BaseModel):\n    health_score: float | None\n    pass_rate: float | None\n    total_runs: int\n    active_connections: int\n\n\nclass TrendPointRead(BaseModel):\n    day: date\n    succeeded: int\n    failed: int\n\n\nclass SuitePerformanceRead(BaseModel):\n    suite_id: uuid.UUID\n    name: str\n    score: float | None\n    state: str  # optimal | stable | critical | unknown\n\n\nclass DashboardSummaryRead(BaseModel):\n    window_days: int\n    kpis: KpisRead\n    trend: list[TrendPointRead]\n    suite_performance: list[SuitePerformanceRead]\n\n\n@router.get(\n    \"/dashboard/summary\",\n    response_model=DashboardSummaryRead,\n    summary=\"Dashboard summary — KPIs, run trend, per-suite performance\",\n)\ndef get_dashboard_summary(\n    current_user: Annotated[User, Depends(get_current_user)],\n    db: Annotated[Session, Depends(get_db)],\n    window_days: Annotated[int, Query(ge=1, le=_WINDOW_MAX)] = _WINDOW_DEFAULT,\n) -> DashboardSummaryRead:\n    summary = svc.dashboard_summary(db, user_id=current_user.id, window_days=window_days)\n    return DashboardSummaryRead(\n        window_days=summary.window_days,\n        kpis=KpisRead(\n            health_score=summary.kpis.health_score,\n            pass_rate=summary.kpis.pass_rate,\n            total_runs=summary.kpis.total_runs,\n            active_connections=summary.kpis.active_connections,\n        ),\n        trend=[\n            TrendPointRead(day=p.day, succeeded=p.succeeded, failed=p.failed) for p in summary.trend\n        ],\n        suite_performance=[\n            SuitePerformanceRead(suite_id=s.suite_id, name=s.name, score=s.score, state=s.state)\n            for s in summary.suite_performance\n        ],\n    )\n"
}
```

```
1	"""Tests for the result retention sweep (`purge_expired_sample_failures`).
2	
3	DB-backed (real Postgres): the sweep is a bulk UPDATE keyed on `created_at` +
4	the JSONB `sample_failures` column, which can't be faithfully faked. Verifies it
5	scrubs only old, unpurged rows that still carry samples, keeps `metric_value`
6	(trends survive — ADR 0012), is idempotent, and honours the disable sentinel.
7	Skips without TEST_DATABASE_URL.
8	"""
9	
10	import uuid
11	from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta
12	from decimal import Decimal
13	from typing import Any
14	
15	from backend.app.db.models import Check, Connection, Result, Run, Suite, User
16	from backend.app.services import run_service
17	
18	NOW = datetime(2026, 6, 21, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC)
19	_UNSET = object()  # sentinel so an explicit sample=None is stored as SQL NULL
20	
21	
22	def _check_and_run(db_session: Any) -> tuple[Check, Run]:
23	    owner = User(aad_object_id=uuid.uuid4().hex, email=f"u-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}@example.com")
24	    db_session.add(owner)
25	    db_session.flush()
26	    conn = Connection(
27	        name=f"sf-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}",
28	        type="snowflake",
29	        env="dev",
30	        config={"account": "a"},
31	        secret_ref="kv-sf",
32	        created_by=owner.id,
33	    )
34	    db_session.add(conn)
35	    db_session.flush()
36	    suite = Suite(name="s", connection_id=conn.id, created_by=owner.id, target={"table": "T"})
37	    db_session.add(suite)
38	    db_session.flush()
39	    check = Check(suite_id=suite.id, name="c", expectation_type="expect_x", config={})
40	    db_session.add(check)
41	    db_session.flush()
42	    run = Run(suite_id=suite.id, status="succeeded")
43	    db_session.add(run)
44	    db_session.flush()
45	    return check, run
46	
47	
48	def _result(
49	    db_session: Any,
50	    *,
51	    age_days: int,
52	    sample: Any = _UNSET,
53	    purged_at: datetime | None = None,
54	    metric: Decimal | None = None,
55	) -> Result:
56	    check, run = _check_and_run(db_session)
57	    row = Result(
58	        run_id=run.id,
59	        check_id=check.id,
60	        status="fail",
61	        metric_value=metric,
62	        sample_failures={"rows": [{"id": 1}]} if sample is _UNSET else sample,
63	        sample_failures_purged_at=purged_at,
64	        created_at=NOW - timedelta(days=age_days),
65	    )
66	    db_session.add(row)
67	    db_session.commit()
68	    return row
69	
70	
71	def test_scrubs_old_rows_keeps_metric(db_session: Any) -> None:
72	    old = _result(db_session, age_days=40, metric=Decimal("9.5"))
73	
74	    purged = run_service.purge_expired_sample_failures(db_session, retention_days=30, now=NOW)
75
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/arijit/Coding/Python/DataQ/backend/tests/services/test_dashboard_service.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Tests for the dashboard aggregates service.\n\nTwo layers: the pure ADR-0005 health-score math (no DB), and the DB-backed\nsummary aggregation — scoping (owned-or-shared only), the window cutoff, the\nrun trend zero-fill, and per-suite performance from the latest run. Skips\nwithout TEST_DATABASE_URL.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport uuid\nfrom datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta\nfrom typing import Any\n\nimport pytest\n\nfrom backend.app.db.models import Check, Connection, Result, Run, Share, Suite, User\nfrom backend.app.services import dashboard_service as svc\n\n# ── pure health-score / pass-rate / state (ADR 0005) ─────────────────────────\n\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\n    (\"counts\", \"expected\"),\n    [\n        ({\"pass\": 4}, 100.0),\n        ({\"fail\": 4}, 50.0),\n        ({\"warn\": 4}, 75.0),\n        ({\"critical\": 4}, 0.0),\n        ({\"fail\": 2, \"pass\": 2}, 75.0),  # the ADR's worked example\n        ({\"pass\": 1, \"warn\": 1, \"fail\": 1, \"critical\": 1}, 56.2),  # (0+.5+1+2)/(4*2)=.4375\n        ({}, None),\n        ({\"skip\": 3, \"error\": 2}, None),  # non-severity statuses excluded → N=0\n    ],\n)\ndef test_health_score(counts: dict[str, int], expected: float | None) -> None:\n    assert svc.health_score(counts) == expected\n\n\ndef test_health_score_excludes_skip_and_error_from_n() -> None:\n    # skip/error don't dilute the score — {pass, fail} alone is 75, with skip/error too.\n    assert svc.health_score({\"pass\": 1, \"fail\": 1}) == 75.0\n    assert svc.health_score({\"pass\": 1, \"fail\": 1, \"skip\": 10, \"error\": 5}) == 75.0\n\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\n    (\"counts\", \"expected\"),\n    [\n        ({\"pass\": 4}, 100.0),\n        ({\"pass\": 1, \"fail\": 3}, 25.0),\n        ({}, None),\n        ({\"skip\": 5}, None),\n    ],\n)\ndef test_pass_rate(counts: dict[str, int], expected: float | None) -> None:\n    assert svc.pass_rate(counts) == expected\n\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\n    (\"score\", \"state\"),\n    [(100.0, \"optimal\"), (90.0, \"optimal\"), (89.9, \"stable\"), (60.0, \"stable\"), (59.9, \"critical\"), (0.0, \"critical\"), (None, \"unknown\")],\n)\ndef test_performance_state(score: float | None, state: str) -> None:\n    assert svc.performance_state(score) == state\n\n\n# ── DB-backed summary ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n\ndef _user(db: Any) -> User:\n    u = User(aad_object_id=uuid.uuid4().hex, email=f\"u-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}@example.com\")\n    db.add(u)\n    db.flush()\n    return u\n\n\ndef _suite(db: Any, owner: User, *, name: str = \"s\") -> Suite:\n    conn = Connection(\n        name=f\"c-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}\",\n        type=\"snowflake\",\n        env=\"dev\",\n        config={\"account\": \"a\"},\n        secret_ref=\"kv\",\n        created_by=owner.id,\n    )\n    db.add(conn)\n    db.flush()\n    suite = Suite(name=name, connection_id=conn.id, created_by=owner.id, target={\"table\": \"T\"})\n    db.add(suite)\n    db.flush()\n    return suite\n\n\ndef _run_with_results(\n    db: Any,\n    suite: Suite,\n    *,\n    run_status: str,\n    result_statuses: list[str],\n    age_days: float = 0.0,\n) -> Run:\n    when = datetime.now(UTC) - timedelta(days=age_days)\n    run = Run(suite_id=suite.id, status=run_status, created_at=when)\n    db.add(run)\n    db.flush()\n    for s in result_statuses:\n        check = Check(suite_id=suite.id, name=f\"chk-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}\", expectation_type=\"e\", config={})\n        db.add(check)\n        db.flush()\n        db.add(Result(run_id=run.id, check_id=check.id, status=s, created_at=when))\n    db.commit()\n    return run\n\n\ndef test_summary_scopes_to_accessible_suites(db_session: Any) -> None:\n    alice, bob = _user(db_session), _user(db_session)\n    mine = _suite(db_session, alice, name=\"mine\")\n    theirs = _suite(db_session, bob, name=\"theirs\")\n    _run_with_results(db_session, mine, run_status=\"succeeded\", result_statuses=[\"pass\", \"pass\"])\n    _run_with_results(db_session, theirs, run_status=\"failed\", result_statuses=[\"critical\", \"fail\"])\n\n    summary = svc.dashboard_summary(db_session, user_id=alice.id, window_days=7)\n\n    # Bob's failing suite must not bleed into Alice's all-pass numbers.\n    assert summary.kpis.health_score == 100.0\n    assert summary.kpis.pass_rate == 100.0\n    assert summary.kpis.active_connections == 1\n    assert [s.name for s in summary.suite_performance] == [\"mine\"]\n\n\ndef test_summary_includes_shared_suite(db_session: Any) -> None:\n    owner, viewer = _user(db_session), _user(db_session)\n    suite = _suite(db_session, owner, name=\"shared\")\n    db_session.add(Share(suite_id=suite.id, user_id=viewer.id, permission=\"view\"))\n    db_session.commit()\n    _run_with_results(db_session, suite, run_status=\"failed\", result_statuses=[\"fail\", \"fail\"])\n\n    summary = svc.dashboard_summary(db_session, user_id=viewer.id, window_days=7)\n    assert summary.kpis.health_score == 50.0\n    assert [s.name for s in summary.suite_performance] == [\"shared\"]\n\n\ndef test_window_excludes_old_results(db_session: Any) -> None:\n    alice = _user(db_session)\n    suite = _suite(db_session, alice)\n    _run_with_results(db_session, suite, run_status=\"succeeded\", result_statuses=[\"pass\"], age_days=1)\n    _run_with_results(db_session, suite, run_status=\"failed\", result_statuses=[\"critical\"], age_days=40)\n\n    summary = svc.dashboard_summary(db_session, user_id=alice.id, window_days=7)\n    # The 40-day-old critical is outside the 7d window → score reflects only the pass.\n    assert summary.kpis.health_score == 100.0\n    assert summary.kpis.total_runs == 1\n\n\ndef test_run_trend_is_contiguous_and_zero_filled(db_session: Any) -> None:\n    alice = _user(db_session)\n    suite = _suite(db_session, alice)\n    _run_with_results(db_session, suite, run_status=\"succeeded\", result_statuses=[\"pass\"], age_days=0)\n    _run_with_results(db_session, suite, run_status=\"failed\", result_statuses=[\"fail\"], age_days=2)\n\n    summary = svc.dashboard_summary(db_session, user_id=alice.id, window_days=7)\n    trend = summary.trend\n\n    # Contiguous daily axis, no gaps.\n    days = [p.day for p in trend]\n    assert days == sorted(days)\n    for earlier, later in zip(days, days[1:]):\n        assert (later - earlier).days == 1\n    # Totals across the window match the runs created.\n    assert sum(p.succeeded for p in trend) == 1\n    assert sum(p.failed for p in trend) == 1\n\n\ndef test_suite_performance_uses_latest_run_worst_first(db_session: Any) -> None:\n    alice = _user(db_session)\n    healthy = _suite(db_session, alice, name=\"healthy\")\n    broken = _suite(db_session, alice, name=\"broken\")\n    # An older bad run on `healthy` that must be ignored in favour of the latest.\n    _run_with_results(db_session, healthy, run_status=\"failed\", result_statuses=[\"critical\"], age_days=2)\n    _run_with_results(db_session, healthy, run_status=\"succeeded\", result_statuses=[\"pass\", \"pass\"], age_days=0)\n    _run_with_results(db_session, broken, run_status=\"failed\", result_statuses=[\"fail\", \"critical\"], age_days=0)\n\n    summary = svc.dashboard_summary(db_session, user_id=alice.id, window_days=7)\n    perf = {s.name: s for s in summary.suite_performance}\n\n    assert perf[\"healthy\"].score == 100.0  # latest run, not the old critical\n    assert perf[\"healthy\"].state == \"optimal\"\n    assert perf[\"broken\"].score == 25.0  # (1.0 + 2.0) / (2 * 2) = .75 → 25\n    # Worst (lowest) first.\n    assert [s.name for s in summary.suite_performance] == [\"broken\", \"healthy\"]\n"
}
```

```
=== a runs/admin api test head for the client+auth pattern ===
"""Run trigger + read API tests against a real Postgres (db_session).

get_db is overridden to the shared rolled-back session; auth runs in dev-bypass
(conftest), which upserts the dev user used as `created_by` for API-created
suites. `run_dispatch.dispatch_run` is stubbed by the autouse conftest fixture
(`stub_run_dispatch`), so triggering never touches a broker; a test that needs
the broker-failure path re-patches it. Skips without TEST_DATABASE_URL.
"""

import uuid
from collections.abc import Iterator
from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Any

import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import select

from backend.app.core.auth import get_current_user
from backend.app.db.models import (
    Connection,
    PipelineRun,
    Result,
    Run,
    Share,
    Suite,
    User,
)
from backend.app.db.session import get_db
from backend.app.main import app
from backend.app.services import run_dispatch


@pytest.fixture
def client(db_session: Any) -> Iterator[TestClient]:
    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = lambda: db_session
    try:
        yield TestClient(app)
    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()


def _as(user: User) -> None:
    app.dependency_overrides[get_current_user] = lambda: user


def _user(db_session: Any, email: str) -> User:
    u = User(aad_object_id=uuid.uuid4().hex, email=email)
    db_session.add(u)
    db_session.flush()
    return u


def _connection(db_session: Any, owner: User, *, type_: str = "snowflake") -> Connection:
    conn = Connection(
        name=f"c-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}",
        type=type_,
        env="dev",
        config={"account": "ab12345.eu-west-1"},
```

```
41	    """ADR-0005 health score from a status histogram, or ``None`` when no

43	
44	    ``100 × (1 − Σ penalty / (N × 2.0))`` over the four tiers. 100 = all pass,
45	    0 = all critical, 50 = all fail, 75 = all warn; ``{fail, fail, pass, pass}``
```

> AGENT

`.tuples()` returns a result that `dict()` mishandles at runtime. The cleanest form that satisfies ruff (no C416), Pyright, and runtime is an explicit loop:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01SsRdd6ouCt1cHJPgYHbtPk
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/arijit/Coding/Python/DataQ/backend/app/services/dashboard_service.py",
  "old_string": "    return dict(session.execute(stmt).tuples())",
  "new_string": "    counts: dict[str, int] = {}\n    for status, count in session.execute(stmt):\n        counts[status] = count\n    return counts"
}
```

> TOOL

```
---ROUTES---
import { DownOutlined, LogoutOutlined } from '@ant-design/icons';
import { Avatar, Dropdown, Flex, Layout, Menu, Spin, Tag, Typography } from 'antd';
import type { MenuProps } from 'antd';
import { lazy, Suspense } from 'react';
import { Link, Navigate, Route, Routes, useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';

import { AuthGate } from './auth/AuthGate';
import { authMode } from './auth/config';
import { useCurrentUser } from './auth/useCurrentUser';
import { useIsWorkspaceAdmin } from './auth/useMe';
import { getMsalInstance } from './auth/msalInstance';
import { BRAND, SHELL } from './theme';

// Route components are code-split so the initial bundle doesn't ship every page
// (and antd-heavy pages only load on navigation). Named exports → map to default.
const Connections = lazy(() =>
  import('./pages/Connections').then((m) => ({ default: m.Connections })),
);
const ConnectionNew = lazy(() =>
  import('./pages/ConnectionNew').then((m) => ({ default: m.ConnectionNew })),
);
const Suites = lazy(() => import('./pages/Suites').then((m) => ({ default: m.Suites })));
const CheckNew = lazy(() => import('./pages/CheckNew').then((m) => ({ default: m.CheckNew })));
const Results = lazy(() => import('./pages/Results').then((m) => ({ default: m.Results })));
const Home = lazy(() => import('./pages/Home').then((m) => ({ default: m.Home })));
const Admin = lazy(() => import('./pages/Admin').then((m) => ({ default: m.Admin })));

const { Header, Sider, Content } = Layout;

const NAV_ITEMS = [
  { key: '/connections', label: <Link to="/connections">Connections</Link> },
  { key: '/suites', label: <Link to="/suites">Suites</Link> },
  { key: '/results', label: <Link to="/results">Results</Link> },
  { key: '/profile', label: <Link to="/profile">Profile</Link> },
];
// Shown only to workspace admins (server-driven via /me). The route is always
// registered — a non-admin who deep-links to /admin hits the page's Forbidden
// state — so this gate is for nav convenience, not the security boundary.
const ADMIN_NAV_ITEM = { key: '/admin', label: <Link to="/admin">Admin</Link> };

export function App() {
  const location = useLocation();
  const isAdmin = useIsWorkspaceAdmin();
  const navItems = isAdmin ? [...NAV_ITEMS, ADMIN_NAV_ITEM] : NAV_ITEMS;
  // Highlight the nav item whose path matches the current location — exact, or a
  // sub-path at a segment boundary (so `/suites` matches `/suites/123` but not a
  // sibling like `/suites-archive`). Plain startsWith would mis-highlight those.
  const selectedKeys = navItems
    .map((i) => i.key)
    .filter((k) => location.pathname === k || location.pathname.startsWith(`${k}/`));

  return (
    <Layout style={{ minHeight: '100vh' }}>
      <Header
        style={{
          display: 'flex',
          alignItems: 'center',
          gap: 16,
          borderBottom: `1px solid ${BRAND.border}`,
        }}
      >
        <Link to="/" aria-label="DataQ home" style={{ flex: 1 }}>
          <Flex align="center" gap={10}>
            <BrandMark />
            <Typography.Text strong style={{ fontSize: 17, color: BRAND.ink }}>
              DataQ
            </Typography.Text>
          </Flex>
        </Link>
        <UserMenu />
      </Header>
      <Layout>
        <Sider
          width={SHELL.siderWidth}
          theme="light"
          breakpoint="lg"
          collapsedWidth={0}
          style={{ borderInlineEnd: `1px solid ${BRAND.border}` }}
        >
          <Menu
```

```
/**
 * A yin-yang app glyph (two-tone indigo) so the header reads as a product, not a
 * bare title — the balance motif nods at DataQ's pass/fail, expected/observed
 * duality. Self-contained colours (dark + light indigo) keep it legible on the
 * white header.
 */
function BrandMark({ size = 30 }: { size?: number }) {
  const dark = BRAND.primary;
  const light = BRAND.primarySoft; // indigo-200
  return (
    <svg width={size} height={size} viewBox="0 0 100 100" role="img" aria-label="DataQ logo">
      <circle cx="50" cy="50" r="49" fill={light} stroke={BRAND.border} strokeWidth="1" />
      {/* The dark half: right lobe + the two interlocking teardrops. */}
      <path
        d="M50 1 a49 49 0 0 1 0 98 a24.5 24.5 0 0 1 0 -49 a24.5 24.5 0 0 0 0 -49 Z"
        fill={dark}
      />
      <circle cx="50" cy="25.5" r="9" fill={light} />
      <circle cx="50" cy="74.5" r="9" fill={dark} />
    </svg>
  );
}

/** Up-to-two-letter initials for the avatar (e.g. "Dev Bypass User" → "DB"). */
function initialsOf(name: string): string {
  const parts = name.trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
  if (parts.length === 0) return '?';
  return (parts[0][0] + (parts.length > 1 ? parts[parts.length - 1][0] : '')).toUpperCase();
}

/**
 * A very subtle brand watermark behind every page: the yin-yang mark bled off
 * the content area's bottom-right corner at low opacity. Decorative only
 * (`aria-hidden`, no pointer events). The mark is clipped by *this* layer
 * (`inset:0; overflow:hidden`), not by the Content itself — so real page
 * content can still overflow/scroll normally; only the watermark is clipped.
 */
function BrandWatermark() {
  return (
    <div
      aria-hidden
      style={{
        position: 'absolute',
        inset: 0,
        overflow: 'hidden',
        pointerEvents: 'none',
      }}
    >
      <div style={{ position: 'absolute', right: -70, bottom: -70, opacity: 0.05, lineHeight: 0 }}>
        <BrandMark size={460} />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

 * with the signed-in identity and a Sign out action. Under dev-bypass there is
```

```
1	import { api } from './client';
2	
3	/**
4	 * Runs / results / pipeline-runs API — the read surface behind the Results page
5	 * (backend `runs.py`, PR-C0b). The DQ-run reads are suite-scoped: the backend
6	 * filters to suites the caller can access, so this client never has to. Manual
7	 * run *triggering* (`runSuite` → `POST /suites/{id}/run`) lives here too, since
8	 * it produces a `Run`.
9	 */
10	
11	/** Run execution lifecycle — `status` is execution, not data quality. */
12	export const RUN_STATUSES = ['queued', 'running', 'succeeded', 'failed', 'cancelled'] as const;
13	export type RunStatus = (typeof RUN_STATUSES)[number];
14	
15	/** Result severity tier (ADR 0005) + the two operational statuses (#122). */
16	export type ResultStatus = 'pass' | 'warn' | 'fail' | 'critical' | 'skip' | 'error';
17	
18	/** Mirrors the backend `RunRead`. */
19	export interface Run {
20	  id: string;
21	  suite_id: string;
22	  status: RunStatus;
23	  triggered_by: string | null;
24	  started_at: string | null;
25	  finished_at: string | null;
26	  created_at: string;
27	}
28	
29	/** Mirrors `ResultRead` — `sample_failures` is withheld by the API (PII, ADR 0018). */
30	export interface Result {
31	  id: string;
32	  check_id: string;
33	  status: ResultStatus;
34	  metric_value: number | null;
35	  duration_ms: number | null;
36	  observed_value: Record<string, unknown> | null;
37	  expected_value: Record<string, unknown> | null;
38	}
39	
40	/** Mirrors `RunDetailRead` — a run plus its result rows. */
41	export interface RunDetail extends Run {
42	  results: Result[];
43	}
44	
45	/** Mirrors `CheckProgressRead` — `status` is null while the check is pending. */
46	export interface CheckProgress {
47	  check_id: string;
48	  name: string;
49	  status: ResultStatus | null;
50	}
51	
52	/**
53	 * Mirrors `RunProgressRead` — the compact live-progress shape the run-progress
54	 * UI polls: run lifecycle + per-check resolution + a status histogram. Lighter
55	 * than the full run+results detail (`getRun`).
56	 */
57	export interface RunProgress {
58	  run_id: string;
59	  suite_id: string;
60	  status: RunStatus;
61	  total_checks: number;
62	  completed_checks: number;
63	  counts: Record<string, number>;
64	  checks: CheckProgress[];
65	  started_at: string | null;
66	  finished_at: string | null;
67	}
68	
69	/** Mirrors `PipelineRunRead` — a monitored orchestrator run (`pipeline_runs` ≠ `runs`). */
70	export interface PipelineRun {
71	  id: string;
72	  provider: 'adf' | 'airflow';
73	  connection_id: string;
74	  provider_run_id: string;
75	  pipeline_or_dag_id: string;
76	  env: string;
77	  status: string;
78	  started_at: string | null;
79	  finished_at: string | null;
80	  failure_reason: string | null;
81	  created_at: string;
82	}
83	
84	export async function listRuns(params?: {
85	  suite_id?: string;
86	  status?: RunStatus;
87	  limit?: number;
88	}): Promise<Run[]> {
89	  const { data } = await api.get<Run[]>('/runs', { params });
90	  return data;
91	}
92	
93	export async function getRun(runId: string): Promise<RunDetail> {
94	  const { data } = await api.get<RunDetail>(`/runs/${runId}`);
95	  return data;
96	}
97	
98	/**
99	 * Trigger a run of a suite (`POST /suites/{id}/run`). Edit-gated; returns the
100	 * queued `Run` (HTTP 202). The backend resolves the suite's target up front, so
101	 * a targetless/misconfigured suite fails with 422, and a broker outage with 503.
102	 */
103	export async function runSuite(suiteId: string): Promise<Run> {
104	  const { data } = await api.post<Run>(`/suites/${suiteId}/run`);
105	  return data;
106	}
107	
108	/**
109	 * Poll a run's live progress (`GET /runs/{id}/progress`). Suite-scoped (view).
110	 * Cheaper than `getRun` — no observed/expected payloads — so it's the call the
111	 * live-progress UI hits on its polling interval.
112	 */
113	export async function getRunProgress(runId: string): Promise<RunProgress> {
114	  const { data } = await api.get<RunProgress>(`/runs/${runId}/progress`);
115	  return data;
116	}
117	
118	/**
119	 * Cancel a non-terminal run (`POST /runs/{id}/cancel`). Edit-gated; returns the
120	 * updated `Run`. An already-finished run → 409. Cancel is cooperative (best-effort
121	 * for an in-flight run), so it may race a fast run to completion.
122	 */
123	export async function cancelRun(runId: string): Promise<Run> {
124	  const { data } = await api.post<Run>(`/runs/${runId}/cancel`);
125	  return data;
126	}
127	
128	export async function listPipelineRuns(params?: {
129	  provider?: 'adf' | 'airflow';
130	  status?: string;
131	  limit?: number;
132	}): Promise<PipelineRun[]> {
133	  const { data } = await api.get<PipelineRun[]>('/pipeline_runs', { params });
134	  return data;
135	}
136
```

```
1	import { Empty } from 'antd';
2	import type { ReactElement } from 'react';
3	import { ResponsiveContainer } from 'recharts';
4	
5	/**
6	 * Thin wrapper every dashboard chart mounts through, so they share one height /
7	 * width contract and one empty state. recharts is only ever imported from here
8	 * (and the chart widgets that use it), and the chart widgets live on lazy routes
9	 * (`/dashboard`, run/check detail) — so recharts stays out of the initial bundle
10	 * and only ships when a chart route loads (ADR 0022).
11	 *
12	 * `ResponsiveContainer` needs exactly one child element — pass a single recharts
13	 * chart (`<BarChart>`, `<LineChart>`, …).
14	 */
15	interface ResponsiveChartProps {
16	  /** A single recharts chart element. */
17	  children: ReactElement;
18	  height?: number;
19	  /** Render the empty state instead of the chart (no data for the range). */
20	  isEmpty?: boolean;
21	  emptyText?: string;
22	}
23	
24	export function ResponsiveChart({
25	  children,
26	  height = 240,
27	  isEmpty = false,
28	  emptyText = 'No data for this range yet',
29	}: ResponsiveChartProps) {
30	  if (isEmpty) {
31	    return (
32	      <Empty
33	        image={Empty.PRESENTED_IMAGE_SIMPLE}
34	        description={emptyText}
35	        style={{
36	          height,
37	          margin: 0,
38	          display: 'flex',
39	          flexDirection: 'column',
40	          alignItems: 'center',
41	          justifyContent: 'center',
42	        }}
43	      />
44	    );
45	  }
46	  return (
47	    <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={height}>
48	      {children}
49	    </ResponsiveContainer>
50	  );
51	}
52
```

```
43	export function Results() {
44	  const [runNowOpen, setRunNowOpen] = useState(false);
45	  return (
46	    <Flex vertical gap={24}>
47	      <Flex justify="space-between" align="center" gap={12}>
48	        <Typography.Title level={3} style={{ margin: 0 }}>
49	          Results
50	        </Typography.Title>

52	          Run now
53	        </Button>
54	      </Flex>
55	      <RunNowPanel open={runNowOpen} onClose={() => setRunNowOpen(false)} />
56	      <Tabs
57	        defaultActiveKey="runs"
58	        items={[
59	          { key: 'runs', label: 'Runs', children: <RunsTab /> },

67	// ───────────────────────────── Runs tab ─────────────────────────────
68	
69	function RunsTab() {
70	  // Fetch a page of runs + the accessible suites (for id→name), then filter by
71	  // status client-side — cheap at this volume and avoids a refetch per filter.
72	  const { state } = useAsyncData(() => listRuns({ limit: LIST_LIMIT }));
73	  const { state: suitesState } = useAsyncData(listSuites);
74	  const [status, setStatus] = useState<RunStatus | 'all'>('all');
75	  const [openRun, setOpenRun] = useState<Run | null>(null);
76	
77	  const suiteNames = useMemo(() => {
78	    const map = new Map<string, string>();
79	    if (suitesState.status === 'ok') {
80	      for (const s of suitesState.data) map.set(s.id, s.name);
81	    }
82	    return map;
83	  }, [suitesState]);
84	
85	  if (state.status === 'loading') return <Spin tip="Loading runs…" size="large" />;
86	  if (state.status === 'error') {
87	    return <Alert type="error" showIcon message="Failed to load runs" description={state.error} />;
88	  }
89	
90	  const runs = status === 'all' ? state.data : state.data.filter((r) => r.status === status);
91	
92	  const columns: ColumnsType<Run> = [
93	    {
94	      title: 'Suite',
95	      dataIndex: 'suite_id',
96	      render: (suiteId: string) =>
97	        suiteNames.get(suiteId) ?? <Typography.Text code>{suiteId.slice(0, 8)}</Typography.Text>,
98	    },
99	    {
100	      title: 'Status',
101	      dataIndex: 'status',
102	      width: 120,
103	      render: (s: RunStatus) => <Tag color={RUN_STATUS_COLORS[s]}>{s}</Tag>,
104	    },
105	    { title: 'Triggered by', dataIndex: 'triggered_by', render: (t: string | null) => t ?? '—' },
106	    {
107	      title: 'Started',
108	      dataIndex: 'started_at',
109	      render: (t: string | null) => formatTimestamp(t),
110	    },
111	    {
112	      title: 'Duration',
113	      width: 110,
114	      render: (_: unknown, run: Run) => formatDuration(run.started_at, run.finished_at),
115	    },
116	  ];
117	
118	  return (
119	    <Flex vertical gap={16}>
120	      <Flex gap={12} align="center">
121	        <Typography.Text type="secondary">Status</Typography.Text>
122	        <Select<RunStatus | 'all'>
123	          value={status}
124	          onChange={setStatus}
125	          style={{ width: 160 }}
126	          options={[
127	            { value: 'all', label: 'All' },
128	            ...RUN_STATUSES.map((s) => ({ value: s, label: s })),
129	          ]}
130	        />
131	      </Flex>
132	      <Table<Run>
133	        rowKey="id"
134	        columns={columns}
135	        dataSource={runs}
136	        pagination={false}
137	        locale={{ emptyText: <Empty description="No runs yet." /> }}
138	        onRow={(run) => ({ onClick: () => setOpenRun(run), style: { cursor: 'pointer' } })}
139	      />

142	        suiteName={openRun ? (suiteNames.get(openRun.suite_id) ?? null) : null}
143	        onClose={() => setOpenRun(null)}
144	      />
145	    </Flex>
146	  );
147	}
148	
149	// ─────────────────────────── Run detail drawer ──────────────────────
150	
151	function RunDetailDrawer({
152	  run,
153	  suiteName,
154	  onClose,
155	}: {
156	  run: Run | null;
157	  suiteName: string | null;
158	  onClose: () => void;
159	}) {
160	  return (
161	    <Drawer
162	      open={run !== null}
163	      onClose={onClose}
164	      width={640}
165	      title={run ? `Run · ${suiteName ?? run.suite_id.slice(0, 8)}` : 'Run'}
166	      destroyOnClose
167	    >
168	      {/* Keyed by run id so switching runs (without closing) remounts and
169	          refetches — useAsyncData only fetches on mount/reload, not on prop
170	          change, so without the key the drawer would show the prior run. */}
171	      {run && <RunDetailBody key={run.id} run={run} />}
172	    </Drawer>
173	  );
174	}
175	
176	function RunDetailBody({ run }: { run: Run }) {
177	  // Remounted per run via the `key` at the call site → these fetch fresh.
178	  const { state } = useAsyncData(() => getRun(run.id));
179	  const { state: checksState } = useAsyncData(() => listChecks(run.suite_id));
180	
181	  const checks = useMemo(() => {
182	    const map = new Map<string, Check>();
183	    if (checksState.status === 'ok') {
184	      for (const c of checksState.data) map.set(c.id, c);
185	    }
186	    return map;
187	  }, [checksState]);
188	
189	  return (
190	    <Flex vertical gap={16}>
191	      <Flex gap={16} wrap>
192	        <Meta label="Status">
193	          <Tag color={RUN_STATUS_COLORS[run.status]}>{run.status}</Tag>
194	        </Meta>
195	        <Meta label="Triggered by">{run.triggered_by ?? '—'}</Meta>
196	        <Meta label="Started">{formatTimestamp(run.started_at)}</Meta>
197	        <Meta label="Duration">{formatDuration(run.started_at, run.finished_at)}</Meta>
198	      </Flex>
199	
200	      {state.status === 'loading' && <Spin tip="Loading results…" />}
201	      {state.status === 'error' && (
202	        <Alert type="error" showIcon message="Failed to load results" description={state.error} />
203	      )}
204	      {state.status === 'ok' && <ResultsTable results={state.data.results} checks={checks} />}
205	    </Flex>
206	  );
207	}
208	
209	function ResultsTable({ results, checks }: { results: Result[]; checks: Map<string, Check> }) {
210	  if (results.length === 0) {
211	    return <Empty description="No check results — the run did not complete." />;
212	  }
213	  const columns: ColumnsType<(typeof results)[number]> = [
214	    {

219	    },

226	        </Typography.Text>

231	      dataIndex: 'status',

234	    },
235	    {
236	      title: 'Metric',
237	      dataIndex: 'metric_value',
238	      width: 90,
239	      render: (v: number | null) => (v === null ? '—' : v),
240	    },
241	    {
242	      title: 'Observed',
243	      dataIndex: 'observed_value',
244	      render: (v: Record<string, unknown> | null) => <ScalarValue value={v} />,
245	    },
246	  ];
247	  return (
248	    <Table rowKey="id" size="small" columns={columns} dataSource={results} pagination={false} />
249	  );
250	}
251

273	    { title: 'Provider', dataIndex: 'provider', width: 110, render: (p: string) => <Tag>{p}</Tag> },
274	    { title: 'Pipeline / DAG', dataIndex: 'pipeline_or_dag_id' },
```

```
1	import type { ResultStatus, RunStatus } from '../../api/runs';
2	import { BRAND } from '../../theme';
3	
4	/**
5	 * Chart colour tokens + shared axis/grid styling for the recharts-based
6	 * dashboard widgets (ADR 0022). Kept framework-free and separate from
7	 * `resultsFormat.ts` for one reason: that module maps statuses to **antd Tag
8	 * names** (`'success'`, `'magenta'`, …) which only antd understands, whereas
9	 * SVG charts need concrete hex. These maps are the chart-domain mirror of the
10	 * same severity/run-status semantics (readme status model · ADR 0005/0016) —
11	 * the hexes track antd v6's default palette so a chart series reads the same
12	 * colour as the matching Tag.
13	 *
14	 * `Record<…>` (not a lookup with a fallback) so adding a `ResultStatus` /
15	 * `RunStatus` value is a compile error here rather than a silently grey series.
16	 */
17	
18	/** Severity → series hex (pass green · warn gold · fail red · critical magenta). */
19	export const RESULT_STATUS_CHART_COLORS: Record<ResultStatus, string> = {
20	  pass: '#52c41a', // green-6
21	  warn: '#faad14', // gold-6
22	  fail: '#ff4d4f', // red-6
23	  critical: '#eb2f96', // magenta-6
24	  skip: '#bfbfbf', // gray-5
25	  error: '#fa541c', // volcano-6
26	};
27	
28	/** Run execution status → series hex (succeeded green · failed red · running indigo · …). */
29	export const RUN_STATUS_CHART_COLORS: Record<RunStatus, string> = {
30	  queued: '#bfbfbf', // gray-5
31	  running: BRAND.primary, // indigo — matches the brand "in-flight" accent
32	  succeeded: '#52c41a', // green-6

38	 * Non-status chart tokens — the indigo primary for neutral/aggregate series
39	 * (e.g. total runs), plus the hairline grid + muted axis tints so every chart
40	 * frames the same way the cards/tables do.
41	 */
42	export const CHART_COLORS = {
43	  primary: BRAND.primary,
44	  grid: BRAND.border,
45	  axis: '#8c8c8c',
46	} as const;

50	export const GRID_PROPS = { stroke: CHART_COLORS.grid, strokeDasharray: '3 3' } as const;
51	export const TOOLTIP_STYLE = {
52	  borderRadius: 8,
53	  border: `1px solid ${BRAND.border}`,
54	  fontSize: 13,
55	} as const;

57	/** Series colour for a result severity. */
58	export function severityColor(status: ResultStatus): string {
59	  return RESULT_STATUS_CHART_COLORS[status];
60	}
61	
62	/** Series colour for a run execution status. */
63	export function runStatusColor(status: RunStatus): string {
64	  return RUN_STATUS_CHART_COLORS[status];
65	}
```

```
1	import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
2	
3	/** Three-state result of an async fetch. */
4	export type AsyncState<T> =
5	  | { status: 'loading' }
6	  | { status: 'ok'; data: T }
7	  | { status: 'error'; error: string };
8	
9	/**
10	 * Fetch on mount (and on `reload()`), with a cancelled-guard so a late
11	 * resolution after unmount doesn't set state, and rejection normalised to a
12	 * string `error`. Shared by the data pages (Home, Connections, …) so the
13	 * cancelled-effect dance lives in one place rather than being re-derived per page.
14	 *
15	 * `reload` re-runs the fetcher (e.g. after a mutation) while keeping the current
16	 * data visible until the refetch resolves — no flash back to the loading state.
17	 */
18	export function useAsyncData<T>(fetcher: () => Promise<T>): {
19	  state: AsyncState<T>;
20	  reload: () => void;
21	} {
22	  const [state, setState] = useState<AsyncState<T>>({ status: 'loading' });
23	  const [nonce, setNonce] = useState(0);
24	
25	  useEffect(() => {
26	    let cancelled = false;
27	    fetcher()
28	      .then((data) => {
29	        if (!cancelled) setState({ status: 'ok', data });
30	      })
31	      .catch((err: unknown) => {
32	        if (!cancelled) {
33	          setState({ status: 'error', error: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err) });
34	        }
35	      });
36	    return () => {
37	      cancelled = true;
38	    };
39	    // Re-run on mount and whenever `reload` bumps the nonce; the fetcher identity
40	    // is intentionally not a dependency.
41	    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
42	  }, [nonce]);
43	
44	  const reload = useCallback(() => setNonce((n) => n + 1), []);
45	  return { state, reload };
46	}
47
```

```
1	"""Dashboard read API — the Enhanced Monitoring Dashboard summary (Week 6, ADR 0022).
2	
3	A single suite-scoped aggregate: KPIs (health score, pass rate, run count, active
4	connections), a per-day run trend, and per-suite performance. All scoping is done
5	in the service via the owned-or-shared accessible-suite filter, so this endpoint
6	is gated on authentication only (the data it returns is already scoped to the
7	caller). Read-only; no JSONB is reduced in Python (ADR 0005 / 0012).
8	"""
9	
10	from __future__ import annotations
11	
12	import uuid
13	from datetime import date
14	from typing import Annotated
15	
16	from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Query
17	from pydantic import BaseModel
18	from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
19	
20	from backend.app.core.auth import get_current_user
21	from backend.app.db.models import User
22	from backend.app.db.session import get_db
23	from backend.app.services import dashboard_service as svc
24	
25	router = APIRouter(tags=["dashboard"])
26	
27	_WINDOW_DEFAULT = 7
28	_WINDOW_MAX = 90
29	
30	
31	class KpisRead(BaseModel):
32	    health_score: float | None
33	    pass_rate: float | None
34	    total_runs: int
35	    active_connections: int
36	
37	
38	class TrendPointRead(BaseModel):
39	    day: date
40	    succeeded: int
41	    failed: int
42	
43	
44	class SuitePerformanceRead(BaseModel):
45	    suite_id: uuid.UUID
46	    name: str
47	    score: float | None
48	    state: str  # optimal | stable | critical | unknown
49	
50	
51	class DashboardSummaryRead(BaseModel):
52	    window_days: int
53	    kpis: KpisRead
54	    trend: list[TrendPointRead]
55	    suite_performance: list[SuitePerformanceRead]
56	
57	
58	@router.get(
59	    "/dashboard/summary",
60	    response_model=DashboardSummaryRead,
61	    summary="Dashboard summary — KPIs, run trend, per-suite performance",
62	)
63	def get_dashboard_summary(
64	    current_user: Annotated[User, Depends(get_current_user)],
65	    db: Annotated[Session, Depends(get_db)],
66	    window_days: Annotated[int, Query(ge=1, le=_WINDOW_MAX)] = _WINDOW_DEFAULT,
67	) -> DashboardSummaryRead:
68	    summary = svc.dashboard_summary(db, user_id=current_user.id, window_days=window_days)
69	    return DashboardSummaryRead(
70	        window_days=summary.window_days,
71	        kpis=KpisRead(
72	            health_score=summary.kpis.health_score,
73	            pass_rate=summary.kpis.pass_rate,
74	            total_runs=summary.kpis.total_runs,
75	            active_connections=summary.kpis.active_connections,
76	        ),
77	        trend=[
78	            TrendPointRead(day=p.day, succeeded=p.succeeded, failed=p.failed) for p in summary.trend
79	        ],
80	        suite_performance=[
81	            SuitePerformanceRead(suite_id=s.suite_id, name=s.name, score=s.score, state=s.state)
82	            for s in summary.suite_performance
83	        ],
84	    )
85
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/arijit/Coding/Python/DataQ/frontend/src/api/dashboard.ts",
  "content": "import { api } from './client';\n\n/**\n * Dashboard summary API — the read model behind the Enhanced Monitoring\n * Dashboard (backend `dashboard.py`, ADR 0022). One suite-scoped aggregate:\n * KPIs + a per-day run trend + per-suite performance. The backend scopes the\n * data to the caller's accessible (owned-or-shared) suites, so this client\n * never has to. KPIs are nullable: an empty workspace returns `null` rather\n * than a fabricated 0/100 (KPI honesty).\n */\n\n/** Per-suite performance state band (mirrors `SuitePerformanceRead.state`). */\nexport type PerformanceState = 'optimal' | 'stable' | 'critical' | 'unknown';\n\n/** Mirrors `KpisRead` — `null` when no severity results are in the window. */\nexport interface Kpis {\n  health_score: number | null;\n  pass_rate: number | null;\n  total_runs: number;\n  active_connections: number;\n}\n\n/** Mirrors `TrendPointRead` — one zero-filled day of succeeded/failed run counts. */\nexport interface TrendPoint {\n  day: string; // ISO date (YYYY-MM-DD)\n  succeeded: number;\n  failed: number;\n}\n\n/** Mirrors `SuitePerformanceRead` — a suite's health from its latest run. */\nexport interface SuitePerformance {\n  suite_id: string;\n  name: string;\n  score: number | null;\n  state: PerformanceState;\n}\n\n/** Mirrors `DashboardSummaryRead`. */\nexport interface DashboardSummary {\n  window_days: number;\n  kpis: Kpis;\n  trend: TrendPoint[];\n  suite_performance: SuitePerformance[];\n}\n\n/**\n * Fetch the dashboard summary over a trailing window (`window_days`, 1–90;\n * default 7 server-side). The range selector maps 24h/7d/30d → 1/7/30.\n */\nexport async function getDashboardSummary(windowDays?: number): Promise<DashboardSummary> {\n  const { data } = await api.get<DashboardSummary>('/dashboard/summary', {\n    params: windowDays ? { window_days: windowDays } : undefined,\n  });\n  return data;\n}\n"
}
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/arijit/Coding/Python/DataQ/frontend/src/components/dashboard/MetricCard.tsx",
  "content": "import { Card, Flex, Progress, Skeleton, Typography } from 'antd';\nimport type { ReactNode } from 'react';\n\nimport { BRAND } from '../../theme';\n\n/**\n * A single KPI tile on the dashboard (prototype `MetricCard`): a label, a large\n * value, an optional unit suffix, an optional progress bar, and an optional\n * footnote — fronted by a tinted icon chip.\n *\n * The prototype also shows a period-over-period delta (\"+0.4%\", trend arrow);\n * we omit it deliberately — the summary endpoint has no historical comparison\n * to back it, and a fabricated delta would violate KPI honesty (ADR 0022 /\n * 0018). Add it only once a query backs it.\n *\n * `value === null` renders an em dash (no data for the window) rather than 0,\n * so an empty workspace reads as \"nothing yet\", not \"scored zero\".\n */\ninterface MetricCardProps {\n  label: string;\n  /** Pre-formatted display value, or `null` for the no-data em dash. */\n  value: ReactNode | null;\n  icon: ReactNode;\n  /** Trailing unit shown next to the value (e.g. \"%\", \"operational\"). */\n  unit?: string;\n  /** 0–100 progress bar under the value; omit for plain count metrics. */\n  progress?: number | null;\n  footnote?: string;\n  loading?: boolean;\n}\n\nexport function MetricCard({\n  label,\n  value,\n  icon,\n  unit,\n  progress,\n  footnote,\n  loading = false,\n}: MetricCardProps) {\n  return (\n    <Card size=\"small\" style={{ height: '100%' }}>\n      <Flex vertical gap={12}>\n        <Flex justify=\"space-between\" align=\"flex-start\" gap={8}>\n          <Typography.Text type=\"secondary\" style={{ fontSize: 13 }}>\n            {label}\n          </Typography.Text>\n          <span\n            aria-hidden\n            style={{\n              display: 'inline-flex',\n              alignItems: 'center',\n              justifyContent: 'center',\n              width: 32,\n              height: 32,\n              borderRadius: 8,\n              background: BRAND.selectedBg,\n              color: BRAND.primary,\n              flexShrink: 0,\n            }}\n          >\n            {icon}\n          </span>\n        </Flex>\n\n        {loading ? (\n          <Skeleton.Input active size=\"large\" style={{ width: 96 }} />\n        ) : (\n          <Flex align=\"baseline\" gap={6}>\n            <Typography.Title level={2} style={{ margin: 0, lineHeight: 1, color: BRAND.ink }}>\n              {value === null ? '—' : value}\n            </Typography.Title>\n            {unit && value !== null && (\n              <Typography.Text type=\"secondary\" style={{ fontSize: 13 }}>\n                {unit}\n              </Typography.Text>\n            )}\n          </Flex>\n        )}\n\n        {progress != null && !loading && (\n          <Progress percent={Math.round(progress)} showInfo={false} size=\"small\" />\n        )}\n        {footnote && (\n          <Typography.Text type=\"secondary\" style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>\n            {footnote}\n          </Typography.Text>\n        )}\n      </Flex>\n    </Card>\n  );\n}\n"
}
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/arijit/Coding/Python/DataQ/frontend/src/pages/Dashboard.tsx",
  "content": "import {\n  ApiOutlined,\n  CheckCircleOutlined,\n  SafetyOutlinedIcon,\n  ThunderboltOutlined,\n} from '@ant-design/icons';\nimport { Alert, Col, Flex, Row, Segmented, Typography } from 'antd';\nimport { useState } from 'react';\n\nimport { getDashboardSummary } from '../api/dashboard';\nimport { MetricCard } from '../components/dashboard/MetricCard';\nimport { useAsyncData } from '../hooks/useAsyncData';\n\n/**\n * Enhanced Monitoring Dashboard (`/dashboard`, ADR 0022) — the post-login\n * landing. Phase 2.2 ships the KPI row + range selector; the Quality Trends\n * chart, Suite Performance bars, and Recent Runs table land in 2.3–2.5.\n *\n * Every KPI is backed by the summary endpoint — the prototype's \"Total\n * Anomalies\" and \"Avg. Time to Resolution\" tiles are intentionally omitted\n * (no query backs them yet; KPI honesty, ADR 0022 / 0018).\n */\n\n/** Range option label → trailing window in days for the summary query. */\nconst RANGES = [\n  { label: 'Last 24h', days: 1 },\n  { label: '7d', days: 7 },\n  { label: '30d', days: 30 },\n] as const;\ntype RangeLabel = (typeof RANGES)[number]['label'];\n\nfunction pct(value: number | null): string | null {\n  return value === null ? null : `${value}`;\n}\n\nexport function Dashboard() {\n  const [range, setRange] = useState<RangeLabel>('7d');\n  const days = RANGES.find((r) => r.label === range)?.days ?? 7;\n  // Re-fetch when the range changes: bump the fetcher via `reload` after the\n  // range state updates (both batch into one render → the effect re-runs with\n  // the new window).\n  const { state, reload } = useAsyncData(() => getDashboardSummary(days));\n\n  const onRangeChange = (value: RangeLabel) => {\n    setRange(value);\n    reload();\n  };\n\n  const loading = state.status === 'loading';\n  const kpis = state.status === 'ok' ? state.data.kpis : null;\n\n  return (\n    <Flex vertical gap={20} style={{ maxWidth: 1200 }}>\n      <Flex justify=\"space-between\" align=\"center\" gap={12} wrap>\n        <div>\n          <Typography.Title level={3} style={{ margin: 0 }}>\n            Monitoring Dashboard\n          </Typography.Title>\n          <Typography.Text type=\"secondary\">Your workspace data-quality health.</Typography.Text>\n        </div>\n        <Segmented<RangeLabel>\n          value={range}\n          onChange={onRangeChange}\n          options={RANGES.map((r) => r.label)}\n        />\n      </Flex>\n\n      {state.status === 'error' && (\n        <Alert\n          type=\"error\"\n          showIcon\n          message=\"Failed to load dashboard\"\n          description={state.error}\n        />\n      )}\n\n      <Row gutter={[16, 16]}>\n        <Col xs={24} sm={12} xl={6}>\n          <MetricCard\n            label=\"Data Integrity Score\"\n            value={pct(kpis?.health_score ?? null)}\n            unit=\"%\"\n            icon={<SafetyOutlinedIcon />}\n            progress={kpis?.health_score ?? null}\n            loading={loading}\n          />\n        </Col>\n        <Col xs={24} sm={12} xl={6}>\n          <MetricCard\n            label=\"Pass Rate\"\n            value={pct(kpis?.pass_rate ?? null)}\n            unit=\"%\"\n            icon={<CheckCircleOutlined />}\n            progress={kpis?.pass_rate ?? null}\n            loading={loading}\n          />\n        </Col>\n        <Col xs={24} sm={12} xl={6}>\n          <MetricCard\n            label=\"Total Runs\"\n            value={kpis ? kpis.total_runs : null}\n            icon={<ThunderboltOutlined />}\n            footnote={`Last ${days === 1 ? '24h' : `${days} days`}`}\n            loading={loading}\n          />\n        </Col>\n        <Col xs={24} sm={12} xl={6}>\n          <MetricCard\n            label=\"Active Connections\"\n            value={kpis ? kpis.active_connections : null}\n            unit=\"operational\"\n            icon={<ApiOutlined />}\n            loading={loading}\n          />\n        </Col>\n      </Row>\n    </Flex>\n  );\n}\n"
}
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/arijit/Coding/Python/DataQ/frontend/tests/pages/Dashboard.test.tsx",
  "content": "import { render, screen, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react';\nimport userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';\nimport { MemoryRouter } from 'react-router-dom';\nimport { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';\n\nimport { type DashboardSummary, getDashboardSummary } from '../../src/api/dashboard';\nimport { Dashboard } from '../../src/pages/Dashboard';\n\nvi.mock('../../src/api/dashboard', async (importOriginal) => {\n  const actual = await importOriginal<typeof import('../../src/api/dashboard')>();\n  return { ...actual, getDashboardSummary: vi.fn() };\n});\n\nconst mockGet = vi.mocked(getDashboardSummary);\n\nconst summary: DashboardSummary = {\n  window_days: 7,\n  kpis: { health_score: 81.2, pass_rate: 50, total_runs: 12, active_connections: 3 },\n  trend: [],\n  suite_performance: [],\n};\n\nafterEach(() => {\n  vi.clearAllMocks();\n});\n\nfunction renderPage() {\n  return render(\n    <MemoryRouter>\n      <Dashboard />\n    </MemoryRouter>,\n  );\n}\n\ndescribe('Dashboard', () => {\n  it('renders the backed KPIs from the summary', async () => {\n    mockGet.mockResolvedValue(summary);\n    renderPage();\n\n    expect(await screen.findByText('81.2')).toBeInTheDocument();\n    expect(screen.getByText('Data Integrity Score')).toBeInTheDocument();\n    expect(screen.getByText('50')).toBeInTheDocument(); // pass rate\n    expect(screen.getByText('12')).toBeInTheDocument(); // total runs\n    expect(screen.getByText('3')).toBeInTheDocument(); // active connections\n    // Default range is 7d.\n    expect(mockGet).toHaveBeenCalledWith(7);\n  });\n\n  it('does not render the unbacked prototype KPIs (KPI honesty)', async () => {\n    mockGet.mockResolvedValue(summary);\n    renderPage();\n    await screen.findByText('81.2');\n\n    expect(screen.queryByText(/anomalies/i)).not.toBeInTheDocument();\n    expect(screen.queryByText(/time to resolution/i)).not.toBeInTheDocument();\n  });\n\n  it('renders em dashes for null KPIs on an empty workspace', async () => {\n    mockGet.mockResolvedValue({\n      window_days: 7,\n      kpis: { health_score: null, pass_rate: null, total_runs: 0, active_connections: 0 },\n      trend: [],\n      suite_performance: [],\n    });\n    renderPage();\n\n    // health_score + pass_rate render as em dashes; total_runs/connections as 0.\n    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getAllByText('—').length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2));\n  });\n\n  it('refetches with the new window when the range changes', async () => {\n    mockGet.mockResolvedValue(summary);\n    renderPage();\n    await screen.findByText('81.2');\n\n    await userEvent.click(screen.getByText('30d'));\n    await waitFor(() => expect(mockGet).toHaveBeenCalledWith(30));\n  });\n\n  it('shows an error alert when the summary fails to load', async () => {\n    mockGet.mockRejectedValue(new Error('boom'));\n    renderPage();\n    expect(await screen.findByText('Failed to load dashboard')).toBeInTheDocument();\n  });\n});\n"
}
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/arijit/Coding/Python/DataQ/frontend/src/components/dashboard/QualityTrends.tsx",
  "content": "import { Card, Flex, Typography } from 'antd';\nimport { Bar, BarChart, CartesianGrid, Legend, Tooltip, XAxis, YAxis } from 'recharts';\n\nimport type { TrendPoint } from '../../api/dashboard';\nimport {\n  AXIS_TICK,\n  GRID_PROPS,\n  RUN_STATUS_CHART_COLORS,\n  TOOLTIP_STYLE,\n} from '../charts/chartTheme';\nimport { ResponsiveChart } from '../charts/ResponsiveChart';\n\n/**\n * Quality Trends (prototype `QualityTrends`): succeeded vs failed runs per day\n * over the selected window, as a stacked bar. Maps the summary's `trend` (a\n * zero-filled daily series, ADR 0022) onto the run-status chart palette so the\n * bars read the same green/red as the run-status tags elsewhere.\n *\n * Rendered in a light card rather than the prototype's dark panel — the app's\n * theme is a light canvas (theme.ts) and a single dark card would fight it; the\n * data, palette, and legend match the prototype.\n */\ninterface QualityTrendsProps {\n  trend: TrendPoint[];\n}\n\n/** ISO `YYYY-MM-DD` → short `Jun 13` axis label. */\nfunction shortDay(iso: string): string {\n  const d = new Date(`${iso}T00:00:00Z`);\n  return d.toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'short', day: '2-digit', timeZone: 'UTC' });\n}\n\nexport function QualityTrends({ trend }: QualityTrendsProps) {\n  const isEmpty = trend.every((p) => p.succeeded === 0 && p.failed === 0);\n  const data = trend.map((p) => ({ ...p, label: shortDay(p.day) }));\n\n  return (\n    <Card size=\"small\" style={{ height: '100%' }}>\n      <Flex vertical gap={4} style={{ marginBottom: 8 }}>\n        <Typography.Text strong style={{ fontSize: 16 }}>\n          Quality Trends\n        </Typography.Text>\n        <Typography.Text type=\"secondary\" style={{ fontSize: 13 }}>\n          Succeeded vs failed runs per day\n        </Typography.Text>\n      </Flex>\n      <ResponsiveChart height={260} isEmpty={isEmpty} emptyText=\"No runs in this range yet\">\n        <BarChart data={data} margin={{ top: 8, right: 8, left: -16, bottom: 0 }}>\n          <CartesianGrid {...GRID_PROPS} vertical={false} />\n          <XAxis dataKey=\"label\" tick={AXIS_TICK} tickLine={false} minTickGap={24} />\n          <YAxis tick={AXIS_TICK} tickLine={false} allowDecimals={false} width={36} />\n          <Tooltip contentStyle={TOOLTIP_STYLE} cursor={{ fill: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.04)' }} />\n          <Legend iconType=\"circle\" wrapperStyle={{ fontSize: 13 }} />\n          <Bar\n            dataKey=\"succeeded\"\n            stackId=\"runs\"\n            name=\"Succeeded\"\n            fill={RUN_STATUS_CHART_COLORS.succeeded}\n            radius={[0, 0, 0, 0]}\n          />\n          <Bar\n            dataKey=\"failed\"\n            stackId=\"runs\"\n            name=\"Failed\"\n            fill={RUN_STATUS_CHART_COLORS.failed}\n            radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]}\n          />\n        </BarChart>\n      </ResponsiveChart>\n    </Card>\n  );\n}\n"
}
```

```json
}
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/arijit/Coding/Python/DataQ/frontend/src/components/dashboard/SuitePerformance.tsx",
  "content": "import { Card, Empty, Flex, Progress, Typography } from 'antd';\n\nimport type { PerformanceState, SuitePerformance as SuitePerf } from '../../api/dashboard';\n\n/**\n * Suite Performance (prototype `SuitePerformance`): per-suite health from each\n * suite's latest run, worst-first (the order the summary endpoint returns). The\n * score is the ADR-0005 health score; the state band (optimal/stable/critical)\n * colours both the label and the progress bar so a glance reads the spread.\n *\n * A suite whose latest run wrote no severity results has `score: null` /\n * `state: 'unknown'` — shown with an em dash and a flat grey bar, not 0%.\n */\ninterface SuitePerformanceProps {\n  suites: SuitePerf[];\n}\n\nconst STATE_COLOR: Record<PerformanceState, string> = {\n  optimal: '#52c41a', // green-6\n  stable: '#faad14', // gold-6\n  critical: '#ff4d4f', // red-6\n  unknown: '#bfbfbf', // gray-5\n};\n\nconst STATE_LABEL: Record<PerformanceState, string> = {\n  optimal: 'Optimal',\n  stable: 'Stable',\n  critical: 'Critical',\n  unknown: 'No data',\n};\n\nexport function SuitePerformance({ suites }: SuitePerformanceProps) {\n  return (\n    <Card size=\"small\" style={{ height: '100%' }}>\n      <Flex vertical gap={4} style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}>\n        <Typography.Text strong style={{ fontSize: 16 }}>\n          Suite Performance\n        </Typography.Text>\n        <Typography.Text type=\"secondary\" style={{ fontSize: 13 }}>\n          Health by suite, worst first\n        </Typography.Text>\n      </Flex>\n\n      {suites.length === 0 ? (\n        <Empty image={Empty.PRESENTED_IMAGE_SIMPLE} description=\"No suites with runs yet\" />\n      ) : (\n        <Flex vertical gap={16}>\n          {suites.map((s) => (\n            <div key={s.suite_id}>\n              <Flex justify=\"space-between\" align=\"center\" style={{ marginBottom: 6 }} gap={8}>\n                <Typography.Text strong ellipsis style={{ fontSize: 14 }}>\n                  {s.name}\n                </Typography.Text>\n                <Typography.Text strong style={{ fontSize: 13, color: STATE_COLOR[s.state] }}>\n                  {STATE_LABEL[s.state]}\n                </Typography.Text>\n              </Flex>\n              <Progress\n                percent={s.score ?? 0}\n                showInfo={s.score !== null}\n                format={(p) => `${p}`}\n                strokeColor={STATE_COLOR[s.state]}\n                size=\"small\"\n              />\n            </div>\n          ))}\n        </Flex>\n      )}\n    </Card>\n  );\n}\n"
}
```

```
1	import { App, Button, Card, Flex, Form, Input, Tag, Typography } from 'antd';
2	import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
3	import { useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
4	
5	import { getConnection } from '../api/connections';
6	import { createCheck, getSuite } from '../api/suites';
7	import { buildCheckPayload } from '../components/checks/checkForm';
8	import { ConfigFieldItem, SeverityThresholdFields } from '../components/checks/checkFormFields';
9	import { ColumnProfilePanel } from '../components/checks/ColumnProfilePanel';
10	import { DryRunPreview } from '../components/checks/DryRunPreview';
11	import {
12	  EXPECTATION_BY_TYPE,
13	  expectationsByCategoryFor,
14	  type ExpectationCategory,
15	} from '../components/checks/expectationCatalog';
16	import { useAsyncData } from '../hooks/useAsyncData';
17	
18	// Monitor-kind categories reserved by ADR 0012 — surfaced (disabled) so the
19	// roadmap is visible; v1 authors GX expectations only.
20	const RESERVED_CATEGORIES = ['Freshness', 'Volume', 'Schema drift'] as const;
21	
22	/**
23	 * Dedicated full-page check authoring flow (GX-Cloud style): pick a category →
24	 * pick an expectation → fill its config + thresholds. Editing an existing check
25	 * still uses the lighter drawer on the suite detail panel.
26	 */
27	export function CheckNew() {
28	  const navigate = useNavigate();
29	  const { suiteId } = useParams<{ suiteId: string }>();
30	  const { message } = App.useApp();
31	  const [category, setCategory] = useState<ExpectationCategory>();
32	  const [expectationType, setExpectationType] = useState<string>();
33	  const [form] = Form.useForm();
34	  const column = Form.useWatch(['config', 'column'], form) as string | undefined;
35	  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
36	  // Load the suite + its connection together: the run target (#215) drives the
37	  // dry-run preview's table/schema, and the connection type gates the Custom-SQL
38	  // category (ADR 0019 — SQL datasources only).
39	  const { state } = useAsyncData(async () => {
40	    if (!suiteId) throw new Error('no suite');
41	    const suite = await getSuite(suiteId);
42	    // Best-effort: a suite may be readable while its connection isn't (shared
43	    // suite). The connection only gates the Custom-SQL category — never let its
44	    // absence break the rest of the page (target / dry-run / profiler).
45	    const connection = await getConnection(suite.connection_id).catch(() => null);
46	    return { suite, connection };
47	  });
48	  const target = state.status === 'ok' ? state.data.suite.target : null;
49	  const connectionType = state.status === 'ok' ? state.data.connection?.type : undefined;
50	  const categories = expectationsByCategoryFor(connectionType);
51	
52	  const backToSuite = () => navigate(suiteId ? `/suites/${suiteId}` : '/suites');
53	  const spec = expectationType ? EXPECTATION_BY_TYPE[expectationType] : undefined;
54	
55	  // Start the config form clean each time an expectation is (re)picked — after
56	  // the <Form> mounts (it only renders in the config step), so the store is
57	  // connected and a re-pick can't leak the prior expectation's fields.
58	  useEffect(() => {
59	    if (expectationType) form.resetFields();
60	  }, [expectationType, form]);
61	
62	  const onFinish = async (values: Record<string, unknown>) => {
63	    if (!suiteId || !expectationType) return;
64	    setSubmitting(true);
65	    try {
66	      await createCheck(
67	        suiteId,
68	        buildCheckPayload({ ...values, expectation_type: expectationType }),
69	      );
70	      message.success(`${values.name as string}: created`);
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/arijit/Coding/Python/DataQ/frontend/src/pages/RunDetail.tsx",
  "content": "import { ArrowLeftOutlined } from '@ant-design/icons';\nimport { Alert, Button, Card, Empty, Flex, Spin, Table, Tag, Typography } from 'antd';\nimport type { ColumnsType } from 'antd/es/table';\nimport { useMemo } from 'react';\nimport { useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';\n\nimport { getRun, type Result, type ResultStatus } from '../api/runs';\nimport { type Check, getSuite, listChecks } from '../api/suites';\nimport {\n  formatDuration,\n  formatTimestamp,\n  RESULT_STATUS_COLORS,\n  RUN_STATUS_COLORS,\n} from '../components/results/resultsFormat';\nimport { ScalarValue } from '../components/results/ScalarValue';\nimport { useAsyncData } from '../hooks/useAsyncData';\n\n/**\n * Routed run-detail page (`/results/:runId`, ADR 0022) — replaces the run-detail\n * drawer so a run is deep-linkable and refreshable. Loads the run + its results\n * by id, plus the suite name and per-check names for display.\n *\n * Sample failing rows stay withheld by the API (PII, #226 / ADR 0018) — this\n * page never requests or renders them.\n */\nexport function RunDetail() {\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\n  const { runId } = useParams<{ runId: string }>();\n\n  const { state } = useAsyncData(async () => {\n    if (!runId) throw new Error('no run');\n    const run = await getRun(runId);\n    // The suite may be readable while details race; tolerate a missing name/checks\n    // rather than failing the whole page.\n    const [suite, checks] = await Promise.all([\n      getSuite(run.suite_id).catch(() => null),\n      listChecks(run.suite_id).catch(() => [] as Check[]),\n    ]);\n    return { run, suiteName: suite?.name ?? null, checks };\n  });\n\n  const back = () => navigate('/results');\n\n  return (\n    <Flex vertical gap={16} style={{ maxWidth: 1000 }}>\n      <div>\n        <Button type=\"text\" icon={<ArrowLeftOutlined />} onClick={back} style={{ paddingLeft: 0 }}>\n          Results\n        </Button>\n      </div>\n\n      {state.status === 'loading' && <Spin tip=\"Loading run…\" size=\"large\" />}\n      {state.status === 'error' && (\n        <Alert type=\"error\" showIcon message=\"Failed to load run\" description={state.error} />\n      )}\n      {state.status === 'ok' && (\n        <RunDetailBody\n          run={state.data.run}\n          suiteName={state.data.suiteName}\n          checks={state.data.checks}\n        />\n      )}\n    </Flex>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction RunDetailBody({\n  run,\n  suiteName,\n  checks,\n}: {\n  run: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof getRun>>;\n  suiteName: string | null;\n  checks: Check[];\n}) {\n  const checksById = useMemo(() => {\n    const map = new Map<string, Check>();\n    for (const c of checks) map.set(c.id, c);\n    return map;\n  }, [checks]);\n\n  const passed = run.results.filter((r) => r.status === 'pass').length;\n\n  return (\n    <Flex vertical gap={16}>\n      <Typography.Title level={3} style={{ margin: 0 }}>\n        {suiteName ?? `Run ${run.suite_id.slice(0, 8)}`}\n      </Typography.Title>\n\n      <Flex gap={12} wrap>\n        <Stat label=\"Status\">\n          <Tag color={RUN_STATUS_COLORS[run.status]}>{run.status}</Tag>\n        </Stat>\n        <Stat label=\"Checks passed\">\n          {run.results.length === 0 ? '—' : `${passed} / ${run.results.length}`}\n        </Stat>\n        <Stat label=\"Triggered by\">{run.triggered_by ?? '—'}</Stat>\n        <Stat label=\"Started\">{formatTimestamp(run.started_at)}</Stat>\n        <Stat label=\"Duration\">{formatDuration(run.started_at, run.finished_at)}</Stat>\n      </Flex>\n\n      <ResultsTable results={run.results} checks={checksById} />\n    </Flex>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction Stat({ label, children }: { label: string; children: React.ReactNode }) {\n  return (\n    <Card size=\"small\" style={{ minWidth: 150 }}>\n      <Flex vertical gap={4}>\n        <Typography.Text type=\"secondary\" style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>\n          {label}\n        </Typography.Text>\n        <span style={{ fontSize: 15 }}>{children}</span>\n      </Flex>\n    </Card>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction ResultsTable({ results, checks }: { results: Result[]; checks: Map<string, Check> }) {\n  if (results.length === 0) {\n    return <Empty description=\"No check results — the run did not complete.\" />;\n  }\n  const columns: ColumnsType<Result> = [\n    {\n      title: 'Check',\n      dataIndex: 'check_id',\n      render: (id: string) =>\n        checks.get(id)?.name ?? <Typography.Text code>{id.slice(0, 8)}</Typography.Text>,\n    },\n    {\n      title: 'Expectation',\n      dataIndex: 'check_id',\n      render: (id: string) => (\n        <Typography.Text type=\"secondary\" style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>\n          {checks.get(id)?.expectation_type ?? '—'}\n        </Typography.Text>\n      ),\n    },\n    {\n      title: 'Status',\n      dataIndex: 'status',\n      width: 100,\n      render: (s: ResultStatus) => <Tag color={RESULT_STATUS_COLORS[s]}>{s}</Tag>,\n    },\n    {\n      title: 'Metric',\n      dataIndex: 'metric_value',\n      width: 90,\n      render: (v: number | null) => (v === null ? '—' : v),\n    },\n    {\n      title: 'Observed',\n      dataIndex: 'observed_value',\n      render: (v: Record<string, unknown> | null) => <ScalarValue value={v} />,\n    },\n  ];\n  return (\n    <Table rowKey=\"id\" size=\"small\" columns={columns} dataSource={results} pagination={false} />\n  );\n}\n"
}
```
