131	// ─────────────────────────── Run detail drawer ──────────────────────
132	
133	function RunDetailDrawer({
134	  run,
135	  suiteName,
136	  onClose,
137	}: {
138	  run: Run | null;
139	  suiteName: string | null;
140	  onClose: () => void;
141	}) {
142	  return (
143	    <Drawer
144	      open={run !== null}
145	      onClose={onClose}
146	      width={640}
147	      title={run ? `Run · ${suiteName ?? run.suite_id.slice(0, 8)}` : 'Run'}
148	      destroyOnClose
149	    >
150	      {/* Keyed by run id so switching runs (without closing) remounts and
151	          refetches — useAsyncData only fetches on mount/reload, not on prop
152	          change, so without the key the drawer would show the prior run. */}
153	      {run && <RunDetailBody key={run.id} run={run} />}
154	    </Drawer>
155	  );
156	}
157	
158	function RunDetailBody({ run }: { run: Run }) {
159	  // Remounted per run via the `key` at the call site → these fetch fresh.
160	  const { state } = useAsyncData(() => getRun(run.id));
161	  const { state: checksState } = useAsyncData(() => listChecks(run.suite_id));
162	
163	  const checks = useMemo(() => {
164	    const map = new Map<string, Check>();
165	    if (checksState.status === 'ok') {
166	      for (const c of checksState.data) map.set(c.id, c);
167	    }
168	    return map;
169	  }, [checksState]);
170	
171	  return (
172	    <Flex vertical gap={16}>
173	      <Flex gap={16} wrap>
174	        <Meta label="Status">
175	          <Tag color={RUN_STATUS_COLORS[run.status]}>{run.status}</Tag>
176	        </Meta>
177	        <Meta label="Triggered by">{run.triggered_by ?? '—'}</Meta>
178	        <Meta label="Started">{formatTimestamp(run.started_at)}</Meta>
179	        <Meta label="Duration">{formatDuration(run.started_at, run.finished_at)}</Meta>
180	      </Flex>
181	
182	      {state.status === 'loading' && <Spin tip="Loading results…" />}
183	      {state.status === 'error' && (
184	        <Alert type="error" showIcon message="Failed to load results" description={state.error} />
185	      )}
186	      {state.status === 'ok' && <ResultsTable results={state.data.results} checks={checks} />}
187	    </Flex>
188	  );
189	}
190	
191	function ResultsTable({ results, checks }: { results: Result[]; checks: Map<string, Check> }) {
192	  if (results.length === 0) {
193	    return <Empty description="No check results — the run did not complete." />;
194	  }
195	  const columns: ColumnsType<(typeof results)[number]> = [
196	    {
197	      title: 'Check',
198	      dataIndex: 'check_id',
199	      render: (id: string) =>
200	        checks.get(id)?.name ?? <Typography.Text code>{id.slice(0, 8)}</Typography.Text>,
201	    },
202	    {
203	      title: 'Expectation',
204	      dataIndex: 'check_id',
205	      render: (id: string) => (
206	        <Typography.Text type="secondary" style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>
207	          {checks.get(id)?.expectation_type ?? '—'}
208	        </Typography.Text>
209	      ),
210	    },
211	    {
212	      title: 'Status',
213	      dataIndex: 'status',
214	      width: 100,
215	      render: (s: ResultStatus) => <Tag color={RESULT_STATUS_COLORS[s]}>{s}</Tag>,
216	    },
217	    {
218	      title: 'Metric',
219	      dataIndex: 'metric_value',
220	      width: 90,
221	      render: (v: number | null) => (v === null ? '—' : v),
222	    },
223	    {
224	      title: 'Observed',
225	      dataIndex: 'observed_value',
226	      render: (v: Record<string, unknown> | null) => <ScalarValue value={v} />,
227	    },
228	  ];
229	  return (
230	    <Table rowKey="id" size="small" columns={columns} dataSource={results} pagination={false} />
231	  );
232	}
233	
234	// ─────────────────────────── Pipeline runs tab ──────────────────────
235

from '../../src/pages/RunDetail';\n\nvi.mock('../../src/api/runs', async (importOriginal) => {\n  const actual = await importOriginal<typeof import('../../src/api/runs')>();\n  return { ...actual, getRun: vi.fn() };\n});\n\nvi.mock('../../src/api/suites', async (importOriginal) => {\n  const actual = await importOriginal<typeof import('../../src/api/suites')>();\n  return { ...actual, getSuite: vi.fn(), listChecks: vi.fn() };\n});\n\nconst mockGetRun = vi.mocked(getRun);\nconst mockGetSuite = vi.mocked(getSuite);\nconst mockListChecks = vi.mocked(listChecks);\n\nconst suite: Suite = {\n  id: 's1',\n  name: 'Orders quality',\n  description: null,\n  connection_id: 'c1',\n  target: { table: 'ORDERS' },\n  created_by: 'u1',\n};\n\nconst check: Check = {\n  id: 'chk1',\n  suite_id: 's1',\n  name: 'order_id not null',\n  kind: 'expectation',\n  expectation_type: 'expect_column_values_to_not_be_null',\n  config: { column: 'order_id' },\n  warn_threshold: null,\n  fail_threshold: null,\n  critical_threshold: null,\n};\n\nconst runDetail: RunDetailType = {\n  id: 'r1',\n  suite_id: 's1',\n  status: 'succeeded',\n  triggered_by: 'manual:u1',\n  started_at: '2026-06-11T00:00:00Z',\n  finished_at: '2026-06-11T00:00:12Z',\n  created_at: '2026-06-11T00:00:00Z',\n  results: [\n    {\n      id: 'res1',\n      check_id: 'chk1',\n      status: 'warn',\n      metric_value: 2,\n      duration_ms: null,\n      observed_value: { unexpected_percent: 2 },\n      expected_value: null,\n    },\n  ],\n};\n\nfunction renderAt(runId: string) {\n  return render(\n    <MemoryRouter initialEntries={[`/results/${runId}`]}>\n      <Routes>\n        <Route path=\"/results/:runId\" element={<RunDetail />} />\n        <Route path=\"/results\" element={<div>results-list</div>} />\n      </Routes>\n    </MemoryRouter>,\n  );\n}\n\nafterEach(() => {\n  vi.clearAllMocks();\n});\n\ndescribe('RunDetail page', () => {\n  it('loads the run by id and renders its per-check results', async () => {\n    mockGetRun.mockResolvedValue(runDetail);\n    mockGetSuite.mockResolvedValue(suite);\n    mockListChecks.mockResolvedValue([check]);\n\n    renderAt('r1');\n\n    expect(await expect(screen.getByText('expect_column_values_to_not_be_null')).toBeInTheDocument();\n    expect(screen.getByText('warn')).toBeInTheDocument();\n    // Checks-passed stat: 0 of 1 passed (the one result is a warn).\n    expect(screen.getByText('0 / 1')).toBeInTheDocument();\n    expect(mockGetRun).toHaveBeenCalledWith('r1');\n  });\n\n  it('still renders when the suite name and checks fail to ```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/arijit/Coding/Python/DataQ/frontend/src/components/dashboard/RecentRuns.tsx",
  "content": "import { Alert, Card, Empty, Flex, Spin, Table, Tag, Typography } from 'antd';\nimport type { ColumnsType } from 'antd/es/table';\nimport { useMemo } from 'react';\nimport { Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';\n\nimport { listRuns, type Run, type RunStatus } from '../../api/runs';\nimport { listSuites } from '../../api/suites';\nimport { useAsyncData } from '../../hooks/useAsyncData';\nimport { formatDuration, formatTimestamp, RUN_STATUS_COLORS } from '../results/resultsFormat';\n\n/**\n * Recent Runs (prototype `RecentRuns`) — a cross-suite feed of the latest runs\n * on the dashboard, each row deep-linking to the routed run-detail page. Fetches\n * its own slice (the summary endpoint carries aggregates, not run rows); the\n * runs are already suite-scoped by the backend. \"View all\" → the Results page.\n *\n * The prototype's \"Anomalies\" column is dropped — there's no anomaly count on a\n * run (KPI honesty, ADR 0022).\n */\nconst RECENT_LIMIT = 8;\n\nexport function RecentRuns() {\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\n  const { state } = useAsyncData(() => listRuns({ limit: RECENT_LIMIT }));\n  const { state: suitesState } = useAsyncData(listSuites);\n\n  const suiteNames = useMemo(() => {\n    const map = new Map<string, string>();\n    if (suitesState.status === 'ok') {\n      for (const s of suitesState.data) map.set(s.id, s.name);\n    }\n    return map;\n  }, [suitesState]);\n\n  const columns: ColumnsType<Run> = [\n    {\n      title: 'Suite',\n      dataIndex: 'suite_id',\n      render: (suiteId: string) =>\n        suiteNames.get(suiteId) ?? <Typography.Text code>{suiteId.slice(0, 8)}</Typography.Text>,\n    },\n    {\n      title: 'Status',\n      dataIndex: 'status',\n      width: 120,\n      render: (s: RunStatus) => <Tag color={RUN_STATUS_COLORS[s]}>{s}</Tag>,\n    },\n    { title: 'Triggered by', dataIndex: 'triggered_by', render: (t: string | null) => t ?? '—' },\n    { title: 'Started', dataIndex: 'started_at', render: (t: string | null) => formatTimestamp(t) },\n    {\n      title: 'Duration',\n      width: 110,\n      render: (_: unknown, run: Run) => formatDuration(run.started_at, run.finished_at),\n    },\n  ];\n\n  return (\n    <Card size=\"small\">\n      <Flex justify=\"space-between\" align=\"center\" style={{ marginBottom: 12 }}>\n        <Typography.Text strong style={{ fontSize: 16 }}>\n          Recent Runs\n        </Typography.Text>\n        <Link to=\"/results\">View all</Link>\n      </Flex>\n\n      {state.status === 'loading' && <Spin tip=\"Loading runs…\" />}\n      {state.status === 'error' && (\n        <Alert type=\"error\" showIcon message=\"Failed to load runs\" description={state.error} />\n      )}\n      {state.status === 'ok' && (\n        <Table<Run>\n          rowKey=\"id\"\n          size=\"small\"\n          columns={columns}\n          dataSource={state.data}\n          pagination={false}\n          locale={{ emptyText: <Empty description=\"No runs yet.\" /> }}\n          onRow={(run) => ({\n            onClick: () => navigate(`/results/${run.id}`),\n            style: { cursor: 'pointer' },\n          })}\n        />\n      )}\n    </Card>\n  );\n}\n"
}
```

=== checks.py imports/deps ===
"""Check CRUD endpoints — GX expectations nested under a suite.

Routes are nested (`/suites/{suite_id}/checks/...`) so every check operation is
scoped to its suite (and, once suite-sharing lands, authorized once at the suite
level). Thin layer over `check_service`.

Threshold typing: requests accept `Decimal` so values land exactly in the
`Numeric` columns (a JSON float → unbounded NUMERIC would pollute precision);
responses emit `float` for clean JSON numbers, coerced from the stored Decimal.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import uuid
from datetime import datetime
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Annotated, Any

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, status
from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from backend.app.core.auth import get_current_user
from backend.app.core.secrets import SecretStore, get_secret_store
from backend.app.db.models import Connection, User
from backend.app.db.session import get_db
from backend.app.services import check_service as svc
from backend.app.services import dryrun_service as dryrun
from backend.app.services.suite_authz import require_permission

router = APIRouter(tags=["checks"])


class CheckCreate(BaseModel):
    name: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=256)
    # v1 authors only 'expectation' (service enforces; reserved kinds 422).
    kind: str = "expectation"
    expectation_type: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=128)
    config: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)
    warn_threshold: Decimal | None = None
    fail_threshold: Decimal | None = None
    critical_threshold: Decimal | None = None


class CheckUpdate(BaseModel):
    name: str | None = Field(default=None, min_length=1, max_length=256)
    expectation_type: str | None = Field(default=None, min_length=1, max_length=128)
    config: dict[str, Any] | None = None
    warn_threshold: Decimal | None = None
    fail_threshold: Decimal | None = None
    critical_threshold: Decimal | None = None


class CheckRead(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)

    id: uuid.UUID
    suite_id: uuid.UUID
    name: str
    kind: str
```

1	"""Check CRUD — checks are GX expectations nested under a suite.
2	
3	A check belongs to exactly one suite (FK + cascade). This layer validates the
4	suite exists, enforces the v1 monitor-kind limit, and treats the check's
5	`config` (the GX expectation kwargs) as free-form JSONB — per-expectation
6	schema validation against live data is the check dry-run path (a later Week-3
7	task), not CRUD.
8	
9	v1 monitor-kind limit (ADR 0012): although the schema CHECK reserves
10	`freshness / volume / schema_drift / anomaly / comparison`, v1 only *runs*
11	`expectation`. The API therefore refuses to author a non-`expectation` check —
12	a reserved kind is schema-valid for forward-compat but not yet runnable, so
13	letting a user create one would just produce a check that can never execute.
14	
15	FastAPI-free like the sibling services: takes a `Session`, returns ORM models,
16	raises `DataQError` subclasses.
17	"""
18

228	def list_check_versions(
229	    session: Session, suite_id: uuid.UUID, check_id: uuid.UUID
230	) -> list[CheckVersion]:
231	    """A check's version history, newest first (#280). 404 if the check is
232	    missing or doesn't belong to `suite_id`. Eager-loads each version's author
233	    (only query that needs it) so the API can name the editor without an N+1.
234	    """
235	    get_check(session, suite_id, check_id)  # 404 / cross-suite guard
236	    return list(
237	        session.scalars(
238	            select(CheckVersion)
239	            .where(CheckVersion.check_id == check_id)
240	            .options(selectinload(CheckVersion.author))
241	            .order_by(CheckVersion.version_no.desc())
242	        )
243	    )
244

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/arijit/Coding/Python/DataQ/backend/app/services/check_service.py",
  "old_string": "    get_check(session, suite_id, check_id)  # 404 / cross-suite guard\n    return list(\n        session.scalars(\n            select(CheckVersion)\n            .where(CheckVersion.check_id == check_id)\n            .options(selectinload(CheckVersion.author))\n            .order_by(CheckVersion.version_no.desc())\n        )\n    )",
  "new_string": "    get_check(session, suite_id, check_id)  # 404 / cross-suite guard\n    return list(\n        session.scalars(\n            select(CheckVersion)\n            .where(CheckVersion.check_id == check_id)\n            .options(selectinload(CheckVersion.author))\n            .order_by(CheckVersion.version_no.desc())\n        )\n    )\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass CheckResultPoint:\n    \"\"\"One past result for a check — the trend datum behind the per-check chart.\"\"\"\n\n    run_id: uuid.UUID\n    status: str\n    metric_value: float | None\n    created_at: datetime\n\n\ndef list_check_result_history(\n    session: Session, suite_id: uuid.UUID, check_id: uuid.UUID, *, limit: int = 30\n) -> list[CheckResultPoint]:\n    \"\"\"A check's recent results in chronological order (oldest→newest) for the\n    per-check trend (ADR 0022). 404 if the check is missing or cross-suite.\n\n    Takes the latest `limit` results (newest-first in SQL, then reversed) so the\n    chart shows the most recent window left-to-right. `metric_value` is the\n    SQL-aggregatable scalar a run measured (ADR 0012); `None` for checks that\n    record no metric. Suite scoping is the caller's (router `require_permission`);\n    the Run join only guards against a result leaking across suites.\n    \"\"\"\n    get_check(session, suite_id, check_id)  # 404 / cross-suite guard\n    stmt = (\n        select(Result.run_id, Result.status, Result.metric_value, Run.created_at)\n        .join(Run, Result.run_id == Run.id)\n        .where(Result.check_id == check_id, Run.suite_id == suite_id)\n        .order_by(Run.created_at.desc())\n        .limit(limit)\n    )\n    rows = [\n        CheckResultPoint(\n            run_id=run_id,\n            status=status,\n            metric_value=float(metric_value) if metric_value is not None else None,\n            created_at=created_at,\n        )\n        for run_id, status, metric_value, created_at in session.execute(stmt)\n    ]\n    rows.reverse()  # chronological for the chart x-axis\n    return rows"
}

1	"""Check endpoint tests against a real Postgres (db_session) via TestClient.
2	
3	Checks are nested under a suite. A connection + suite are created per test for
4	the FK chain; auth runs in dev-bypass (conftest). Skips without
5	TEST_DATABASE_URL.
6	"""
7	
8	import uuid
9	from collections.abc import Iterator
10	from typing import Any
11	
12	import pytest
13	from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
14	
15	from backend.app.core.auth import get_current_user
16	from backend.app.datasources.base import CheckOutcome, SuiteOutcome
17	from backend.app.db.models import Connection, Suite, User
18	from backend.app.db.session import get_db
19	from backend.app.main import app
20	from backend.app.services import dryrun_service
21	
22	
23	@pytest.fixture
24	def client(db_session: Any) -> Iterator[TestClient]:
25	    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = lambda: db_session
26	    try:
27	        yield TestClient(app)
28	    finally:
29	        app.dependency_overrides.clear()
30	
31	
32	def _suite_id(client: TestClient, db_session: Any, conn_type: str = "snowflake") -> str:
33	    """Create a connection (ORM) + suite (API) and return the suite id.
34	
35	    `conn_type` lets a test pick the datasource (e.g. 's3' to exercise custom-SQL
36	    datasource gating); defaults to Snowflake.
37	    """
38	    owner = User(aad_object_id=uuid.uuid4().hex, email="owner@example.com")
39	    db_session.add(owner)
40	    db_session.flush()
41	    config = {"account": "ab12345.eu-west-1"} if conn_type == "snowflake" else {}
42	    conn = Connection(
43	        name=f"{conn_type}-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}",
44	        type=conn_type,
45	        env="dev",
46	        config=config,
47	        created_by=owner.id,
48	    )
49	    db_session.add(conn)
50	    db_session.commit()
51	    resp = client.post(
52	        "/api/v1/suites",
53	        json={"name": "finance", "description": None, "connection_id": str(conn.id)},
54	    )
55	    return str(resp.json()["id"])
56	
57	
58	def _payload(**overrides: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
59	    body: dict[str, Any] = {
60	        "name": "orders not null",
61	        "expectation_type": "expect_column_values_to_not_be_null",
62	        "config": {"column": "order_id"},
63	    }
64	    body.update(overrides)
65	    return body
66	
67	
68	# ───────────────────────── create ──────────────────────────────────
69	
70

329	def test_create_records_initial_version(client: TestClient, db_session: Any) -> None:

344	def test_update_appends_version_newest_first(client: TestClient, db_session: Any) -> None:
345	    sid = _suite_id(client, db_session)
346	    cid = client.post(f"/api/v1/suites/{sid}/checks", json=_payload()).json()["id"]
347	    client.patch(

351	
352	    versions = client.get(f"/api/v1/suites/{sid}/checks/{cid}/versions").json()
353	    assert [v["version_no"] for v in versions] == [2, 1]  # newest first
354	    # v2 is the post-update state; v1 still carries the original config (the
355	    # whole point — "see previous config before overwriting").
356	    assert versions[0]["config"] == {"column": "amount"}
357	    assert versions[0]["warn_threshold"] == 0.9
358	    assert versions[1]["config"] == {"column": "order_id"}
359	    assert versions[1]["warn_threshold"] is None

259	# ───────────────────────── access enforcement (PR-E2) ──────────────
260	
261	
262	def _as(user: User) -> None:
263	    app.dependency_overrides[get_current_user] = lambda: user
264	
265	
266	def _owner_b_e_suite(db_session: Any) -> tuple[User, User, User, str]:
267	    """owner + B + E and a suite owned by `owner` (checks are added per-test)."""
268	    owner = User(aad_object_id=uuid.uuid4().hex, email="owner@ex")
269	    b = User(aad_object_id=uuid.uuid4().hex, email="b@ex")
270	    e = User(aad_object_id=uuid.uuid4().hex, email="e@ex")
271	    db_session.add_all([owner, b, e])
272	    db_session.flush()
273	    conn = Connection(
274	        name=f"sf-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}",
275	        type="snowflake",
276	        env="dev",
277	        config={"account": "x"},
278	        created_by=owner.id,
279	    )
280	    db_session.add(conn)
281	    db_session.flush()
282	    suite = Suite(name="s", connection_id=conn.id, created_by=owner.id)
283	    db_session.add(suite)
284	    db_session.commit()
285	    return owner, b, e, str(suite.id)
286	
287	
288	def _grant(client: TestClient, owner: User, sid: str, target: User, perm: str) -> None:
289	    _as(owner)
290	    granted = client.post(
291	        f"/api/v1/suites/{sid}/shares", json={"user_id": str(target.id), "permission": perm}
292	    )
293	    assert granted.status_code == 201
294

  "new_string": "    v1 = client.get(f\"/api/v1/suites/{sid}/checks/{cid}/versions\").json()[0]\n    assert v1[\"changed_by\"] == str(owner.id)\n    assert v1[\"changed_by_name\"] == \"Ed Editor\"\n\n\n# ───────────────────────── result history (trend, ADR 0022) ─────────\n\n\ndef _run_with_result(\n    db_session: Any,\n    suite_id: str,\n    check_id: str,\n    *,\n    status: str,\n    metric_value: float | None,\n    age_days: float,\n) -> None:\n    from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta\n\n    from backend.app.db.models import Result, Run\n\n    when = datetime.now(UTC) - timedelta(days=age_days)\n    run = Run(suite_id=uuid.UUID(suite_id), status=\"succeeded\", created_at=when)\n    db_session.add(run)\n    db_session.flush()\n    db_session.add(\n        Result(\n            run_id=run.id,\n            check_id=uuid.UUID(check_id),\n            status=status,\n            metric_value=metric_value,\n            created_at=when,\n        )\n    )\n    db_session.commit()\n\n\ndef test_history_returns_results_oldest_first(client: TestClient, db_session: Any) -> None:\n    sid = _suite_id(client, db_session)\n    cid = client.post(f\"/api/v1/suites/{sid}/checks\", json=_payload()).json()[\"id\"]\n    _run_with_result(db_session, sid, cid, status=\"pass\", metric_value=0.0, age_days=2)\n    _run_with_result(db_session, sid, cid, status=\"warn\", metric_value=2.5, age_days=0)\n\n    history = client.get(f\"/api/v1/suites/{sid}/checks/{cid}/history\").json()\n    assert [p[\"status\"] for p in history] == [\"pass\", \"warn\"]  # chronological\n    assert [p[\"metric_value\"] for p in history] == [0.0, 2.5]\n\n\ndef test_history_empty_for_check_with_no_runs(client: TestClient, db_session: Any) -> None:\n    sid = _suite_id(client, db_session)\n    cid = client.post(f\"/api/v1/suites/{sid}/checks\", json=_payload()).json()[\"id\"]\n    assert client.get(f\"/api/v1/suites/{sid}/checks/{cid}/history\").json() == []\n\n\ndef test_history_honours_limit_keeping_most_recent(client: TestClient, db_session: Any) -> None:\n    sid = _suite_id(client, db_session)\n    cid = client.post(f\"/api/v1/suites/{sid}/checks\", json=_payload()).json()[\"id\"]\n    for age in (3, 2, 1):\n        _run_with_result(db_session, sid, cid, status=\"pass\", metric_value=age, age_days=age)\n\n    history = client.get(f\"/api/v1/suites/{sid}/checks/{cid}/history?limit=2\").json()\n    # Latest 2 by run time, returned chronologically: age=2 then age=1.\n    assert [p[\"metric_value\"] for p in history] == [2.0, 1.0]\n    assert client.get(f\"/api/v1/suites/{sid}/checks/{cid}/history?limit=0\").status_code == 422\n\n\ndef test_history_unknown_check_returns_404(client: TestClient, db_session: Any) -> None:\n    sid = _suite_id(client, db_session)\n    assert (\n        client.get(f\"/api/v1/suites/{sid}/checks/{uuid.uuid4()}/history\").status_code == 404\n    )\n\n\ndef test_history_outsider_cannot_read(client: TestClient, db_session: Any) -> None:\n    owner, _b, e, sid = _owner_b_e_suite(db_session)\n    _as(owner)\n    cid = client.post(f\"/api/v1/suites/{sid}/checks\", json=_payload()).json()[\"id\"]\n    _as(e)  # not owner, not shared\n    assert client.get(f\"/api/v1/suites/{sid}/checks/{cid}/history\").status_code == 403"

  "old_string": "/** A check's version history, newest first. Requires `view` on the suite. */\nexport async function listCheckVersions(suiteId: string, checkId: string): Promise<CheckVersion[]> {\n  const { data } = await api.get<CheckVersion[]>(`/suites/${suiteId}/checks/${checkId}/versions`);\n  return data;\n}",
  "new_string": "/** A check's version history, newest first. Requires `view` on the suite. */\nexport async function listCheckVersions(suiteId: string, checkId: string): Promise<CheckVersion[]> {\n  const { data } = await api.get<CheckVersion[]>(`/suites/${suiteId}/checks/${checkId}/versions`);\n  return data;\n}\n\n/**\n * Mirrors the backend `CheckResultPointRead` — one past result of a check\n * (status + `metric_value` + run time), the datum behind the per-check trend\n * chart (ADR 0022). `metric_value` is null for checks that record no metric.\n */\nexport interface CheckResultPoint {\n  run_id: string;\n  status: string;\n  metric_value: number | null;\n  created_at: string;\n}\n\n/**\n * A check's recent results in chronological order (oldest→newest) for the trend\n * chart. Requires `view` on the suite; `limit` caps the window (1–180, default 30).\n */\nexport async function listCheckHistory(\n  suiteId: string,\n  checkId: string,\n  limit?: number,\n): Promise<CheckResultPoint[]> {\n  const { data } = await api.get<CheckResultPoint[]>(\n    `/suites/${suiteId}/checks/${checkId}/history`,\n    { params: limit ? { limit } : undefined },\n  );\n  return data;\n}"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/arijit/Coding/Python/DataQ/frontend/src/components/checks/CheckTrend.tsx",
  "content": "import { Alert, Spin } from 'antd';\nimport { CartesianGrid, Line, LineChart, Tooltip, XAxis, YAxis } from 'recharts';\n\nimport { listCheckHistory } from '../../api/suites';\nimport { useAsyncData } from '../../hooks/useAsyncData';\nimport {\n  AXIS_TICK,\n  CHART_COLORS,\n  GRID_PROPS,\n  severityColor,\n  TOOLTIP_STYLE,\n} from '../charts/chartTheme';\nimport type { ResultStatus } from '../../api/runs';\nimport { ResponsiveChart } from '../charts/ResponsiveChart';\n\n/**\n * Per-check historical trend (Phase 2.6, ADR 0022): a check's `metric_value`\n * over its recent runs, as a recharts line with each point coloured by that\n * run's severity. Lazily fetched per check (only when a run-detail row expands),\n * so it doesn't fan out a request per check on page load.\n *\n * Empty when the check has no runs, or records no metric (e.g. a binary\n * pass/fail check) — there's nothing to plot.\n */\ninterface CheckTrendProps {\n  suiteId: string;\n  checkId: string;\n}\n\n/** ISO timestamp → short `Jun 13` label. */\nfunction shortDay(iso: string): string {\n  return new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'short', day: '2-digit' });\n}\n\nexport function CheckTrend({ suiteId, checkId }: CheckTrendProps) {\n  const { state } = useAsyncData(() => listCheckHistory(suiteId, checkId));\n\n  if (state.status === 'loading') return <Spin size=\"small\" />;\n  if (state.status === 'error') {\n    return <Alert type=\"error\" showIcon message=\"Failed to load trend\" description={state.error} />;\n  }\n\n  const withMetric = state.data.filter((p) => p.metric_value !== null);\n  const data = withMetric.map((p) => ({\n    label: shortDay(p.created_at),\n    metric: p.metric_value,\n    status: p.status as ResultStatus,\n  }));\n\n  return (\n    <ResponsiveChart height={180} isEmpty={data.length === 0} emptyText=\"No metric history yet\">\n      <LineChart data={data} margin={{ top: 8, right: 12, left: -16, bottom: 0 }}>\n        <CartesianGrid {...GRID_PROPS} vertical={false} />\n        <XAxis dataKey=\"label\" tick={AXIS_TICK} tickLine={false} minTickGap={24} />\n        <YAxis tick={AXIS_TICK} tickLine={false} width={40} />\n        <Tooltip contentStyle={TOOLTIP_STYLE} />\n        <Line\n          type=\"monotone\"\n          dataKey=\"metric\"\n          name=\"Metric\"\n          stroke={CHART_COLORS.primary}\n          strokeWidth={2}\n          dot={(props) => {\n            const { cx, cy, payload, index } = props as {\n              cx: number;\n              cy: number;\n              index: number;\n              payload: { status: ResultStatus };\n            };\n            return (\n              <circle\n                key={index}\n                cx={cx}\n                cy={cy}\n                r={3.5}\n                fill={severityColor(payload.status)}\n                stroke=\"#fff\"\n                strokeWidth={1}\n              />\n            );\n          }}\n        />\n      </LineChart>\n    </ResponsiveChart>\n  );\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/arijit/Coding/Python/DataQ/frontend/tests/components/CheckTrend.test.tsx",
  "content": "import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';\nimport { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';\n\nimport { type CheckResultPoint, listCheckHistory } from '../../src/api/suites';\nimport { CheckTrend } from '../../src/components/checks/CheckTrend';\n\nvi.mock('../../src/api/suites', async (importOriginal) => {\n  const actual = await importOriginal<typeof import('../../src/api/suites')>();\n  return { ...actual, listCheckHistory: vi.fn() };\n});\n\nconst mockHistory = vi.mocked(listCheckHistory);\n\nafterEach(() => {\n  vi.clearAllMocks();\n});\n\ndescribe('CheckTrend', () => {\n  it('fetches the check history for the given suite + check', async () => {\n    const points: CheckResultPoint[] = [\n      { run_id: 'r1', status: 'pass', metric_value: 0, created_at: '2026-06-10T00:00:00Z' },\n      { run_id: 'r2', status: 'warn', metric_value: 2.5, created_at: '2026-06-11T00:00:00Z' },\n    ];\n    mockHistory.mockResolvedValue(points);\n    render(<CheckTrend suiteId=\"s1\" checkId=\"c1\" />);\n\n    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(mockHistory).toHaveBeenCalledWith('s1', 'c1'));\n    // With metric data, the empty state is not shown.\n    expect(screen.queryByText('No metric history yet')).not.toBeInTheDocument();\n  });\n\n  it('shows an empty state when no point records a metric', async () => {\n    mockHistory.mockResolvedValue([\n      { run_id: 'r1', status: 'pass', metric_value: null, created_at: '2026-06-10T00:00:00Z' },\n    ]);\n    render(<CheckTrend suiteId=\"s1\" checkId=\"c1\" />);\n    expect(await screen.findByText('No metric history yet')).toBeInTheDocument();\n  });\n\n  it('shows an error when the history fails to load', async () => {\n    mockHistory.mockRejectedValue(new Error('boom'));\n    render(<CheckTrend suiteId=\"s1\" checkId=\"c1\" />);\n    expect(await screen.findByText('Failed to load trend')).toBeInTheDocument();\n  });\n});\n"
}

## What landed this session

| Phase | Commit | What |
|---|---|---|
| 2.2 | `9a71c0b` | `/dashboard` KPI cards + `api/dashboard.ts` client + nav/landing |
| 0.1 fix | `b600447` | recharts split into its own lazy chunk (vendor 388→42 kB) |
| 2.3 | `fa25374` | Quality Trends stacked bar chart |
| 2.4 | `fe87482` | Per-suite health bars (worst-first) |
| 1.1 | `878496e` | Routed `/results/:runId` run-detail page (drawer removed) |
| 2.5 | `995a280` | Recent Runs audit table (deep-links into run-detail) |
| 2.6 | `2faa7a2` + `c4dd595` | Per-check history endpoint + expandable trend chart |

(Plus the earlier `9a71c0b` predecessors: Phase 2.1 backend aggregates + Phase 0.1 chart primitives.)

## Verification
- **Frontend:** prettier/eslint/tsc clean; **241 tests pass** (was 222 → +19); production build confirms recharts (360 kB) ships only on the lazy `/dashboard` + `/results/:runId` chunks, not the initial bundle.
- **Backend:** ruff/black/mypy/bandit clean; **849 pass, 1 skip** (the new history endpoint has 5 tests; the two bandit B101 hits are pre-existing asserts, not my lines).

## Honesty calls I made (flagged in commits)
- Dropped the prototype's **"Total Anomalies"** and **"Avg. Time to Resolution"** KPIs and the Recent-Runs **Anomalies** column — no query backs them (KPI honesty, ADR 0022/0018). Null KPIs render `—`, not a fabricated 0/100.
- Quality Trends uses a **light card**, not the prototype's dark panel — a lone dark card fights the app's light-canvas theme (theme.ts). Data/palette/legend match.
- Per-check trend home = **run-detail expandable row** (resolved the open TODO decision).

## Where we are in Week 6
Phase 0.1, 1.1, and all of Phase 2 (2.1–2.6) are done — that's the dashboard fully built across KPIs, trends, suite health, recent runs, and per-check history. Remaining: **0.2** (full nav/footer-group restructure + error-page catch-all), **Phase 1.2** (CSV/JSON export on run detail), **Phase 3** (results filters), **Phase 4** (drawer→page restructures), **Phase 5** (Profile/Settings/Admin), **Phase 6** (error pages), **Phase 7** (alerting).

35	  if (!me.data.is_workspace_admin) {
36	    return <Forbidden message="The admin overview is restricted to workspace admins." />;
37	  }
38	
39	  return (
40	    <Flex vertical gap={24}>
41	      <Typography.Title level={3} style={{ margin: 0 }}>
42	        Admin
43	      </Typography.Title>

{

  "content": "import { Alert, Flex, Spin, Typography } from 'antd';\n\nimport { useMe } from '../auth/useMe';\nimport { Forbidden } from '../components/Forbidden';\n\n/**\n * Workspace Settings (`/settings`, ADR 0022). Phase 0.2 lands the routed shell +\n * the workspace-admin gate; Phase 5.2 fills in the tabs (General · Secrets ·\n * Notifications · Danger zone) — and only the controls a backend actually backs\n * (the rest stay labelled placeholders, KPI/feature honesty).\n *\n * Admin-only like the Admin page: gated on `/me`'s server-driven\n * `is_workspace_admin`; a non-admin who deep-links here sees Forbidden.\n */\nexport function Settings() {\n  const me = useMe();\n\n  if (me.status === 'loading') {\n    return <Spin size=\"large\" style={{ marginTop: 80 }} />;\n  }\n  if (me.status === 'error') {\n    return (\n      <Alert type=\"error\" showIcon message=\"Couldn't verify your access\" description={me.error} />\n    );\n  }\n  if (!me.data.is_workspace_admin) {\n    return <Forbidden message=\"Workspace settings are restricted to workspace admins.\" />;\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <Flex vertical gap={16}>\n      <Typography.Title level={3} style={{ margin: 0 }}>\n        Settings\n      </Typography.Title>\n      <Alert\n        type=\"info\"\n        showIcon\n        message=\"Workspace settings are coming soon.\"\n        description=\"General, secrets, notifications, and danger-zone controls land in a later Week-6 step — each wired only once a backend backs it.\"\n      />\n    </Flex>\n  );\n}\n"
}

37	  { key: '/profile', label: <Link to="/profile">Profile</Link> },
38	];
39	// Shown only to workspace admins (server-driven via /me). The route is always
40	// registered — a non-admin who deep-links to /admin hits the page's Forbidden
41	// state — so this gate is for nav convenience, not the security boundary.
42	const ADMIN_NAV_ITEM = { key: '/admin', label: <Link to="/admin">Admin</Link> };
43	
44	export function App() {
45	  const location = useLocation();
46	  const isAdmin = useIsWorkspaceAdmin();
47	  const navItems = isAdmin ? [...NAV_ITEMS, ADMIN_NAV_ITEM] : NAV_ITEMS;
48	  // Highlight the nav item whose path matches the current location — exact, or a
49	  // sub-path at a segment boundary (so `/suites` matches `/suites/123` but not a
50	  // sibling like `/suites-archive`). Plain startsWith would mis-highlight those.
51	  const selectedKeys = navItems
52	    .map((i) => i.key)
53	    .filter((k) => location.pathname === k || location.pathname.startsWith(`${k}/`));

Admin + Settings show only to workspace\n// admins (server-driven via /me) — the routes stay registered either way, so a\n// non-admin who deep-links hits the page's Forbidden state; this gate is for nav\n// convenience, not the security boundary. }

  "content": "import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';\nimport { MemoryRouter } from 'react-router-dom';\nimport { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';\n\nimport type { MeResponse } from '../../src/api/me';\nimport { MeContext } from '../../src/auth/meContext';\nimport type { AsyncState } from '../../src/hooks/useAsyncData';\nimport { Settings } from '../../src/pages/Settings';\n\nconst adminMe: AsyncState<MeResponse> = {\n  status: 'ok',\n  data: {\n    id: 'u-1',\n    aad_object_id: 'oid-1',\n    email: 'admin@dataq.io',\n    display_name: 'Ada Admin',\n    last_seen_at: null,\n    is_workspace_admin: true,\n  },\n};\n\nfunction renderSettings(me: AsyncState<MeResponse>) {\n  return render(\n    <MemoryRouter>\n      <MeContext.Provider value={me}>\n        <Settings />\n      </MeContext.Provider>\n    </MemoryRouter>,\n  );\n}\n\ndescribe('Settings', () => {\n  it('renders the settings shell for a workspace admin', () => {\n    renderSettings(adminMe);\n    expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Settings' })).toBeInTheDocument();\n    expect(screen.getByText('Workspace settings are coming soon.')).toBeInTheDocument();\n  });\n\n  it('shows the Forbidden page for a non-admin (server-driven via /me)', () => {\n    renderSettings({ ...adminMe, data: { ...adminMe.data, is_workspace_admin: false } });\n    expect(screen.getByText('403 — Forbidden')).toBeInTheDocument();\n  });\n});\n"
}

> AGENT

Phase 0.2 done — Phase 0 (foundation) and the full dashboard slice are now complete.

## Phase 0.2 — nav/shell restructure (`0c6a219`)
- **Primary nav** (Dashboard · Connections · Suites · Results · Profile) with icons, plus a **bottom-pinned footer group** (Admin · Settings · Documentation) separated by a hairline — matching the prototype's `AppShell`.
- **Admin + Settings** appear in the footer only for workspace admins (server-driven via `/me`); routes stay registered so a deep-link hits **Forbidden**, not a dead link. **Documentation** is a disabled placeholder until a docs site exists.
- Catch-all `*` → `/dashboard` (becomes a 404 page in Phase 6).
- New **`/settings` shell** — admin-gated like the Admin page (Forbidden for non-admins); tabs land in Phase 5.2.

## A scoping call I made
The TODO listed registering the Phase-4 edit/new routes (`/suites/new`, `/connections/:id/edit`, etc.) in 0.2 "with stub pages." I **deferred those to Phase 4** instead — they land with their real pages, so no throwaway stubs to rip out. I recorded this explicitly in the TODO so it's tracked, not dropped.

## Verification
prettier/eslint/tsc clean; **243 tests pass** (+2 Settings gate tests); build splits a tiny `/settings` lazy chunk; recharts still isolated to its own chunk.

## Where Week 6 stands
Done: **0.1, 0.2, 1.1, 2.1–2.6** — foundation + the complete Enhanced Monitoring Dashboard + routed run detail. Remaining: **1.2** (CSV/JSON export on run detail), **3** (results filters + orchestration poll), **4** (drawer→page restructures), **5** (Profile/Settings/Admin content), **6** (error pages), **7** (alerting).

Want me to continue with **Phase 1.2** (CSV/JSON export on the run-detail page) next? It's small and self-contained — reuses the existing `utils/download.ts`.

1	/**
2	 * Trigger a browser download of `data` serialised as pretty-printed JSON.
3	 *
4	 * Builds an object-URL blob and clicks a transient anchor — the standard
5	 * no-backend "save this JSON" path (used by the suite export). The URL is
6	 * revoked immediately after the click so the blob doesn't leak.
7	 */
8	export function downloadJson(filename: string, data: unknown): void {
9	  const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(data, null, 2)], { type: 'application/json' });
10	  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
11	  const anchor = document.createElement('a');
12	  anchor.href = url;
13	  anchor.download = filename;
14	  document.body.appendChild(anchor);
15	  anchor.click();
16	  anchor.remove();
17	  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
18	}
19	
20	/**
21	 * Turn an arbitrary label into a safe, lowercase filename stem: non-word
22	 * characters collapse to underscores, runs trim to one, leading/trailing
23	 * underscores drop. Falls back to `fallback` when nothing usable remains (e.g.
24	 * a name of only punctuation).
25	 */
26	export function toFilenameStem(label: string, fallback = 'suite'): string {
27	  const stem = label
28	    .trim()
29	    .toLowerCase()
30	    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '_')
31	    .replace(/^_+|_+$/g, '');
32	  return stem || fallback;
33	}
34

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/arijit/Coding/Python/DataQ/frontend/src/utils/download.ts",
  "old_string": "/**\n * Trigger a browser download of `data` serialised as pretty-printed JSON.\n *\n * Builds an object-URL blob and clicks a transient anchor — the standard\n * no-backend \"save this JSON\" path (used by the suite export). The URL is\n * revoked immediately after the click so the blob doesn't leak.\n */\nexport function downloadJson(filename: string, data: unknown): void {\n  const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(data, null, 2)], { type: 'application/json' });\n  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);\n  const anchor = document.createElement('a');\n  anchor.href = url;\n  anchor.download = filename;\n  document.body.appendChild(anchor);\n  anchor.click();\n  anchor.remove();\n  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);\n}",
  "new_string": "/**\n * Trigger a browser download of `content` as a file. Builds an object-URL blob\n * and clicks a transient anchor — the standard no-backend \"save this\" path. The\n * URL is revoked immediately after the click so the blob doesn't leak.\n */\nexport function downloadText(filename: string, content: string, mime: string): void {\n  const blob = new Blob([content], { type: mime });\n  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);\n  const anchor = document.createElement('a');\n  anchor.href = url;\n  anchor.download = filename;\n  document.body.appendChild(anchor);\n  anchor.click();\n  anchor.remove();\n  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);\n}\n\n/** Trigger a download of `data` serialised as pretty-printed JSON. */\nexport function downloadJson(filename: string, data: unknown): void {\n  downloadText(filename, JSON.stringify(data, null, 2), 'application/json');\n}\n\n/**\n * Quote a CSV cell per RFC 4180: a field containing a comma, double-quote, or\n * newline is wrapped in double-quotes with inner quotes doubled. `null`/\n * `undefined` become an empty field. Everything is coerced via `String()` — an\n * object should be JSON-stringified by the caller before it gets here.\n */\nfunction csvCell(value: unknown): string {\n  if (value === null || value === undefined) return '';\n  const s = String(value);\n  return /[\",\\n\\r]/.test(s) ? `\"${s.replace(/\"/g, '\"\"')}\"` : s;\n}\n\n/** Build an RFC-4180 CSV string from a header row + data rows (CRLF line ends). */\nexport function toCsv(headers: string[], rows: unknown[][]): string {\n  return [headers, ...rows].map((row) => row.map(csvCell).join(',')).join('\\r\\n');\n}\n\n/** Trigger a download of a CSV built from `headers` + `rows`. */\nexport function downloadCsv(filename: string, headers: string[], rows: unknown[][]): void {\n  downloadText(filename, toCsv(headers, rows), 'text/csv;charset=utf-8');\n}"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/arijit/Coding/Python/DataQ/frontend/src/pages/RunDetail.tsx",
  "old_string": "function Stat({ label, children }: { label: string; children: React.ReactNode }) {\n  return (\n    <Card size=\"small\" style={{ minWidth: 150 }}>\n      <Flex vertical gap={4}>\n        <Typography.Text type=\"secondary\" style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>\n          {label}\n        </Typography.Text>\n        <span style={{ fontSize: 15 }}>{children}</span>\n      </Flex>\n    </Card>\n  );\n}",
  "new_string": "function Stat({ label, children }: { label: string; children: React.ReactNode }) {\n  return (\n    <Card size=\"small\" style={{ minWidth: 150 }}>\n      <Flex vertical gap={4}>\n        <Typography.Text type=\"secondary\" style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>\n          {label}\n        </Typography.Text>\n        <span style={{ fontSize: 15 }}>{children}</span>\n      </Flex>\n    </Card>\n  );\n}\n\n// ─────────────────────────── export (CSV / JSON) ────────────────────\n\ntype RunWithResults = Awaited<ReturnType<typeof getRun>>;\n\n/** Compact, stable string for a JSONB scalar in a flat export cell. */\nfunction exportScalar(value: Record<string, unknown> | null): string {\n  return value === null ? '' : JSON.stringify(value);\n}\n\nfunction DownloadMenu({\n  run,\n  suiteName,\n  checks,\n}: {\n  run: RunWithResults;\n  suiteName: string | null;\n  checks: Map<string, Check>;\n}) {\n  const stem = `${toFilenameStem(suiteName ?? 'run')}_run_${run.id.slice(0, 8)}`;\n  const checkName = (id: string) => checks.get(id)?.name ?? id;\n  const expectation = (id: string) => checks.get(id)?.expectation_type ?? '';\n\n  const exportCsv = () => {\n    downloadCsv(\n      `${stem}.csv`,\n      ['check', 'expectation', 'status', 'metric_value', 'observed'],\n      run.results.map((r) => [\n        checkName(r.check_id),\n        expectation(r.check_id),\n        r.status,\n        r.metric_value,\n        exportScalar(r.observed_value),\n      ]),\n    );\n  };\n\n  const exportJson = () => {\n    // Sample failing rows are never in the payload (PII, withheld by the API).\n    downloadJson(`${stem}.json`, {\n      run: {\n        id: run.id,\n        suite_id: run.suite_id,\n        suite_name: suiteName,\n        status: run.status,\n        triggered_by: run.triggered_by,\n        started_at: run.started_at,\n        finished_at: run.finished_at,\n      },\n      checks: run.results.map((r) => ({\n        check: checkName(r.check_id),\n        expectation_type: expectation(r.check_id) || null,\n        status: r.status,\n        metric_value: r.metric_value,\n        observed_value: r.observed_value,\n        expected_value: r.expected_value,\n      })),\n    });\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <Dropdown\n      menu={{\n        items: [\n          { key: 'csv', label: 'Download CSV', onClick: exportCsv },\n          { key: 'json', label: 'Download JSON', onClick: exportJson },\n        ],\n      }}\n      disabled={run.results.length === 0}\n    >\n      <Button icon={<DownloadOutlined />}>Download</Button>\n    </Dropdown>\n  );\n}"
}

import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';

import { downloadJson, toFilenameStem } from '../../src/utils/download';

describe('toFilenameStem', () => {
  it('lowercases and collapses non-word runs to single underscores', () => {
    expect(toFilenameStem('Orders Suite')).toBe('orders_suite');
    expect(toFilenameStem('  A / B  ')).toBe('a_b');
    expect(toFilenameStem('orders__suite')).toBe('orders_suite');
  });

  it('falls back when nothing usable remains', () => {
    expect(toFilenameStem('!!!')).toBe('suite');
    expect(toFilenameStem('###', 'doc')).toBe('doc');
  });
});

describe('downloadJson', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    URL.createObjectURL = vi.fn(() => 'blob:mock');
    URL.revokeObjectURL = vi.fn();

  it('serialises pretty JSON, clicks an anchor, and revokes the URL', async () => {

      .spyOn(HTMLAnchorElement.prototype, 'click')

Phase 1.2 done — **Phase 1 (results run-detail) is complete**, and that's now **13 commits** on the local branch chain.

## Phase 1.2 — CSV/JSON export (`8a405e9`)
- **Download menu** (CSV · JSON) on the run-detail header, disabled when the run has no results.
- **CSV**: check · expectation · status · metric · observed, with RFC-4180 escaping (quotes/commas/newlines, CRLF).
- **JSON**: run metadata + a checks array.
- Filename slugged from suite name + short run id (e.g. `orders_quality_run_r1.csv`).
- Generalised `utils/download.ts` — a shared `downloadText` primitive now backs `downloadJson` plus new `toCsv`/`downloadCsv`.
- Sample failing rows are in **neither** payload — the API withholds them (PII, #226).

## Decision resolved
**PDF export → deferred to backlog** (an extra dependency for little gain over CSV/JSON). Recorded in the TODO + commit. Since we're local-only (no push/PR), I haven't filed a GitHub issue for it yet — flag me when you want the deferred items (PDF, the Phase-4 route registrations) turned into tracked issues with the Week-6 milestone.

Confirmed: no formula-injection handling or test. I have everything needed.

## Review: Week 6 Results dashboard branch

### Authz / PII / data-leak
- **Dashboard summary** (`dashboard_service.py`, `dashboard.py`): every aggregate is filtered through `suite_service.accessible_suite_ids(user_id)` in SQL (`_status_counts`, `_run_trend`, `_suite_performance`, `_active_connections`, `_total_runs`). Endpoint gated on `get_current_user`. No leak path. Tests cover owned + shared scoping and window exclusion. **Clean.**
- **Per-check history** (`check_service.list_check_result_history`): router does `require_permission(view)`; service adds `get_check` cross-suite guard + `Run.suite_id == suite_id` join. **Clean.**
- **RunDetail page** loads `getRun(runId)` directly, but `GET /runs/{id}` enforces `require_permission(view)` against the run's suite (404 hides existence). No foreign-run leak. **Clean.**
- **PII**: neither the API nor the export touches sample failing rows; `RunDetail`/CSV/JSON export only emit status/metric_value/observed_value/expected_value. **Clean.**

### KPI honesty (ADR 0022/0018)
- `health_score`/`pass_rate` return `None` when N=0; `MetricCard` renders `null` as em dash (not 0/100) and omits the progress bar. Fabricated tiles (anomalies, time-to-resolution, period delta) are explicitly omitted with comments. **Clean.**

### Health score (ADR 0005) & monitor-kind (ADR 0012)
- `health_score` = `100*(1 - penalty/(N*2.0))` over the four tiers; skip/error excluded from N; tests assert all-fail=50, skip/error don't dilute. Aggregation is pure SQL over `status`/`metric_value` — no JSONB reduced in Python. **Clean.**

### Frontend bundle / status maps
- recharts isolated to its own `manualChunks` chunk and only imported by lazy route components (`Dashboard`, `RunDetail`/`CheckTrend`) — stays off the initial bundle. **Clean.**
- All status→colour maps are exhaustive `Record<ResultStatus|RunStatus, …>` (`chartTheme.ts`, `resultsFormat.ts`, `SuitePerformance.STATE_COLOR/LABEL`) — a new status is a compile error. **Clean.**
- No migrations added (confirmed). No Azure lock-in in the new business logic. Router mounted in `main.py`. **Clean.**

---

### Findings

**1. should-fix — CSV formula injection in export** (`frontend/src/utils/download.ts:29` `csvCell`, used by `RunDetail.tsx:147` `exportCsv`)
The new CSV path quotes per RFC 4180 but does nothing about spreadsheet formula injection (CWE-1236). Exported cells include **user-authored** check names and expectation types plus `observed_value` derived from source data — any of which can start with `=`, `+`, `-`, `@`, or tab/CR. Opened in Excel/Sheets, a cell like `=HYPERLINK(...)` or `=cmd|...` executes. This is a brand-new attack surface (CSV export didn't exist before this branch) over data an attacker can influence (a shared-suite collaborator names a check `=...`).
*Fix:* in `csvCell`, prefix a leading `=`/`+`/`-`/`@`/tab/CR with a `'` (or wrap such cells), e.g. `if (/^[=+\-@\t\r]/.test(s)) s = "'" + s;` before the RFC-4180 quoting. Add a test alongside the existing `toCsv` cases.

**2. nit — trend day-bucketing is timezone-dependent** (`dashboard_service.py:141` `_run_trend`)
`func.date(Run.created_at)` buckets a `timestamptz` in the Postgres **session** timezone, while the zero-fill cursor uses UTC dates (`since.date()` … `datetime.now(UTC).date()`). If the DB session TimeZone isn't UTC, runs near midnight bucket into a day the cursor may not emit (dropped) or the keys misalign (off-by-one bar). Harmless if the deployment session is UTC, but it's an implicit dependency.
*Fix:* bucket explicitly in UTC, e.g. `func.date(func.timezone("UTC", Run.created_at))`, so SQL bucketing and the UTC cursor agree regardless of session TZ.

**3. nit — "Checks passed" denominator includes skip/error** (`RunDetail.tsx:84,100`)
`passed = results.filter(status === 'pass')` over `results.length` counts `skip`/`error` results in the denominator, so a run with skipped checks reads e.g. `3 / 5` and looks worse than its health. Inconsistent with the ADR-0005 convention (skip/error excluded from N) the dashboard uses. Display-only.
*Fix:* either exclude skip/error from the denominator or label it "evaluated" explicitly.

Everything else in the area reviewed is sound.

---

### Verdict
**Safe to PR** — no blockers. The authz/PII/honesty/health-score/bundle requirements all hold and are well-tested. Address finding #1 (CSV formula injection) before merge since it's a security issue on a new export surface and is a one-line guard + test; #2 and #3 are nits that can be folded in or filed as follow-ups.

An object should be JSON-stringified by\n * the caller before it gets here.\n *\n * Text cells are also guarded against spreadsheet formula injection (CWE-1236):\n * a value starting with `=`, `+`, `-`, `@`, tab, or CR makes Excel/Sheets\n * evaluate it as a formula, so we prefix such text with an apostrophe to force\n * it literal. Suite-scoped via `accessible_suite_ids`; aggregated in SQL over `status`/`metric_value` (ADR 0012); null KPIs on an empty workspace.\n- `GET /api/v1/suites/{sid}/checks/{cid}/history` — a check's recent results (status + metric_value + run time), chronological, `view`-scoped.\n\n### Frontend\n- **`/dashboard`** — KPI cards + recharts quality-trend bar + per-suite health bars + recent-runs table + 24h/7d/30d range; lands as the post-login page.\n- **`/results/:runId`** — routed run-detail page replacing the old drawer (deep-linkable); per-check results table with an expandable per-check metric trend; **CSV/JSON export**.\n- **Nav/shell** — primary nav + bottom footer group (Admin · Settings · Documentation), admin-gated `/settings` shell, catch-all → `/dashboard`.\n- **recharts** split into its own lazy chunk (vendor 388→42 kB) — ships only with a chart route.\n\n## KPI honesty (ADR 0022/0018)\nDropped the prototype's **Total Anomalies**, **Avg. Time to Resolution**, and recent-runs **Anomalies** column — no query backs them.   // Root → /connections (App.tsx Navigate).

});

import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';

// Seeded runs / results / pipeline-runs (backend/scripts/demo_data.py) read
// through the real API and rendered on the in-app Results page (ADR 0018 — the
// suite-scoped, redaction-aware surface, not Grafana). The seed lands, on the
// "Orders quality" suite, two succeeded runs — a pass/pass/warn/fail severity
// spread (seed:run:succeeded) and an operational-spectrum run with
// critical/error/skip (seed:run:mixed) — plus a terminal-failed run, and two
// monitored pipeline runs.
test.describe('Results page', () => {
  test.beforeEach(async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/results');
    await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Results', level: 3 })).toBeVisible();
  });

  test('lists seeded runs and drills into the severity-spread run (pass/warn/fail)', async ({
    page,
  }) => {
    // The seeded runs resolve to the suite name; the run status tags render.
    await expect(page.getByText('Orders quality').first()).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByText('succeeded').first()).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByText('failed').first()).toBeVisible();

    // Target the severity-spread run by its "Triggered by" marker (there are two
    // succeeded runs now — this one and the operational-spectrum run).
    await page
      .locator('tr.ant-table-row')
      .filter({ hasText: 'seed:run:succeeded' })
      .first()
      .click();
    const drawer = page.getByRole('dialog');
    await expect(drawer.getByText('order_id not null')).toBeVisible();
    await expect(drawer.getByText('amount in range')).toBeVisible();
    await expect(drawer.getByText('expect_column_values_to_be_between').first()).toBeVisible();
    // The warn + fail severity tiers from the seeded spread are visible.
    await expect(drawer.getByText('warn').first()).toBeVisible();
    await expect(drawer.getByText('fail').first()).toBeVisible();
  });

  test('drills into the operational-spectrum run (critical / error / skip)', async ({ page }) => {
    // The second succeeded run carries the operational vocabulary the first
    // doesn't: a critical breach, an error (evaluation threw), and a skip.
    await page.locator('tr.ant-table-row').filter({ hasText: 'seed:run:mixed' }).first().click();
    const drawer = page.getByRole('dialog');
    await expect(drawer.getByText('status in set')).toBeVisible();
    await expect(drawer.getByText('critical').first()).toBeVisible();
    await expect(drawer.getByText('error').first()).toBeVisible();
    await expect(drawer.getByText('skip').first()).toBeVisible();
  });

  test('shows the orchestration pipeline-runs monitoring feed', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.getByRole('tab', { name: 'Pipeline runs' }).click();

    // Both seeded pipeline runs (ADF succeeded, Airflow failed) are listed.
    await expect(page.getByText('daily_orders_load')).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByText('events_streaming')).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.getByText('upstream source timed out')).toBeVisible();
  });
});
